```
- path: src/lib/rag/config.ts
  content: |
      /**
       * RAG provider settings. Shared by the ingestion worker, query embedding and
       * retrieval. EMBEDDING_DIMENSIONS must match document_chunks.embedding
       * (vector(1536)) in drizzle/migrations/0010_rag.sql; changing one without the
       * other silently breaks retrieval, so change them together.
       */
      export const EMBEDDING_MODEL = "qwen/qwen3-embedding-8b";
      export const EMBEDDING_DIMENSIONS = 1536;
      
      /** Provider limit is 100 input texts per embeddings call. */
      export const EMBEDDING_BATCH_SIZE = 100;
      
      /** Recorded on every chunk so a model switch is visible, not guessed. */
      export const EMBEDDING_MODEL_KEY = EMBEDDING_MODEL;
      
      /**
       * Provider price per embedding token, derived from reported usage
       * (859 tokens cost $0.0000859). Only used when a batched response omits cost,
       * which this provider does above a few inputs.
       */
      export const EMBEDDING_USD_PER_TOKEN = 1e-7;
      
- path: AGENTS.md
  content: |
      <!-- LOVABLE:BEGIN -->
      > [!IMPORTANT]
      > This project is connected to [Lovable](https://lovable.dev). Avoid rewriting
      > published git history — force pushing, or rebasing/amending/squashing commits
      > that are already pushed — as it rewrites history on Lovable's side and the
      > user will likely lose their project history.
      >
      > Commits you push to the connected branch sync back to Lovable and show up in
      > the editor, so keep the branch in a working state.
      <!-- LOVABLE:END -->
      
      ## Project rules
      - Content grids render in incremental batches through ProgressiveList keyed by filters; Insights activity pages live in URL search, and file triggers reuse FilePreviewDialog to preserve private viewing and activity logging. File tiles use a full-area preview button behind non-interactive content, with separate action buttons above it to avoid nested controls.
      - Bundled photos use WebP; document engines load on demand and PDFs retain one parsed document across page changes to reduce repeated work.
      - User changes and sign-out clear query caches; short explicit freshness windows avoid repeated unchanged metadata reads without bypassing RLS.
      - Usage insights verify is_content_admin before route entry and aggregate through an admin-checked database RPC; raw identity-bearing activity is visible only to its owner or verified admins.
      - Recovery redirects use an exact origin allowlist; persistent atomic quotas guard account recovery and paid cover generation, and accounts are not auto-confirmed before mailbox verification.
      - Library search keeps typed text in local state and saves its URL on blur or Enter, so asynchronous navigation cannot drop keystrokes.
      - The admin dashboard verifies access with is_content_admin before loading paginated file metadata; deletions remain enforced by existing database and Storage policies, and storage totals deduplicate file paths and flag unknown sizes rather than estimate.
      - Uploads on an industry page derive the destination slug from the current route, never retained form state; industry page local state resets on slug changes to prevent cross-folder assignments.
      - File previews download through authenticated Storage into revocable browser blob URLs; PDF uses dynamically imported PDF.js and DOCX/PPTX use browser renderers inside script-disabled frames, never external hosted document viewers.
      - Roles are stored in protected user_roles rows and enforced by RLS; client role queries only control presentation. Verified designated admin assignment runs on auth lifecycle events, while all users receive member grants.
      - Company rename/delete uses an admin-checked transactional database RPC to update both saved companies and content labels; deleting a company preserves its files as industry-wide content.
      - The page logo and browser/touch icons derive from the same bundled brand asset so the app identity stays consistent.
      - Allowed email domains live in `src/lib/domains.ts` and are mirrored in the DB function `public.is_allowed_email` (trigger on auth.users + RLS); change both together — the DB is the real gate.
      - First-time access and forgot-password share one server function (`requestPasswordLink`) that pre-creates the user then emails a recovery link; there is no public signup.
      - Industries live in the `industries` table (anyone can read, allowed users add). The original 8 use bundled images keyed by slug; new ones get an AI-generated cover stored in the private `industry-images` bucket and served via `/api/public/industry-image/$slug` (public buckets are blocked in this workspace). Customers per industry = rows in `customers` table merged with names used in `content_items.customer_name`.
```

```
- path: src/lib/rag/ingest.ts
  content: |
      /**
       * Ingestion building blocks shared by the worker and tests: chunk shaping,
       * content hashing and batched embedding. Runs in Node/Bun; the worker is the
       * only caller of the provider.
       */
      import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
      import { chunkSections, type ChunkableSection } from "./chunk";
      import {
        EMBEDDING_BATCH_SIZE,
        EMBEDDING_DIMENSIONS,
        EMBEDDING_MODEL,
        EMBEDDING_USD_PER_TOKEN,
      } from "./config";
      
      export type ChunkRow = {
        chunk_index: number;
        content: string;
        estimated_tokens: number;
        page: number | null;
        slide: number | null;
        heading: string | null;
        embedding_model: string;
      };
      
      export type EmbeddedChunk = ChunkRow & { embedding: number[] };
      
      /**
       * Turns extracted sections into database rows. PDF page numbers land in `page`,
       * slide numbers in `slide`; DOCX has neither and keeps only its heading.
       */
      export function buildChunkRows(
        format: string,
        sections: readonly ChunkableSection[],
        sourceFile: string,
      ): ChunkRow[] {
        return chunkSections(sections, { sourceFile }).map((chunk) => ({
          chunk_index: chunk.chunkIndex,
          content: chunk.content,
          estimated_tokens: Math.max(chunk.estimatedTokens, 1),
          page: format === "pdf" ? chunk.pageOrSlide : null,
          slide: format === "pptx" ? chunk.pageOrSlide : null,
          heading: chunk.heading,
          embedding_model: EMBEDDING_MODEL,
        }));
      }
      
      /** Stable fingerprint of the extracted text, used to skip unchanged documents. */
      export function hashChunkContents(contents: readonly string[]): string {
        return createHash("sha256").update(contents.join("\u0000")).digest("hex");
      }
      
      export type EmbedOptions = {
        apiKey: string;
        baseUrl: string;
        fetchImpl?: typeof fetch;
        model?: string;
        dimensions?: number;
        batchSize?: number;
      };
      
      export type EmbeddingResult = {
        embeddings: number[][];
        promptTokens: number | null;
        costUsd: number | null;
        /** True when cost was derived from the token count instead of provider usage. */
        costEstimated: boolean;
      };
      
      type EmbeddingResponse = {
        data?: Array<{ embedding?: number[]; index?: number }>;
        usage?: { prompt_tokens?: number; cost?: number };
        error?: { message?: string } | string;
      };
      
      /** Embeds texts in provider-sized batches, preserving input order. */
      export async function embedTexts(
        texts: readonly string[],
        options: EmbedOptions,
      ): Promise<EmbeddingResult> {
        const embeddings: number[][] = [];
        let promptTokens = 0;
        let costUsd = 0;
        let sawUsage = false;
        let sawCost = false;
      
        const batchSize = Math.max(1, Math.min(options.batchSize ?? EMBEDDING_BATCH_SIZE, 100));
        const dimensions = options.dimensions ?? EMBEDDING_DIMENSIONS;
        const model = options.model ?? EMBEDDING_MODEL;
        const doFetch = options.fetchImpl ?? fetch;
        const baseUrl = options.baseUrl.replace(/\/+$/, "");
      
        for (let start = 0; start < texts.length; start += batchSize) {
          const batch = texts.slice(start, start + batchSize);
          const response = await doFetch(`${baseUrl}/embeddings`, {
            method: "POST",
            headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", Authorization: `Bearer ${options.apiKey}` },
            body: JSON.stringify({ model, input: batch, dimensions }),
          });
      
          const raw = await response.text();
          if (!response.ok) {
            throw new Error(`Embedding provider failed (${response.status}): ${providerMessage(raw)}`);
          }
      
          let body: EmbeddingResponse;
          try {
            body = JSON.parse(raw) as EmbeddingResponse;
          } catch {
            throw new Error("Embedding provider returned a non-JSON response");
          }
      
          const data = body.data;
          if (!Array.isArray(data) || data.length !== batch.length) {
            throw new Error(
              `Embedding provider returned ${Array.isArray(data) ? data.length : 0} vectors for ${batch.length} inputs`,
            );
          }
      
          // Providers may reorder; index is authoritative when present.
          const ordered = [...data].sort((a, b) => (a.index ?? 0) - (b.index ?? 0));
          for (const entry of ordered) {
            const vector = entry.embedding;
            if (!Array.isArray(vector) || vector.length !== dimensions) {
              throw new Error(
                `Embedding dimension mismatch: expected ${dimensions}, received ${vector?.length ?? 0}`,
              );
            }
            embeddings.push(vector);
          }
      
          if (body.usage) {
            sawUsage = true;
            promptTokens += body.usage.prompt_tokens ?? 0;
            if (typeof body.usage.cost === "number") {
              sawCost = true;
              costUsd += body.usage.cost;
            }
          }
        }
      
        // Batched responses omit cost, so fall back to the observed per-token price.
        const costEstimated = sawUsage && !sawCost;
        if (costEstimated) costUsd = promptTokens * EMBEDDING_USD_PER_TOKEN;
      
        return {
          embeddings,
          promptTokens: sawUsage ? promptTokens : null,
          costUsd: sawUsage ? costUsd : null,
          costEstimated,
        };
      }
      
      /** Keeps provider text in logs useful without dumping a whole response body. */
      function providerMessage(raw: string): string {
        try {
          const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as EmbeddingResponse;
          const message = typeof parsed.error === "string" ? parsed.error : (parsed.error?.message ?? "");
          if (message) return message.slice(0, 300);
        } catch {
          // fall through to the raw body
        }
        return raw.slice(0, 300);
      }
      
- path: src/lib/rag/chunk.ts
  content: |
      /**
       * Pure, runtime-agnostic chunker shared by the extraction spike and the
       * ingestion Edge Function (Deno). No imports on purpose: keep it portable.
       */
      
      export type ChunkableSection = {
        text: string;
        pageOrSlide?: number | null;
        heading?: string | null;
      };
      
      export type Chunk = {
        chunkIndex: number;
        content: string;
        pageOrSlide: number | null;
        heading: string | null;
        sourceFile: string;
        estimatedTokens: number;
      };
      
      export type ChunkOptions = {
        sourceFile: string;
        targetTokens?: number;
        overlapTokens?: number;
      };
      
      // Approximate token count. Good enough for chunk sizing; embeddings do the rest.
      const CHARS_PER_TOKEN = 4;
      
      export function estimateTokens(text: string): number {
        return Math.ceil(text.trim().length / CHARS_PER_TOKEN);
      }
      
      /**
       * Splits sections (pages, slides or heading blocks) into ~targetTokens chunks.
       * Sections are never merged, so page/slide numbers stay accurate. Long sections
       * are split on sentence/line boundaries with ~overlapTokens of repeated text.
       */
      export function chunkSections(
        sections: readonly ChunkableSection[],
        options: ChunkOptions,
      ): Chunk[] {
        const targetChars = Math.max(1, Math.round((options.targetTokens ?? 600) * CHARS_PER_TOKEN));
        const overlapChars = Math.max(0, Math.round((options.overlapTokens ?? 80) * CHARS_PER_TOKEN));
        const chunks: Chunk[] = [];
      
        for (const section of sections) {
          const text = normalizeText(section.text);
          if (!text) continue;
      
          const heading = section.heading?.trim() || null;
          const pageOrSlide = section.pageOrSlide ?? null;
          const units = splitUnits(text).flatMap((unit) =>
            splitOversizedUnit(unit, targetChars, overlapChars),
          );
      
          let current: string[] = [];
          let currentChars = 0;
      
          const flush = () => {
            const body = current.join("\n").trim();
            if (!body) {
              current = [];
              currentChars = 0;
              return;
            }
            const content = heading ? `${heading}\n\n${body}` : body;
            chunks.push({
              chunkIndex: chunks.length,
              content,
              pageOrSlide,
              heading,
              sourceFile: options.sourceFile,
              estimatedTokens: estimateTokens(content),
            });
            current = [];
            currentChars = 0;
          };
      
          for (const unit of units) {
            const separator = current.length > 0 ? 1 : 0;
            if (current.length > 0 && currentChars + separator + unit.length > targetChars) {
              // Seed the next chunk with the tail of this one so context carries over.
              const seed = trailingText(current, overlapChars);
              flush();
              if (seed && seed.length + 1 + unit.length <= targetChars) {
                current = [seed];
                currentChars = seed.length;
              }
            }
            const joiner = current.length > 0 ? 1 : 0;
            current.push(unit);
            currentChars += joiner + unit.length;
          }
      
          flush();
        }
      
        return chunks;
      }
      
      function normalizeText(text: string): string {
        return text
          .replace(/\r\n?/g, "\n")
          .replace(/[ \t]+\n/g, "\n")
          .replace(/\n{3,}/g, "\n\n")
          .trim();
      }
      
      function splitUnits(text: string): string[] {
        return text
          .replace(/([.!?])\s+|\n+/g, (_match: string, punctuation?: string) =>
            punctuation ? `${punctuation}\n` : "\n",
          )
          .split("\n")
          .map((unit) => unit.trim())
          .filter(Boolean);
      }
      
      function splitOversizedUnit(unit: string, targetChars: number, overlapChars: number): string[] {
        if (unit.length <= targetChars) return [unit];
      
        // Leave room for the overlap seed (and its newline) the caller adds.
        const pieceLimit = Math.max(1, targetChars - overlapChars - 1);
        const words = unit.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
        const pieces: string[] = [];
        let current: string[] = [];
        let currentChars = 0;
      
        for (const word of words) {
          const separator = current.length > 0 ? 1 : 0;
          if (current.length > 0 && currentChars + separator + word.length > pieceLimit) {
            pieces.push(current.join(" "));
            current = [];
            currentChars = 0;
          }
          const joiner = current.length > 0 ? 1 : 0;
          current.push(word);
          currentChars += joiner + word.length;
        }
        if (current.length > 0) pieces.push(current.join(" "));
        return pieces;
      }
      
      function trailingText(units: readonly string[], maxChars: number): string {
        if (maxChars <= 0) return "";
        const joined = units.join("\n");
        if (joined.length <= maxChars) return joined;
        const cut = joined.slice(joined.length - maxChars);
        const firstSpace = cut.search(/\s/);
        return (firstSpace === -1 ? cut : cut.slice(firstSpace + 1)).trim();
      }
      
- path: src/lib/rag/extract.ts
  content: |
      import { Buffer } from "node:buffer";
      import { strFromU8, unzipSync } from "fflate";
      import mammoth from "mammoth";
      import { extractText, getDocumentProxy } from "unpdf";
      import { documentFormat, type DocumentFormat } from "@/lib/document-format";
      
      export const MIN_USEFUL_CHARS = 20;
      
      // Real decks use custom heading styles; map the common ones to HTML headings.
      const DOCX_STYLE_MAP = [
        "p[style-name='Section Heading'] => h1:fresh",
        "p[style-name='Heading 0'] => h1:fresh",
        "p[style-name='Title'] => h1:fresh",
      ];
      
      export type ExtractedSection = {
        text: string;
        pageOrSlide: number | null;
        heading: string | null;
      };
      
      export type EmptyUnit = {
        pageOrSlide: number | null;
        heading: string | null;
      };
      
      export type ExtractedDocument = {
        format: DocumentFormat;
        sections: ExtractedSection[];
        emptyUnits: EmptyUnit[];
        warnings: string[];
      };
      
      export type ExtractionResult =
        { ok: true; document: ExtractedDocument } | { ok: false; format: DocumentFormat; error: string };
      
      /**
       * Extracts text for one document. Runs in Node (spike) and Deno (Edge
       * Function); never in the browser or the Cloudflare Worker.
       */
      export async function extractDocument(
        fileName: string,
        mimeType: string,
        bytes: Uint8Array,
      ): Promise<ExtractionResult> {
        const format = documentFormat(fileName, mimeType);
        try {
          switch (format) {
            case "pdf":
              return { ok: true, document: await extractPdf(bytes) };
            case "docx":
              return { ok: true, document: await extractDocx(bytes) };
            case "pptx":
              return { ok: true, document: extractPptx(bytes) };
            default:
              return { ok: false, format, error: `Unsupported format: ${format}` };
          }
        } catch (error) {
          return { ok: false, format, error: errorMessage(error) };
        }
      }
      
      /** True when a page or slide has too little text to be worth embedding (probable scan). */
      export function isLowText(text: string): boolean {
        return text.replace(/\s+/g, "").length < MIN_USEFUL_CHARS;
      }
      
      async function extractPdf(bytes: Uint8Array): Promise<ExtractedDocument> {
        // verbosity 0 silences PDF.js font warnings; one file per ingestion run.
        const pdf = await getDocumentProxy(bytes, { verbosity: 0 });
        const { text } = await extractText(pdf, { mergePages: false });
        const pages = Array.isArray(text) ? text : [text];
        const sections = pages.map((page, index) => ({
          text: cleanText(page),
          pageOrSlide: index + 1,
          heading: null,
        }));
        return { format: "pdf", sections, emptyUnits: emptyUnitsFrom(sections), warnings: [] };
      }
      
      async function extractDocx(bytes: Uint8Array): Promise<ExtractedDocument> {
        const result = await mammoth.convertToHtml(
          { buffer: Buffer.from(bytes) },
          { styleMap: DOCX_STYLE_MAP },
        );
        const sections = sectionsFromHtml(result.value);
        if (sections.length === 0) sections.push({ text: "", pageOrSlide: null, heading: null });
        const warnings = result.messages
          .filter((message) => message.type === "warning")
          .map((message) => message.message);
        return { format: "docx", sections, emptyUnits: emptyUnitsFrom(sections), warnings };
      }
      
      function extractPptx(bytes: Uint8Array): ExtractedDocument {
        // Only decompress slide/notes XML; image-heavy decks can be hundreds of MB.
        const files = unzipSync(bytes, {
          filter: (file) =>
            /^ppt\/slides\/slide\d+\.xml$/.test(file.name) ||
            /^ppt\/notesSlides\/notesSlide\d+\.xml$/.test(file.name) ||
            /^ppt\/notesSlides\/_rels\/notesSlide\d+\.xml\.rels$/.test(file.name),
        });
        const notes = pptxNotes(files);
        const slides = Object.keys(files)
          .map((path) => ({ path, match: /^ppt\/slides\/slide(\d+)\.xml$/.exec(path) }))
          .filter((entry): entry is { path: string; match: RegExpExecArray } => entry.match !== null)
          .sort((a, b) => Number(a.match[1]) - Number(b.match[1]));
      
        if (slides.length === 0) throw new Error("No slides found in PowerPoint file");
      
        const warnings: string[] = [];
        const sections: ExtractedSection[] = [];
        for (const { path, match } of slides) {
          const number = Number(match[1]);
          const data = files[path];
          if (!data) continue;
          try {
            // Slide text comes only from the slide XML. Image relationships are
            // ignored, so decks with broken media links still extract.
            const slideText = slideXmlToText(strFromU8(data));
            const note = notes.get(number);
            sections.push({
              text: note ? `${slideText}\n\nNotes: ${note}`.trim() : slideText,
              pageOrSlide: number,
              heading: null,
            });
          } catch (error) {
            warnings.push(`Slide ${number}: ${errorMessage(error)}`);
            sections.push({ text: "", pageOrSlide: number, heading: null });
          }
        }
      
        return { format: "pptx", sections, emptyUnits: emptyUnitsFrom(sections), warnings };
      }
      
      /** Drops the slide-number placeholder from notes, keeping real speaker notes. */
      function cleanNotes(text: string): string {
        return text
          .split("\n")
          .map((line) => line.trim())
          .filter((line) => line.length > 0 && !/^\d+$/.test(line))
          .join("\n");
      }
      
      function pptxNotes(files: Record<string, Uint8Array>): Map<number, string> {
        const notes = new Map<number, string>();
        for (const [path, data] of Object.entries(files)) {
          const match = /^ppt\/notesSlides\/notesSlide(\d+)\.xml$/.exec(path);
          if (!match) continue;
          try {
            const text = cleanNotes(slideXmlToText(strFromU8(data)));
            if (!text) continue;
            let slideNumber = Number(match[1]);
            const rels = files[`ppt/notesSlides/_rels/notesSlide${match[1]}.xml.rels`];
            if (rels) {
              const target = /Target="[^"]*slides\/slide(\d+)\.xml"/.exec(strFromU8(rels));
              if (target?.[1]) slideNumber = Number(target[1]);
            }
            notes.set(slideNumber, text);
          } catch {
            // Notes are optional; malformed notes must not fail the deck.
          }
        }
        return notes;
      }
      
      /** Pulls the a:t text runs out of one slide or notes XML file. */
      export function slideXmlToText(xml: string): string {
        const paragraphs = [...xml.matchAll(/<a:p\b[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/a:p>/g)];
        const lines = paragraphs
          .map((paragraph) =>
            [...(paragraph[1] ?? "").matchAll(/<a:t\b[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/a:t>/g)]
              .map((run) => decodeXmlEntities(run[1] ?? ""))
              .join("")
              .trim(),
          )
          .filter(Boolean);
      
        if (lines.length > 0) return lines.join("\n");
      
        return [...xml.matchAll(/<a:t\b[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/a:t>/g)]
          .map((run) => decodeXmlEntities(run[1] ?? "").trim())
          .filter(Boolean)
          .join("\n");
      }
      
      function sectionsFromHtml(html: string): ExtractedSection[] {
        const sections: ExtractedSection[] = [];
        let heading: string | null = null;
        let buffer = "";
      
        const flush = () => {
          const text = htmlToText(buffer);
          if (text) sections.push({ text, pageOrSlide: null, heading });
          buffer = "";
        };
      
        const headingPattern = /<h([1-6])\b[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/h\1>/gi;
        let lastIndex = 0;
        for (const match of html.matchAll(headingPattern)) {
          const index = match.index ?? 0;
          buffer += html.slice(lastIndex, index);
          flush();
          heading = htmlToText(match[2] ?? "");
          lastIndex = index + match[0].length;
        }
        buffer += html.slice(lastIndex);
        flush();
        return sections;
      }
      
      function htmlToText(html: string): string {
        return decodeXmlEntities(
          html
            .replace(/<\/(?:p|h[1-6]|li|tr|blockquote)>/gi, "\n")
            .replace(/<br\s*\/?>/gi, "\n")
            .replace(/<[^>]*>/g, ""),
        )
          .replace(/[ \t]+\n/g, "\n")
          .replace(/\n{3,}/g, "\n\n")
          .trim();
      }
      
      const NAMED_ENTITIES: Record<string, string> = {
        amp: "&",
        lt: "<",
        gt: ">",
        quot: '"',
        apos: "'",
      };
      
      function decodeXmlEntities(value: string): string {
        return value.replace(/&(#x[0-9a-f]+|#[0-9]+|[a-z]+);/gi, (match, entity: string) => {
          const normalized = entity.toLowerCase();
          if (normalized.startsWith("#")) {
            const hex = normalized.startsWith("#x");
            const code = Number.parseInt(normalized.slice(hex ? 2 : 1), hex ? 16 : 10);
            try {
              return Number.isFinite(code) ? String.fromCodePoint(code) : match;
            } catch {
              return match;
            }
          }
          return NAMED_ENTITIES[normalized] ?? match;
        });
      }
      
      function emptyUnitsFrom(sections: readonly ExtractedSection[]): EmptyUnit[] {
        return sections
          .filter((section) => isLowText(section.text))
          .map((section) => ({ pageOrSlide: section.pageOrSlide, heading: section.heading }));
      }
      
      function cleanText(text: string): string {
        return text
          .replace(/\r\n?/g, "\n")
          .replace(/[ \t]+\n/g, "\n")
          .replace(/\n{3,}/g, "\n\n")
          .trim();
      }
      
      function errorMessage(error: unknown): string {
        return error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
      }
```

```
- path: scripts/ingest-worker.ts
  content: |
      /**
       * Standalone ingestion worker. Claims one queued job at a time, downloads the
       * private file, extracts text, chunks, embeds and replaces the document's
       * passages atomically.
       *
       * Usage:
       *   bun scripts/ingest-worker.ts                 # drain the queue, then exit
       *   bun scripts/ingest-worker.ts --watch         # poll until interrupted
       *   bun scripts/ingest-worker.ts --limit 5       # stop after five jobs
       *   bun scripts/ingest-worker.ts --local-dir spike-input
       *   bun scripts/ingest-worker.ts --backfill     # queue library items missing a job
       *
       * File bytes come from the private "content" bucket, authenticated with the
       * service role key when present, otherwise a member session. --local-dir reads
       * the same files from disk instead, for local pipeline checks without Storage.
       *
       * Never print document text or credentials from here: logs carry counts only.
       */
      import { createClient, type SupabaseClient } from "@supabase/supabase-js";
      import { existsSync, readFileSync, readdirSync, statSync } from "node:fs";
      import { basename, join, resolve } from "node:path";
      import postgres from "postgres";
      import type { Database } from "../src/integrations/supabase/types";
      import { documentFormat } from "../src/lib/document-format";
      import { extractDocument } from "../src/lib/rag/extract";
      import {
        buildChunkRows,
        embedTexts,
        hashChunkContents,
        type EmbeddedChunk,
      } from "../src/lib/rag/ingest";
      
      const BUCKET = "content";
      const MAX_ATTEMPTS = 3;
      const SUPPORTED = new Set(["pdf", "docx", "pptx"]);
      
      type Job = {
        id: string;
        content_id: string;
        attempts: number;
        content_hash: string | null;
        chunk_count: number;
        file_path: string;
        file_name: string;
        mime_type: string | null;
      };
      
      type Counters = { claimed: number; ready: number; empty: number; skipped: number; failed: number };
      
      const args = process.argv.slice(2);
      const flag = (name: string) => args.includes(name);
      const value = (name: string) => {
        const index = args.indexOf(name);
        return index >= 0 ? args[index + 1] : undefined;
      };
      
      const watch = flag("--watch");
      const backfill = flag("--backfill");
      const limit = Number(value("--limit") ?? 0) || Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
      const localDir = value("--local-dir") ? resolve(value("--local-dir")!) : null;
      
      if (!process.env.SUPABASE_DB_URL) {
        console.error("SUPABASE_DB_URL is missing from .env");
        process.exit(1);
      }
      if (!process.env.TOKENFACTORY_API_BASE || !process.env.TOKENFACTORY_API_KEY) {
        console.error("TOKENFACTORY_API_BASE / TOKENFACTORY_API_KEY are missing from .env");
        process.exit(1);
      }
      
      const sql = postgres(process.env.SUPABASE_DB_URL, { ssl: "require", max: 2 });
      const embedConfig = {
        apiKey: process.env.TOKENFACTORY_API_KEY,
        baseUrl: process.env.TOKENFACTORY_API_BASE,
      };
      
      let storage: SupabaseClient<Database> | null = null;
      let localFiles: Map<string, string> | null = null;
      
      async function createStorageClient(): Promise<SupabaseClient<Database>> {
        const url = process.env.SUPABASE_URL;
        const publishable = process.env.SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY;
        const serviceKey = process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY;
        const email = process.env.LIBRARY_USER_EMAIL;
        const password = process.env.LIBRARY_USER_PASSWORD;
      
        if (!url) throw new Error("SUPABASE_URL is missing");
        const options = { auth: { persistSession: false, autoRefreshToken: false } };
      
        if (serviceKey) {
          return createClient<Database>(url, serviceKey, options);
        }
        if (publishable && email && password) {
          const client = createClient<Database>(url, publishable, options);
          const { error } = await client.auth.signInWithPassword({ email, password });
          if (error) throw new Error(`Member sign-in failed: ${error.message}`);
          return client;
        }
        throw new Error(
          "Set SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY, or LIBRARY_USER_EMAIL + LIBRARY_USER_PASSWORD, or pass --local-dir",
        );
      }
      
      function findLocalFile(fileName: string): string {
        if (!localFiles) {
          const dir = localDir!;
          localFiles = new Map();
          for (const entry of readdirSync(dir)) {
            const path = join(dir, entry);
            if (!existsSync(path) || !statSync(path).isFile()) continue;
            localFiles.set(entry, path);
            // Spike downloads are prefixed with a content id: "<id8>-<file name>".
            const suffix = entry.indexOf("-");
            if (suffix > 0) localFiles.set(entry.slice(suffix + 1), path);
          }
        }
        const match = localFiles.get(fileName) ?? localFiles.get(basename(fileName));
        if (!match) throw new Error(`No local file matching ${fileName} in ${localDir}`);
        return match;
      }
      
      async function readBytes(job: Job): Promise<Uint8Array> {
        if (localDir) {
          return new Uint8Array(readFileSync(findLocalFile(job.file_name)));
        }
        storage ??= await createStorageClient();
        const { data, error } = await storage.storage.from(BUCKET).download(job.file_path);
        if (error || !data) throw new Error(`Download failed: ${error?.message ?? "empty response"}`);
        return new Uint8Array(await data.arrayBuffer());
      }
      
      /** Claims a single job, so two workers never pick the same one. */
      async function claimJob(): Promise<Job | null> {
        return sql.begin(async (tx) => {
          const rows = await tx<Job[]>`
            select j.id, j.content_id, j.attempts, j.content_hash, j.chunk_count,
                   ci.file_path, ci.file_name, ci.mime_type
            from public.ingestion_jobs j
            join public.content_items ci on ci.id = j.content_id
            where j.status = 'queued'
            order by j.created_at
            limit 1
            for update of j skip locked
          `;
          const job = rows[0];
          if (!job) return null;
      
          const [updated] = await tx<{ attempts: number }[]>`
            update public.ingestion_jobs
               set status = 'running', attempts = attempts + 1, started_at = now(), updated_at = now()
             where id = ${job.id}
             returning attempts
          `;
          return { ...job, attempts: updated.attempts };
        });
      }
      
      async function finish(
        job: Job,
        status: "ready" | "empty" | "skipped",
        patch: { chunkCount: number; contentHash?: string | null; warnings: string[] },
      ) {
        await sql`
          update public.ingestion_jobs
             set status = ${status},
                 chunk_count = ${patch.chunkCount},
                 content_hash = ${patch.contentHash ?? null},
                 warnings = ${sql.json(patch.warnings)},
                 error = null,
                 finished_at = now(),
                 updated_at = now()
           where id = ${job.id}
        `;
      }
      
      /** Records the failure; retries until MAX_ATTEMPTS, then stays failed. */
      async function fail(job: Job, message: string): Promise<"queued" | "failed"> {
        const status = job.attempts >= MAX_ATTEMPTS ? "failed" : "queued";
        await sql`
          update public.ingestion_jobs
             set status = ${status},
                 error = ${message.slice(0, 500)},
                 finished_at = ${status === "failed" ? sql`now()` : null},
                 updated_at = now()
           where id = ${job.id}
        `;
        return status;
      }
      
      function lowTextWarning(emptyUnits: number): string[] {
        return emptyUnits > 0
          ? [`${emptyUnits} page(s) or slide(s) had almost no extractable text (possible scan)`]
          : [];
      }
      
      async function processJob(job: Job, counters: Counters) {
        const startedAt = Date.now();
        const label = `${job.file_name}`;
        const format = documentFormat(job.file_name, job.mime_type ?? "");
      
        if (!SUPPORTED.has(format)) {
          await finish(job, "skipped", { chunkCount: 0, warnings: [`Format not extracted: ${format}`] });
          counters.skipped += 1;
          console.log(`job ${job.id} ${label} -> skipped (${format})`);
          return;
        }
      
        const bytes = await readBytes(job);
        const extracted = await extractDocument(job.file_name, job.mime_type ?? "", bytes);
        if (!extracted.ok) throw new Error(extracted.error);
      
        const rows = buildChunkRows(
          extracted.document.format,
          extracted.document.sections,
          job.file_name,
        );
        const warnings = [
          ...extracted.document.warnings,
          ...lowTextWarning(extracted.document.emptyUnits.length),
        ];
      
        if (rows.length === 0) {
          await sql`select public.replace_document_chunks(${job.content_id}, ${sql.json([])}, null, 'empty', ${sql.json(warnings)})`;
          counters.empty += 1;
          console.log(`job ${job.id} ${label} -> empty (no extractable text)`);
          return;
        }
      
        const contentHash = hashChunkContents(rows.map((row) => row.content));
        if (contentHash === job.content_hash && job.chunk_count > 0) {
          // Text unchanged (a move, or a re-run): keep the existing vectors.
          await finish(job, "ready", {
            chunkCount: job.chunk_count,
            contentHash,
            warnings: [...warnings, "Text unchanged; embeddings reused"],
          });
          counters.ready += 1;
          console.log(`job ${job.id} ${label} -> ready (unchanged, ${job.chunk_count} chunks kept)`);
          return;
        }
      
        const embedded = await embedTexts(
          rows.map((row) => row.content),
          embedConfig,
        );
        const payload: EmbeddedChunk[] = rows.map((row, index) => ({
          ...row,
          embedding: embedded.embeddings[index],
        }));
      
        const [replaced] = await sql<{ n: number }[]>`
          select public.replace_document_chunks(
            ${job.content_id}, ${sql.json(payload)}, ${contentHash}, 'ready', ${sql.json(warnings)}
          ) as n
        `;
      
        counters.ready += 1;
        const seconds = ((Date.now() - startedAt) / 1000).toFixed(1);
        const cost =
          embedded.costUsd === null
            ? "n/a"
            : `${embedded.costEstimated ? "~$" : "$"}${embedded.costUsd.toFixed(6)}`;
        console.log(
          `job ${job.id} ${label} -> ready (${replaced.n} chunks, ${embedded.promptTokens ?? "?"} tokens, ${cost}, ${seconds}s)`,
        );
      }
      
      async function run() {
        const counters: Counters = { claimed: 0, ready: 0, empty: 0, skipped: 0, failed: 0 };
      
        // A worker that died mid-job leaves the row in 'running' with no owner.
        const reclaimed = await sql<{ id: string }[]>`
          update public.ingestion_jobs
             set status = 'queued', updated_at = now()
           where status = 'running' and started_at < now() - interval '15 minutes'
          returning id`;
        if (reclaimed.length > 0) console.log(`reclaimed ${reclaimed.length} stale job(s)`);
      
        if (backfill) {
          const queued = await sql<{ content_id: string }[]>`
            insert into public.ingestion_jobs (content_id)
            select ci.id from public.content_items ci
             where not exists (
               select 1 from public.ingestion_jobs j where j.content_id = ci.id
             )
            on conflict (content_id) do nothing
            returning content_id`;
          console.log(`backfilled ${queued.length} item(s) with no ingestion job`);
        }
      
        for (;;) {
          const job = await claimJob();
          if (!job) {
            if (!watch) break;
            await new Promise((done) => setTimeout(done, 5000));
            continue;
          }
      
          counters.claimed += 1;
          try {
            await processJob(job, counters);
          } catch (error) {
            const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
            const status = await fail(job, message);
            if (status === "failed") counters.failed += 1;
            console.error(`job ${job.id} ${job.file_name} -> ${status}: ${message}`);
          }
      
          if (counters.claimed >= limit) break;
        }
      
        console.log(
          `\nclaimed ${counters.claimed}, ready ${counters.ready}, empty ${counters.empty}, ` +
            `skipped ${counters.skipped}, failed ${counters.failed}`,
        );
        if (counters.claimed === 0) console.log("Queue is empty.");
      }
      
      try {
        await run();
      } catch (error) {
        console.error(error instanceof Error ? error.message : error);
        process.exitCode = 1;
      } finally {
        await sql.end();
      }
      
- path: drizzle/migrations/0010_rag.sql
  content: |
      -- Phase 1: passage index, ingestion state, and owner-only chat history.
      -- Vector search stays in Postgres so RLS and cascade deletes apply to it.
      create extension if not exists vector with schema extensions;
      set search_path = public, extensions;
      
      create table public.document_chunks (
        id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
        content_id uuid not null references public.content_items(id) on delete cascade,
        chunk_index integer not null check (chunk_index >= 0),
        content text not null check (content <> ''),
        estimated_tokens integer not null check (estimated_tokens > 0),
        page integer check (page is null or page > 0),
        slide integer check (slide is null or slide > 0),
        heading text,
        embedding vector(1536) not null,
        embedding_model text not null default 'qwen/qwen3-embedding-8b',
        created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
        unique (content_id, chunk_index),
        check (page is null or slide is null)
      );
      
      create index document_chunks_embedding_idx
        on public.document_chunks
        using hnsw (embedding vector_cosine_ops);
      
      create table public.ingestion_jobs (
        id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
        content_id uuid not null unique references public.content_items(id) on delete cascade,
        status text not null default 'queued'
          check (status in ('queued', 'running', 'ready', 'empty', 'skipped', 'failed')),
        attempts integer not null default 0 check (attempts >= 0),
        chunk_count integer not null default 0 check (chunk_count >= 0),
        embedding_model text not null default 'qwen/qwen3-embedding-8b',
        dimensions integer not null default 1536,
        warnings jsonb not null default '[]'::jsonb
          check (jsonb_typeof(warnings) = 'array'),
        error text,
        started_at timestamptz,
        finished_at timestamptz,
        created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
        updated_at timestamptz not null default now()
      );
      
      create table public.chat_threads (
        id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
        user_id uuid not null default auth.uid()
          references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
        title text not null default 'New chat',
        created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
        updated_at timestamptz not null default now()
      );
      
      create index chat_threads_owner_idx
        on public.chat_threads (user_id, updated_at desc);
      
      create table public.chat_messages (
        id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
        thread_id uuid not null references public.chat_threads(id) on delete cascade,
        role text not null check (role in ('user', 'assistant')),
        content text not null check (content <> ''),
        citations jsonb not null default '[]'::jsonb
          check (jsonb_typeof(citations) = 'array'),
        created_at timestamptz not null default now()
      );
      
      create index chat_messages_thread_idx
        on public.chat_messages (thread_id, created_at);
      
      grant select on public.document_chunks to authenticated;
      grant all on public.document_chunks to service_role;
      alter table public.document_chunks enable row level security;
      create policy "Allowed users read chunks" on public.document_chunks
        for select to authenticated
        using (
          public.is_allowed_email(auth.jwt() ->> 'email')
          and exists (
            select 1 from public.content_items ci
            where ci.id = document_chunks.content_id
          )
        );
      
      grant select on public.ingestion_jobs to authenticated;
      grant all on public.ingestion_jobs to service_role;
      alter table public.ingestion_jobs enable row level security;
      create policy "Admins read ingestion jobs" on public.ingestion_jobs
        for select to authenticated
        using (public.is_content_admin());
      
      grant select, insert, update, delete on public.chat_threads to authenticated;
      grant all on public.chat_threads to service_role;
      alter table public.chat_threads enable row level security;
      create policy "Owners manage threads" on public.chat_threads
        for all to authenticated
        using (user_id = auth.uid() and public.is_allowed_email(auth.jwt() ->> 'email'))
        with check (user_id = auth.uid() and public.is_allowed_email(auth.jwt() ->> 'email'));
      
      grant select, insert on public.chat_messages to authenticated;
      grant all on public.chat_messages to service_role;
      alter table public.chat_messages enable row level security;
      create policy "Owners manage messages" on public.chat_messages
        for all to authenticated
        using (
          exists (
            select 1 from public.chat_threads t
            where t.id = chat_messages.thread_id and t.user_id = auth.uid()
          )
        )
        with check (
          exists (
            select 1 from public.chat_threads t
            where t.id = chat_messages.thread_id and t.user_id = auth.uid()
          )
        );
      
      grant usage on schema extensions to authenticated;
      
      create or replace function public.match_document_chunks(
        query_embedding vector,
        match_count integer default 8,
        filter_industry text default null
      )
      returns table (
        chunk_id uuid,
        content_id uuid,
        content text,
        page integer,
        slide integer,
        heading text,
        title text,
        industry text,
        customer_name text,
        file_name text,
        distance double precision
      )
      language sql
      stable
      security invoker
      set search_path = public, extensions
      as $$
        select
          c.id as chunk_id,
          c.content_id,
          c.content,
          c.page,
          c.slide,
          c.heading,
          ci.title,
          ci.industry,
          ci.customer_name,
          ci.file_name,
          c.embedding <=> query_embedding as distance
        from public.document_chunks c
        join public.content_items ci on ci.id = c.content_id
        where filter_industry is null or ci.industry = filter_industry
        order by c.embedding <=> query_embedding
        limit least(greatest(coalesce(match_count, 8), 1), 20);
      $$;
      
      revoke all on function public.match_document_chunks(vector, integer, text)
        from public, anon;
      grant execute on function public.match_document_chunks(vector, integer, text)
        to authenticated;
      
      reset search_path;
      
```

```
- path: drizzle/migrations/0011_rag_grants.sql
  content: |
      -- Supabase's default privileges grant ALL on new public tables to anon and
      -- authenticated. Keep the RAG tables to their intended privileges.
      revoke all on public.document_chunks from anon, authenticated;
      grant select on public.document_chunks to authenticated;
      
      revoke all on public.ingestion_jobs from anon, authenticated;
      grant select on public.ingestion_jobs to authenticated;
      
      revoke all on public.chat_threads from anon, authenticated;
      grant select, insert, update, delete on public.chat_threads to authenticated;
      
      revoke all on public.chat_messages from anon, authenticated;
      grant select, insert on public.chat_messages to authenticated;
      
- path: drizzle/migrations/0012_rag_reconcile.sql
  content: |
      -- Phase 1 reconciliation. 0010 created the passage index and RLS but left out
      -- three things Phase 2/3 depend on: the full-text index, a content hash for
      -- idempotent re-ingestion, and an atomic chunk replacement RPC.
      --
      -- Status values stay as applied in 0010 (queued/running/ready/empty/skipped/
      -- failed); they carry more meaning than a single 'unsupported': 'empty' means a
      -- supported format yielded no usable text (probable scan), 'skipped' means the
      -- format is not extracted. Images/videos land in 'skipped'.
      set search_path = public, extensions;
      
      -- Full-text half of hybrid retrieval. An explicit regconfig keeps the
      -- expression immutable, which generated columns require.
      alter table public.document_chunks
        add column tsv tsvector generated always as (to_tsvector('english', content)) stored;
      
      create index document_chunks_tsv_idx
        on public.document_chunks using gin (tsv);
      
      -- Hash of the extracted text, so a retry can skip embedding unchanged content.
      alter table public.ingestion_jobs
        add column content_hash text;
      
      -- Claim query filters on status and orders oldest first.
      create index ingestion_jobs_claim_idx
        on public.ingestion_jobs (status, created_at);
      
      -- Replaces every chunk for one item in a single statement set, so a retry can
      -- never duplicate passages and a failure leaves the previous chunks intact.
      -- Callable by the trusted ingestion worker only; user sessions never write here.
      create or replace function public.replace_document_chunks(
        p_content_id uuid,
        p_chunks jsonb,
        p_content_hash text default null,
        p_status text default 'ready',
        p_warnings jsonb default '[]'::jsonb
      )
      returns integer
      language plpgsql
      security definer
      set search_path = public, extensions
      as $$
      declare
        v_chunk_count integer;
      begin
        if p_chunks is null or jsonb_typeof(p_chunks) <> 'array' then
          raise exception 'p_chunks must be a JSON array';
        end if;
        if p_status not in ('ready', 'empty') then
          raise exception 'p_status must be ready or empty, got %', p_status;
        end if;
        if p_warnings is null or jsonb_typeof(p_warnings) <> 'array' then
          raise exception 'p_warnings must be a JSON array';
        end if;
        if not exists (select 1 from public.content_items ci where ci.id = p_content_id) then
          raise exception 'content item % not found', p_content_id;
        end if;
      
        delete from public.document_chunks where content_id = p_content_id;
      
        insert into public.document_chunks (
          content_id,
          chunk_index,
          content,
          estimated_tokens,
          page,
          slide,
          heading,
          embedding,
          embedding_model
        )
        select
          p_content_id,
          (chunk ->> 'chunk_index')::integer,
          chunk ->> 'content',
          greatest(coalesce((chunk ->> 'estimated_tokens')::integer, 1), 1),
          nullif(chunk ->> 'page', '')::integer,
          nullif(chunk ->> 'slide', '')::integer,
          nullif(chunk ->> 'heading', ''),
          (chunk -> 'embedding')::text::vector,
          coalesce(nullif(chunk ->> 'embedding_model', ''), 'qwen/qwen3-embedding-8b')
        from jsonb_array_elements(p_chunks) as chunk;
      
        select count(*) into v_chunk_count
        from public.document_chunks
        where content_id = p_content_id;
      
        update public.ingestion_jobs
           set status = p_status,
               chunk_count = v_chunk_count,
               content_hash = coalesce(p_content_hash, content_hash),
               warnings = p_warnings,
               error = null,
               finished_at = now(),
               updated_at = now()
         where content_id = p_content_id;
      
        return v_chunk_count;
      end;
      $$;
      
      revoke all on function public.replace_document_chunks(uuid, jsonb, text, text, jsonb)
        from public, anon, authenticated;
      grant execute on function public.replace_document_chunks(uuid, jsonb, text, text, jsonb)
        to service_role;
      
      reset search_path;
      
- path: drizzle/migrations/0013_ingestion_enqueue.sql
  content: |
      -- Queue an ingestion job whenever content enters the library, or when its stored
      -- file changes (binary replacement, move/copy). Metadata edits such as a title
      -- or customer rename never re-queue, so company renames stay cheap.
      set search_path = public, extensions;
      
      create or replace function public.enqueue_ingestion_job()
      returns trigger
      language plpgsql
      security definer
      set search_path = public
      as $$
      begin
        insert into public.ingestion_jobs (content_id, status)
        values (new.id, 'queued')
        on conflict (content_id) do update
           set status = 'queued',
               attempts = 0,
               error = null,
               started_at = null,
               finished_at = null,
               updated_at = now();
        return new;
      end;
      $$;
      
      revoke all on function public.enqueue_ingestion_job() from public, anon, authenticated;
      
      create trigger content_enqueue_ingestion
        after insert on public.content_items
        for each row execute function public.enqueue_ingestion_job();
      
      create trigger content_requeue_ingestion_on_file_change
        after update of file_path, file_size on public.content_items
        for each row
        when (
          old.file_path is distinct from new.file_path
          or old.file_size is distinct from new.file_size
        )
        execute function public.enqueue_ingestion_job();
      
      reset search_path;
      
- path: scripts/apply-sql.ts
  content: |
      /**
       * Applies explicit SQL migration files in order, all inside one transaction.
       * Usage: bun scripts/apply-sql.ts --all
       *        bun scripts/apply-sql.ts drizzle/migrations/0012_foo.sql
       */
      import { readFileSync, readdirSync } from "node:fs";
      import { join } from "node:path";
      import postgres from "postgres";
      
      const dir = "drizzle/migrations";
      const args = process.argv.slice(2);
      const files = args.includes("--all")
        ? readdirSync(dir)
            .filter((file) => file.endsWith(".sql"))
            .sort()
            .map((file) => join(dir, file))
        : args.filter((arg) => arg !== "--all");
      
      if (!process.env.SUPABASE_DB_URL) {
        console.error("SUPABASE_DB_URL is missing from .env");
        process.exit(1);
      }
      
      if (files.length === 0) {
        console.error("Pass SQL files to apply, or --all for a fresh database.");
        process.exit(1);
      }
      
      const sql = postgres(process.env.SUPABASE_DB_URL, { ssl: "require", max: 1 });
      
      try {
        await sql.begin(async (tx) => {
          for (const file of files) {
            const contents = readFileSync(file, "utf8");
            await tx.unsafe(contents);
            console.log(`applied ${file}`);
          }
        });
        console.log(`done: ${files.length} file(s)`);
      } catch (error) {
        console.error("migration failed, transaction rolled back");
        console.error(error instanceof Error ? error.message : error);
        process.exitCode = 1;
      } finally {
        await sql.end();
      }
      
- path: scripts/verify-rag.ts
  content: |
      /**
       * RLS smoke test for the RAG tables. Creates throwaway users/content inside one
       * transaction and rolls everything back, so it is safe to run against any DB.
       * Usage: bun scripts/verify-rag.ts
       */
      import postgres from "postgres";
      
      if (!process.env.SUPABASE_DB_URL) {
        console.error("SUPABASE_DB_URL is missing from .env");
        process.exit(1);
      }
      
      type User = { id: string; email: string };
      type Tx = Parameters<Parameters<ReturnType<typeof postgres>["begin"]>[0]>[0];
      
      const sql = postgres(process.env.SUPABASE_DB_URL, { ssl: "require", max: 1 });
      const memberA: User = { id: crypto.randomUUID(), email: "rag-member-a@aionos.ai" };
      const memberB: User = { id: crypto.randomUUID(), email: "rag-member-b@aionos.ai" };
      const admin: User = { id: crypto.randomUUID(), email: "rag-admin@aionos.ai" };
      let passed = 0;
      
      function check(ok: boolean, label: string) {
        if (!ok) throw new Error(`FAIL: ${label}`);
        passed += 1;
        console.log(`PASS: ${label}`);
      }
      
      async function asUser<T>(tx: Tx, user: User, fn: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
        await tx`select set_config('request.jwt.claims', ${JSON.stringify({ sub: user.id, email: user.email })}, true)`;
        await tx`set local role authenticated`;
        try {
          return await fn();
        } finally {
          await tx`reset role`;
        }
      }
      
      async function expectDenied(tx: Tx, user: User | null, label: string, fn: () => Promise<unknown>) {
        await tx.unsafe("savepoint expected_denial");
        try {
          if (user) {
            await tx`select set_config('request.jwt.claims', ${JSON.stringify({ sub: user.id, email: user.email })}, true)`;
            await tx`set local role authenticated`;
          } else {
            await tx`set local role anon`;
          }
          await fn();
          throw new Error(`EXPECTED_DENIAL_SUCCEEDED:${label}`);
        } catch (error) {
          const err = error as { code?: string; message?: string };
          if (err.message?.startsWith("EXPECTED_DENIAL_SUCCEEDED")) throw error;
          await tx.unsafe("rollback to savepoint expected_denial");
          if (err.code !== "42501") throw error;
          passed += 1;
          console.log(`PASS: ${label} (${err.code})`);
        }
      }
      
      async function seedUser(tx: Tx, user: User) {
        await tx`
          insert into auth.users
            (id, email, email_confirmed_at, raw_app_meta_data, raw_user_meta_data, aud, role, created_at, updated_at)
          values
            (${user.id}, ${user.email}, now(),
             ${JSON.stringify({ provider: "email", providers: ["email"] })}::jsonb, '{}'::jsonb,
             'authenticated', 'authenticated', now(), now())
        `;
        await tx`
          insert into auth.identities
            (provider_id, user_id, identity_data, provider, last_sign_in_at, created_at, updated_at)
          values
            (${user.id}, ${user.id}, ${JSON.stringify({ sub: user.id, email: user.email })}::jsonb,
             'email', now(), now(), now())
        `;
      }
      
      // Same shape the ingestion worker sends to replace_document_chunks. Pass the
      // value as JSON (sql.json), never as a string cast to jsonb: a cast turns the
      // whole payload into a JSON string instead of an array.
      function chunkEntries(entries: Array<{ index: number; content: string }>) {
        return entries.map((entry) => ({
          chunk_index: entry.index,
          content: entry.content,
          estimated_tokens: 3,
          page: 1,
          slide: null,
          heading: null,
          embedding: new Array(1536).fill(0),
        }));
      }
      
      try {
        await sql.begin(async (tx) => {
          const fn = await tx`
            select p.prosecdef
            from pg_proc p
            join pg_namespace n on n.oid = p.pronamespace
            where n.nspname = 'public' and p.proname = 'match_document_chunks'
          `;
          check(fn[0]?.prosecdef === false, "match_document_chunks is security invoker");
      
          const index = await tx`
            select indexdef from pg_indexes
            where schemaname = 'public' and indexname = 'document_chunks_embedding_idx'
          `;
          check(index[0]?.indexdef.includes("hnsw"), "document_chunks_embedding_idx uses HNSW");
      
          const anonGrant = await tx`
            select has_table_privilege('anon', 'public.document_chunks', 'SELECT') as can_select
          `;
          check(!anonGrant[0].can_select, "anon has no chunk SELECT grant");
      
          await seedUser(tx, memberA);
          await seedUser(tx, memberB);
          await seedUser(tx, admin);
          await tx`insert into public.user_roles (user_id, role) values (${admin.id}, 'admin') on conflict do nothing`;
      
          const [content] = await tx`
            insert into public.content_items
              (industry, title, file_path, file_name, uploaded_by, uploader_email)
            values
              ('airlines', 'RLS test file', ${`test/${crypto.randomUUID()}.pdf`}, 'rls.pdf',
               ${memberA.id}, ${memberA.email})
            returning id
          `;
          await tx`
            insert into public.document_chunks
              (content_id, chunk_index, content, estimated_tokens, page, embedding)
            values
              (${content.id}, 0, 'RLS test passage', 3, 1, array_fill(0.0::real, ARRAY[1536])::vector)
          `;
          const [queuedJob] = await tx`
            select status from public.ingestion_jobs where content_id = ${content.id}
          `;
          check(queuedJob?.status === "queued", "content insert queues an ingestion job");
      
          const chunkCount = await asUser(
            tx,
            memberA,
            async () => (await tx`select count(*)::int as n from public.document_chunks`)[0].n,
          );
          check(chunkCount === 1, "member A reads chunks");
      
          const rpcCount = await asUser(
            tx,
            memberA,
            async () =>
              (
                await tx`select count(*)::int as n from public.match_document_chunks(
                  array_fill(0.0::real, ARRAY[1536])::vector, 5, null
                )`
              )[0].n,
          );
          check(rpcCount === 1, "member A retrieves via match_document_chunks");
      
          const filteredIn = await asUser(
            tx,
            memberA,
            async () =>
              (
                await tx`select count(*)::int as n from public.match_document_chunks(
                  array_fill(0.0::real, ARRAY[1536])::vector, 5, 'airlines'
                )`
              )[0].n,
          );
          check(filteredIn === 1, "industry filter includes matching chunk");
      
          const filteredOut = await asUser(
            tx,
            memberA,
            async () =>
              (
                await tx`select count(*)::int as n from public.match_document_chunks(
                  array_fill(0.0::real, ARRAY[1536])::vector, 5, 'bfsi'
                )`
              )[0].n,
          );
          check(filteredOut === 0, "industry filter excludes non-matching chunk");
      
          const memberBChunks = await asUser(
            tx,
            memberB,
            async () => (await tx`select count(*)::int as n from public.document_chunks`)[0].n,
          );
          check(memberBChunks === 1, "member B reads chunks");
      
          const memberJobs = await asUser(
            tx,
            memberA,
            async () => (await tx`select count(*)::int as n from public.ingestion_jobs`)[0].n,
          );
          check(memberJobs === 0, "member A cannot read ingestion jobs");
      
          const adminJobs = await asUser(
            tx,
            admin,
            async () => (await tx`select count(*)::int as n from public.ingestion_jobs`)[0].n,
          );
          check(adminJobs === 1, "admin reads ingestion jobs");
      
          const replacement = chunkEntries([
            { index: 0, content: "First replacement passage." },
            { index: 1, content: "Second replacement passage." },
          ]);
          const [firstReplace] = await tx`
            select public.replace_document_chunks(
              ${content.id}, ${tx.json(replacement)}, 'hash-v1'
            ) as n
          `;
          check(firstReplace.n === 2, "replace_document_chunks writes every chunk");
      
          const [secondReplace] = await tx`
            select public.replace_document_chunks(
              ${content.id}, ${tx.json(replacement)}, 'hash-v1'
            ) as n
          `;
          const afterReplace = await tx`
            select count(*)::int as n from public.document_chunks where content_id = ${content.id}
          `;
          check(
            secondReplace.n === 2 && afterReplace[0].n === 2,
            "re-running replacement never duplicates chunks",
          );
      
          const [job] = await tx`
            select content_hash, chunk_count, status from public.ingestion_jobs
            where content_id = ${content.id}
          `;
          check(
            job.content_hash === "hash-v1" && job.chunk_count === 2 && job.status === "ready",
            "replacement records hash, chunk count and status",
          );
      
          const [fts] = await tx`
            select count(*)::int as n from public.document_chunks
            where content_id = ${content.id}
              and tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', 'replacement passage')
          `;
          check(fts.n === 2, "full-text index covers replaced chunks");
      
          const [ftsFiltered] = await tx`
            select count(*)::int as n from public.document_chunks
            where tsv @@ websearch_to_tsquery('english', 'zebra')
          `;
          check(ftsFiltered.n === 0, "full-text search misses unrelated terms");
      
          await tx`update public.content_items set title = 'Renamed file' where id = ${content.id}`;
          const [afterTitle] = await tx`
            select status from public.ingestion_jobs where content_id = ${content.id}
          `;
          check(afterTitle.status === "ready", "metadata edits do not requeue ingestion");
      
          await tx`update public.content_items set file_size = 4242 where id = ${content.id}`;
          const [afterFile] = await tx`
            select status from public.ingestion_jobs where content_id = ${content.id}
          `;
          check(afterFile.status === "queued", "changing the stored file requeues ingestion");
      
          await expectDenied(tx, memberA, "member cannot replace chunks", async () => {
            await tx`select public.replace_document_chunks(${content.id}, '[]'::jsonb)`;
          });
      
          await expectDenied(tx, null, "anon cannot replace chunks", async () => {
            await tx`select public.replace_document_chunks(${content.id}, '[]'::jsonb)`;
          });
      
          const [thread] = await asUser(
            tx,
            memberA,
            async () =>
              await tx`insert into public.chat_threads (title) values ('A thread') returning id`,
          );
          await asUser(
            tx,
            memberA,
            async () =>
              await tx`insert into public.chat_messages (thread_id, role, content)
                       values (${thread.id}, 'user', 'hello')`,
          );
      
          const ownThreads = await asUser(
            tx,
            memberA,
            async () => (await tx`select count(*)::int as n from public.chat_threads`)[0].n,
          );
          check(ownThreads === 1, "member A reads own thread");
      
          const ownMessages = await asUser(
            tx,
            memberA,
            async () => (await tx`select count(*)::int as n from public.chat_messages`)[0].n,
          );
          check(ownMessages === 1, "member A reads own message");
      
          const otherThreads = await asUser(
            tx,
            memberB,
            async () => (await tx`select count(*)::int as n from public.chat_threads`)[0].n,
          );
          check(otherThreads === 0, "member B cannot read member A's thread");
      
          const otherMessages = await asUser(
            tx,
            memberB,
            async () => (await tx`select count(*)::int as n from public.chat_messages`)[0].n,
          );
          check(otherMessages === 0, "member B cannot read member A's message");
      
          const adminThreads = await asUser(
            tx,
            admin,
            async () => (await tx`select count(*)::int as n from public.chat_threads`)[0].n,
          );
          check(adminThreads === 0, "admin cannot read another user's thread");
      
          await expectDenied(tx, memberB, "member B cannot insert into member A's thread", async () => {
            await tx`insert into public.chat_messages (thread_id, role, content)
                     values (${thread.id}, 'user', 'intrusion')`;
          });
      
          await expectDenied(tx, memberA, "member A cannot insert chunks", async () => {
            await tx`insert into public.document_chunks
                     (content_id, chunk_index, content, estimated_tokens, page, embedding)
                     values (${content.id}, 1, 'poison', 3, 1, array_fill(0.0::real, ARRAY[1536])::vector)`;
          });
      
          await expectDenied(tx, admin, "admin cannot insert chunks", async () => {
            await tx`insert into public.document_chunks
                     (content_id, chunk_index, content, estimated_tokens, page, embedding)
                     values (${content.id}, 2, 'poison', 3, 1, array_fill(0.0::real, ARRAY[1536])::vector)`;
          });
      
          await expectDenied(tx, null, "anon cannot read chunks", async () => {
            await tx`select count(*) from public.document_chunks`;
          });
      
          await expectDenied(tx, null, "anon cannot execute match_document_chunks", async () => {
            await tx`select count(*) from public.match_document_chunks(
              array_fill(0.0::real, ARRAY[1536])::vector, 5, null
            )`;
          });
      
          await tx`delete from public.content_items where id = ${content.id}`;
          const chunksAfterDelete = await tx`select count(*)::int as n from public.document_chunks`;
          check(chunksAfterDelete[0].n === 0, "content delete cascades chunks");
          const jobsAfterDelete = await tx`select count(*)::int as n from public.ingestion_jobs`;
          check(jobsAfterDelete[0].n === 0, "content delete cascades ingestion job");
      
          await tx`delete from public.chat_threads where id = ${thread.id}`;
          const messagesAfterDelete = await tx`select count(*)::int as n from public.chat_messages`;
          check(messagesAfterDelete[0].n === 0, "thread delete cascades messages");
      
          throw new Error("__ROLLBACK__");
        });
      } catch (error) {
        if ((error as Error).message !== "__ROLLBACK__") {
          console.error((error as Error).message);
          process.exitCode = 1;
        }
      } finally {
        await sql.end();
      }
      
      if (process.exitCode !== 1) console.log(`\n${passed} checks passed; test data rolled back.`);
```

> _Reasoning_
> So current state:
> - Phase 0: extraction spike done (scripts/extract-spike.ts, src/lib/rag/chunk.ts, extract.ts, tests, spike-input, spike-output)
> - Phase 1: schema done (0010, 0011, 0012)
> - Phase 2: ingestion worker done as a local worker script (scripts/ingest-worker.ts), enqueue trigger 0013. There's no Edge Function supabase/functions/ingest-document; instead a Bun standalone worker. Need to check with the user's brief — brief said Edge Function. But the code chose a standalone worker (prior phase decisions). Let me check if there's mention in a docs or phase report.
> - Phase 3: retrieval — match_document_chunks exists (vector only, not hybrid). No searchLibrary server function, no debug page, no evals.
> - Phase 4/5: chat and eval — not done. Tables exist (chat_threads, chat_messages).
> 
> So the "next phase" is Phase 3: Retrieval and evaluation. The user says: "I will shortly receive the Service Role key and database connection string. Till then carry on with the next phase. Make sure the code is plug and play since i'm currently using my email password in .env in order to access the files from supabase. Also the supabase url, key, publishable key will be switched."
