```
- path: src/integrations/supabase/auth-middleware.ts
  content: |
      // This file is automatically generated. Do not edit it directly.
      import { createMiddleware } from '@tanstack/react-start'
      import { getRequest } from '@tanstack/react-start/server'
      import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'
      import type { Database } from './types'
      
      
      
      
      function isNewSupabaseApiKey(value: string): boolean {
        return value.startsWith('sb_publishable_') || value.startsWith('sb_secret_');
      }
      
      function createSupabaseFetch(supabaseKey: string): typeof fetch {
        return (input, init) => {
          const headers = new Headers(
            typeof Request !== 'undefined' && input instanceof Request ? input.headers : undefined,
          );
      
          if (init?.headers) {
            new Headers(init.headers).forEach((value, key) => headers.set(key, value));
          }
      
          // New Supabase API keys are opaque strings, not bearer JWTs.
          if (isNewSupabaseApiKey(supabaseKey) && headers.get('Authorization') === `Bearer ${supabaseKey}`) {
            headers.delete('Authorization');
          }
      
          headers.set('apikey', supabaseKey);
          return fetch(input, { ...init, headers });
        };
      }
      
      export const requireSupabaseAuth = createMiddleware({ type: 'function' }).server(
        async ({ next }) => {
          
          const SUPABASE_URL = process.env['SUPABASE_URL'];
          const SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY = process.env['SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY'];
      
          if (!SUPABASE_URL || !SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY) {
            const missing = [
              ...(!SUPABASE_URL ? ['SUPABASE_URL'] : []),
              ...(!SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY ? ['SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY'] : []),
            ];
            const message = `Missing Supabase environment variable(s): ${missing.join(', ')}. Connect Supabase in Lovable Cloud.`;
            console.error(`[Supabase] ${message}`);
            throw new Error(message);
          }
          
          const request = getRequest();
      
          if (!request?.headers) {
            throw new Error('Unauthorized: No request headers available');
          }
      
      
          const authHeader = request.headers.get('authorization');
      
          if (!authHeader) {
            throw new Error('Unauthorized: No authorization header provided');
          }
      
          if (!authHeader.startsWith('Bearer ')) {
            throw new Error('Unauthorized: Only Bearer tokens are supported');
          }
      
          const token = authHeader.replace('Bearer ', '');
          if (!token) {
            throw new Error('Unauthorized: No token provided');
          }
      
          if (token.split('.').length !== 3) {
            throw new Error('Unauthorized: Invalid token');
          }
      
          const supabase = createClient<Database>(
            SUPABASE_URL!,
            SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY!,
            {
              global: {
                fetch: createSupabaseFetch(SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY!),
                headers: {
                  Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
                },
              },
              auth: {
                storage: undefined,
                persistSession: false,
                autoRefreshToken: false,
              },
            }
          );
      
          const { data, error } = await supabase.auth.getClaims(token);
          if (error || !data?.claims) {
            throw new Error('Unauthorized: Invalid token');
          }
      
          if (!data.claims.sub) {
            throw new Error('Unauthorized: No user ID found in token');
          }
      
          return next({
            context: {
              supabase,
              userId: data.claims.sub,
              claims: data.claims,
            },
          });
        },
      );
      
- path: src/integrations/supabase/client.server.ts
  content: |
      // This file is automatically generated. Do not edit it directly.
      // Server-side Supabase client with service role key - bypasses RLS.
      // Use this for admin operations in server functions and server routes only.
      // For user-authenticated queries (with RLS), use the auth middleware instead.
      import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
      import type { Database } from './types';
      
      function isNewSupabaseApiKey(value: string): boolean {
        return value.startsWith('sb_publishable_') || value.startsWith('sb_secret_');
      }
      
      function createSupabaseFetch(supabaseKey: string): typeof fetch {
        return (input, init) => {
          const headers = new Headers(
            typeof Request !== 'undefined' && input instanceof Request ? input.headers : undefined,
          );
      
          if (init?.headers) {
            new Headers(init.headers).forEach((value, key) => headers.set(key, value));
          }
      
          // New Supabase API keys are opaque strings, not bearer JWTs.
          if (isNewSupabaseApiKey(supabaseKey) && headers.get('Authorization') === `Bearer ${supabaseKey}`) {
            headers.delete('Authorization');
          }
      
          headers.set('apikey', supabaseKey);
          return fetch(input, { ...init, headers });
        };
      }
      
      function createSupabaseAdminClient() {
        const SUPABASE_URL = process.env['SUPABASE_URL'];
        const SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY = process.env['SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY'];
      
        if (!SUPABASE_URL || !SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY) {
          const missing = [
            ...(!SUPABASE_URL ? ['SUPABASE_URL'] : []),
            ...(!SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY ? ['SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY'] : []),
          ];
          const message = `Missing Supabase environment variable(s): ${missing.join(', ')}. Connect Supabase in Lovable Cloud.`;
          console.error(`[Supabase] ${message}`);
          throw new Error(message);
        }
      
        return createClient<Database>(SUPABASE_URL, SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY, {
          global: {
            fetch: createSupabaseFetch(SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY),
          },
          auth: {
            storage: undefined,
            persistSession: false,
            autoRefreshToken: false,
          }
        });
      }
      
      let _supabaseAdmin: ReturnType<typeof createSupabaseAdminClient> | undefined;
      
      // Server-side Supabase client with service role - bypasses RLS
      // SECURITY: Only use this for trusted server-side operations, never expose to client code
      // Load inside server handlers: const { supabaseAdmin } = await import("@/integrations/supabase/client.server");
      // Top-level import is safe only in other .server.ts modules - route files and *.functions.ts ship to the client bundle.
      export const supabaseAdmin = new Proxy({} as ReturnType<typeof createSupabaseAdminClient>, {
        get(_, prop, receiver) {
          if (!_supabaseAdmin) _supabaseAdmin = createSupabaseAdminClient();
          return Reflect.get(_supabaseAdmin, prop, receiver);
        },
      });
      
- path: src/integrations/supabase/cron-auth.ts
  content: |
      // This file is automatically generated. Do not edit it directly.
      export async function authenticateCronRequest(
        request: Request,
      ): Promise<Response | null> {
        const currentSecret = process.env['LOVABLE_CRON_SECRET']
        const previousSecret = process.env['LOVABLE_CRON_SECRET_PREVIOUS']
      
        if (!currentSecret) {
          return new Response('Server configuration error', { status: 500 })
        }
      
        const match = /^Bearer ([^\s,]+)$/.exec(
          request.headers.get('authorization') ?? '',
        )
        const token = match?.[1]
        if (!token) {
          return new Response('Unauthorized', { status: 401 })
        }
      
        const { createHash, timingSafeEqual } = await import('node:crypto')
        const digest = (value: string) =>
          createHash('sha256').update(value, 'utf8').digest()
        const providedDigest = digest(token)
        const currentMatches = timingSafeEqual(providedDigest, digest(currentSecret))
        const previousMatches = timingSafeEqual(
          providedDigest,
          digest(previousSecret ?? currentSecret),
        )
      
        if (!currentMatches && !previousMatches) {
          return new Response('Unauthorized', { status: 401 })
        }
      
        return null
      }
```

```
- path: src/lib/rag/chunk.test.ts
  content: |
      import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
      import { chunkSections, estimateTokens } from "./chunk";
      
      describe("chunkSections", () => {
        it("keeps short sections whole and preserves page, heading, index and source", () => {
          const chunks = chunkSections(
            [
              { text: "First page.", pageOrSlide: 1 },
              { text: "Overview body.", pageOrSlide: 2, heading: "Overview" },
            ],
            { sourceFile: "deck.pptx" },
          );
      
          expect(chunks).toHaveLength(2);
          expect(chunks[0]).toMatchObject({
            chunkIndex: 0,
            pageOrSlide: 1,
            heading: null,
            sourceFile: "deck.pptx",
          });
          expect(chunks[1]).toMatchObject({
            chunkIndex: 1,
            pageOrSlide: 2,
            heading: "Overview",
            sourceFile: "deck.pptx",
          });
          expect(chunks[1]?.content).toBe("Overview\n\nOverview body.");
        });
      
        it("never merges separate pages or slides", () => {
          const chunks = chunkSections(
            [
              { text: "Page one.", pageOrSlide: 1 },
              { text: "Page two.", pageOrSlide: 2 },
              { text: "Page three.", pageOrSlide: 3 },
            ],
            { sourceFile: "report.pdf" },
          );
      
          expect(chunks.map((chunk) => chunk.pageOrSlide)).toEqual([1, 2, 3]);
          expect(chunks.map((chunk) => chunk.chunkIndex)).toEqual([0, 1, 2]);
        });
      
        it("splits long text and repeats the tail of the previous chunk", () => {
          const text = Array.from(
            { length: 40 },
            (_, index) => `Sentence ${index} explains the proposal in enough detail to fill space.`,
          ).join(" ");
          const chunks = chunkSections([{ text, pageOrSlide: 7 }], {
            sourceFile: "long.pdf",
            targetTokens: 150,
            overlapTokens: 20,
          });
      
          expect(chunks.length).toBeGreaterThan(1);
          expect(chunks.every((chunk) => chunk.pageOrSlide === 7)).toBe(true);
          expect(chunks.map((chunk) => chunk.chunkIndex)).toEqual(chunks.map((_, index) => index));
      
          const first = chunks[0];
          const second = chunks[1];
          expect(first).toBeDefined();
          expect(second).toBeDefined();
          const lastWords = (first?.content ?? "").split(/\s+/).slice(-3).join(" ");
          expect(second?.content).toContain(lastWords);
        });
      
        it("splits a single oversized paragraph without exceeding the target", () => {
          const text = Array.from({ length: 400 }, (_, index) => `token${index}`).join(" ");
          const chunks = chunkSections([{ text }], {
            sourceFile: "scan.pdf",
            targetTokens: 100,
            overlapTokens: 20,
          });
      
          expect(chunks.length).toBeGreaterThan(1);
          expect(chunks.every((chunk) => estimateTokens(chunk.content) <= 100)).toBe(true);
        });
      
        it("keeps the heading on every chunk of a long section", () => {
          const text = Array.from(
            { length: 30 },
            (_, index) => `Pricing paragraph ${index} contains enough words to be split.`,
          ).join(" ");
          const chunks = chunkSections([{ text, pageOrSlide: 3, heading: "Pricing" }], {
            sourceFile: "proposal.docx",
            targetTokens: 100,
            overlapTokens: 20,
          });
      
          expect(chunks.length).toBeGreaterThan(1);
          expect(chunks.every((chunk) => chunk.content.startsWith("Pricing\n\n"))).toBe(true);
        });
      
        it("skips sections with no extractable text", () => {
          const chunks = chunkSections(
            [{ text: "  \n\n " }, { text: "" }, { text: "Real text.", pageOrSlide: 9 }],
            { sourceFile: "mixed.pdf" },
          );
      
          expect(chunks).toHaveLength(1);
          expect(chunks[0]?.chunkIndex).toBe(0);
          expect(chunks[0]?.pageOrSlide).toBe(9);
        });
      });
      
      describe("estimateTokens", () => {
        it("approximates four characters per token", () => {
          expect(estimateTokens("")).toBe(0);
          expect(estimateTokens("1234")).toBe(1);
          expect(estimateTokens("12345")).toBe(2);
        });
      });
      
- path: src/lib/rag/extract.test.ts
  content: |
      import { strToU8, zipSync } from "fflate";
      import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
      import { extractDocument, isLowText, slideXmlToText } from "./extract";
      
      const SLIDE_NS =
        'xmlns:a="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/drawingml/2006/main" ' +
        'xmlns:p="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/presentationml/2006/main"';
      
      function zip(parts: Record<string, string>): Uint8Array {
        return zipSync(
          Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(parts).map(([path, text]) => [path, strToU8(text)])),
        );
      }
      
      // A deck shaped like the known-bad library files: valid slide XML, broken image rels.
      const brokenDeck = zip({
        "[Content_Types].xml":
          '<Types xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/content-types"/>',
        "ppt/slides/slide1.xml": `<p:sld ${SLIDE_NS}><p:cSld><p:spTree>
          <a:p><a:r><a:t>Acme Energy &amp; Utilities</a:t></a:r></a:p>
          <a:p><a:r><a:t>Q3 pipeline review</a:t></a:r></a:p>
        </p:spTree></p:cSld></p:sld>`,
        "ppt/slides/_rels/slide1.xml.rels": '<Relationships><Relationship Target="../media/image1.png"',
        "ppt/slides/slide2.xml": `<p:sld ${SLIDE_NS}><p:cSld><p:spTree>
          <a:p><a:r><a:t>Roadmap</a:t></a:r></a:p>
        </p:spTree></p:cSld></p:sld>`,
        "ppt/slides/slide3.xml": `<p:sld ${SLIDE_NS}><p:cSld><p:spTree><p:pic/></p:spTree></p:cSld></p:sld>`,
        "ppt/notesSlides/notesSlide1.xml": `<p:notes ${SLIDE_NS}>
          <a:p><a:r><a:t>Mention the new logo.</a:t></a:r></a:p>
        </p:notes>`,
        "ppt/notesSlides/_rels/notesSlide1.xml.rels":
          '<Relationships><Relationship Target="../slides/slide2.xml"/></Relationships>',
      });
      
      const docx = zip({
        "[Content_Types].xml": `<Types xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/content-types">
          <Default Extension="rels" ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-package.relationships+xml"/>
          <Default Extension="xml" ContentType="application/xml"/>
          <Override PartName="/word/document.xml" ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document.main+xml"/>
          <Override PartName="/word/styles.xml" ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.styles+xml"/>
        </Types>`,
        "_rels/.rels": `<Relationships xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/relationships">
          <Relationship Id="rId1" Type="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships/officeDocument" Target="word/document.xml"/>
        </Relationships>`,
        "word/_rels/document.xml.rels": `<Relationships xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/relationships">
          <Relationship Id="rId1" Type="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships/styles" Target="styles.xml"/>
        </Relationships>`,
        "word/styles.xml": `<w:styles xmlns:w="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/wordprocessingml/2006/main">
          <w:style w:type="paragraph" w:styleId="Heading1"><w:name w:val="heading 1"/></w:style>
          <w:style w:type="paragraph" w:styleId="SectionHeading"><w:name w:val="Section Heading"/></w:style>
        </w:styles>`,
        "word/document.xml": `<w:document xmlns:w="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/wordprocessingml/2006/main">
          <w:body>
            <w:p><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="Heading1"/></w:pPr><w:r><w:t>Pricing</w:t></w:r></w:p>
            <w:p><w:r><w:t>Enterprise plan costs $50k.</w:t></w:r></w:p>
            <w:p><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="Heading1"/></w:pPr><w:r><w:t>Timeline</w:t></w:r></w:p>
            <w:p><w:r><w:t>Six weeks from kickoff.</w:t></w:r></w:p>
            <w:p><w:pPr><w:pStyle w:val="SectionHeading"/></w:pPr><w:r><w:t>Appendix</w:t></w:r></w:p>
            <w:p><w:r><w:t>Extra details are in the appendix.</w:t></w:r></w:p>
          </w:body>
        </w:document>`,
      });
      
      describe("extractDocument", () => {
        it("extracts PPTX text while ignoring a broken image relationship", async () => {
          const result = await extractDocument("known-broken.pptx", "", brokenDeck);
      
          expect(result.ok).toBe(true);
          if (!result.ok) return;
          expect(result.document.sections.map((section) => section.pageOrSlide)).toEqual([1, 2, 3]);
          expect(result.document.sections[0]?.text).toBe("Acme Energy & Utilities\nQ3 pipeline review");
          expect(result.document.sections[1]?.text).toBe("Roadmap\n\nNotes: Mention the new logo.");
          expect(result.document.emptyUnits).toEqual([{ pageOrSlide: 3, heading: null }]);
          expect(result.document.warnings).toEqual([]);
        });
      
        it("keeps DOCX headings as section boundaries", async () => {
          const result = await extractDocument("proposal.docx", "", docx);
      
          expect(result.ok).toBe(true);
          if (!result.ok) return;
          expect(result.document.sections).toEqual([
            { text: "Enterprise plan costs $50k.", pageOrSlide: null, heading: "Pricing" },
            { text: "Six weeks from kickoff.", pageOrSlide: null, heading: "Timeline" },
            { text: "Extra details are in the appendix.", pageOrSlide: null, heading: "Appendix" },
          ]);
          expect(result.document.emptyUnits).toEqual([]);
        });
      
        it("reports a corrupt PowerPoint instead of throwing", async () => {
          const result = await extractDocument("broken.pptx", "", strToU8("not a zip"));
      
          expect(result.ok).toBe(false);
          if (result.ok) return;
          expect(result.format).toBe("pptx");
          expect(result.error).toBeTruthy();
        });
      
        it("marks video and images unsupported rather than failed", async () => {
          await expect(extractDocument("demo.mp4", "video/mp4", new Uint8Array())).resolves.toEqual({
            ok: false,
            format: "video",
            error: "Unsupported format: video",
          });
          await expect(extractDocument("scan.png", "image/png", new Uint8Array())).resolves.toEqual({
            ok: false,
            format: "image",
            error: "Unsupported format: image",
          });
        });
      });
      
      describe("slideXmlToText", () => {
        it("decodes XML entities in text runs", () => {
          expect(slideXmlToText("<a:p><a:r><a:t>R&amp;D &#8212; &#x2713;</a:t></a:r></a:p>")).toBe(
            "R&D — ✓",
          );
        });
      
        it("joins multiple runs inside one paragraph", () => {
          expect(
            slideXmlToText("<a:p><a:r><a:t>Hello </a:t></a:r><a:r><a:t>world</a:t></a:r></a:p>"),
          ).toBe("Hello world");
        });
      });
      
      describe("isLowText", () => {
        it("flags blank and nearly blank pages", () => {
          expect(isLowText("   \n\n ")).toBe(true);
          expect(isLowText("short")).toBe(true);
          expect(isLowText("This page has enough text to embed.")).toBe(false);
        });
      });
      
- path: src/lib/rag/ingest.test.ts
  content: |
      import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
      import { buildChunkRows, embedTexts, hashChunkContents } from "./ingest";
      
      const section = (text: string, pageOrSlide: number | null = 1) => ({
        text,
        pageOrSlide,
        heading: null,
      });
      
      function fakeEmbeddingProvider(dimensions = 1536) {
        const calls: Array<{ model: string; dimensions: number | undefined; inputs: string[] }> = [];
        const fetchImpl = vi.fn(async (_url: string, init: RequestInit) => {
          const body = JSON.parse(String(init.body)) as {
            model: string;
            input: string[];
            dimensions?: number;
          };
          calls.push({ model: body.model, dimensions: body.dimensions, inputs: body.input });
          return new Response(
            JSON.stringify({
              data: body.input.map((text, index) => ({
                index,
                embedding: new Array(dimensions).fill(text.length),
              })),
              usage: { prompt_tokens: body.input.length, cost: 0.001 },
            }),
            { status: 200 },
          );
        });
        return { calls, fetchImpl: fetchImpl as unknown as typeof fetch };
      }
      
      describe("buildChunkRows", () => {
        it("maps PDF pages into the page column", () => {
          const rows = buildChunkRows("pdf", [section("Alpha beta gamma.", 3)], "a.pdf");
          expect(rows).toHaveLength(1);
          expect(rows[0]?.page).toBe(3);
          expect(rows[0]?.slide).toBeNull();
          expect(rows[0]?.chunk_index).toBe(0);
          expect(rows[0]?.content).toContain("Alpha beta gamma.");
        });
      
        it("maps PowerPoint slides into the slide column", () => {
          const rows = buildChunkRows("pptx", [section("Slide body text.", 7)], "a.pptx");
          expect(rows[0]?.slide).toBe(7);
          expect(rows[0]?.page).toBeNull();
        });
      
        it("leaves both columns empty for Word documents", () => {
          const rows = buildChunkRows("docx", [section("Word body text.")], "a.docx");
          expect(rows[0]?.page).toBeNull();
          expect(rows[0]?.slide).toBeNull();
        });
      
        it("numbers chunks consecutively across sections", () => {
          const rows = buildChunkRows(
            "pdf",
            [section("First page text.", 1), section("Second page text.", 2)],
            "a.pdf",
          );
          expect(rows.map((row) => row.chunk_index)).toEqual([0, 1]);
          expect(rows.map((row) => row.estimated_tokens)).toEqual([
            expect.any(Number),
            expect.any(Number),
          ]);
          expect(rows.every((row) => row.estimated_tokens > 0)).toBe(true);
        });
      
        it("drops empty sections instead of writing blank chunks", () => {
          const rows = buildChunkRows("pdf", [section("   ", 1), section("Real text.", 2)], "a.pdf");
          expect(rows).toHaveLength(1);
          expect(rows[0]?.page).toBe(2);
        });
      });
      
      describe("hashChunkContents", () => {
        it("is stable for identical text", () => {
          expect(hashChunkContents(["a", "b"])).toBe(hashChunkContents(["a", "b"]));
        });
      
        it("changes when text changes", () => {
          expect(hashChunkContents(["a", "b"])).not.toBe(hashChunkContents(["a", "c"]));
        });
      
        it("is order sensitive", () => {
          expect(hashChunkContents(["a", "b"])).not.toBe(hashChunkContents(["b", "a"]));
        });
      });
      
      describe("embedTexts", () => {
        it("batches at the provider limit and preserves order", async () => {
          const { calls, fetchImpl } = fakeEmbeddingProvider();
          const texts = Array.from({ length: 250 }, (_, index) => "x".repeat(index + 1));
          const result = await embedTexts(texts, {
            apiKey: "test-key",
            baseUrl: "https://example.test/v1/",
            fetchImpl,
          });
      
          expect(calls.map((call) => call.inputs.length)).toEqual([100, 100, 50]);
          expect(calls[0]?.model).toBe("qwen/qwen3-embedding-8b");
          expect(calls[0]?.dimensions).toBe(1536);
          expect(result.embeddings).toHaveLength(250);
          // Fake vectors encode input length, so order is observable.
          expect(result.embeddings[0]?.[0]).toBe(1);
          expect(result.embeddings[249]?.[0]).toBe(250);
        });
      
        it("never exceeds 100 inputs per call even when asked to", async () => {
          const { calls, fetchImpl } = fakeEmbeddingProvider();
          await embedTexts(["a", "b", "c"], {
            apiKey: "test-key",
            baseUrl: "https://example.test/v1",
            batchSize: 500,
            fetchImpl,
          });
          expect(calls).toHaveLength(1);
          expect(calls[0]?.inputs).toHaveLength(3);
        });
      
        it("rejects vectors of the wrong dimension", async () => {
          const { fetchImpl } = fakeEmbeddingProvider(8);
          await expect(
            embedTexts(["a"], {
              apiKey: "test-key",
              baseUrl: "https://example.test/v1",
              dimensions: 1536,
              fetchImpl,
            }),
          ).rejects.toThrow(/dimension mismatch/i);
        });
      
        it("surfaces provider errors with their status", async () => {
          const fetchImpl = vi.fn(
            async () =>
              new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: { message: "rate limited" } }), { status: 429 }),
          ) as unknown as typeof fetch;
          await expect(
            embedTexts(["a"], { apiKey: "test-key", baseUrl: "https://example.test/v1", fetchImpl }),
          ).rejects.toThrow(/429.*rate limited/);
        });
      
        it("estimates cost when batched responses omit it", async () => {
          // Verified provider behaviour: 100-input responses carry usage without cost.
          const fetchImpl = vi.fn(async (_url: string, init: RequestInit) => {
            const body = JSON.parse(String(init.body)) as { input: string[] };
            return new Response(
              JSON.stringify({
                data: body.input.map((_text, index) => ({ index, embedding: new Array(1536).fill(0) })),
                usage: { prompt_tokens: 2000 },
              }),
              { status: 200 },
            );
          }) as unknown as typeof fetch;
      
          const result = await embedTexts(["a", "b"], {
            apiKey: "test-key",
            baseUrl: "https://example.test/v1",
            fetchImpl,
          });
          expect(result.costEstimated).toBe(true);
          expect(result.costUsd).toBeCloseTo(2000 * 1e-7, 12);
        });
      
        it("rejects malformed provider payloads", async () => {
          const fetchImpl = vi.fn(
            async () => new Response("not json", { status: 200 }),
          ) as unknown as typeof fetch;
          await expect(
            embedTexts(["a"], { apiKey: "test-key", baseUrl: "https://example.test/v1", fetchImpl }),
          ).rejects.toThrow(/non-JSON/i);
        });
      
        it("aggregates token and cost usage across batches", async () => {
          const { fetchImpl } = fakeEmbeddingProvider();
          const result = await embedTexts(["a", "b", "c"], {
            apiKey: "test-key",
            baseUrl: "https://example.test/v1",
            batchSize: 2,
            fetchImpl,
          });
          expect(result.promptTokens).toBe(3);
          expect(result.costUsd).toBeCloseTo(0.002, 6);
          expect(result.costEstimated).toBe(false);
        });
      });
```

````
# @lovable.dev/ai-gateway

Server-only SDK for the Lovable AI Gateway. Set `LOVABLE_API_KEY` in
Node or Deno; `LOVABLE_AI_GATEWAY_URL` overrides the gateway URL.

```ts
import { generateImageStream } from "@lovable.dev/ai-gateway";
const events = await generateImageStream({ model, prompt }, { signal });
return events.toResponse();
```

Each capability exports functions directly through `src/index.ts`. Calls resolve
their transport through the shared `request()` function. Pass `apiKey` in the
optional call options to override the environment credential for that call.

## Generate model types

Use `npx ai-gateway --help` for commands, `npx ai-gateway generate --help` for
generation options and defaults, and `npx ai-gateway --version` for the installed
SDK version.

After installing the SDK, run this from the app root with `LOVABLE_API_KEY` set:

```bash
npx ai-gateway generate
```

The command calls `listModels()` and writes `src/generated/ai-gateway/models.d.ts` and
`src/generated/ai-gateway/models.d.cts` for ESM and CommonJS consumers. It groups
workspace-available model IDs by `supported_surfaces`. Without `--model`, only
model–surface combinations with bundled payload types are included. The generated
module augmentation supplies `Model`, `ModelFor<Surface>`, and capability unions
such as `ImageModels`. Ensure the generated files are included by the app's
TypeScript configuration. Re-running replaces the catalog, including removing
models or surface memberships that are no longer returned.

Use `npx ai-gateway generate --output <directory>` to choose a different output
directory. Relative paths resolve from the app root where the command runs;
absolute paths are also accepted. Each directory receives `models.d.ts` and
`models.d.cts`.

Repeat `--model <slug>` to add models without bundled contracts. Available bundled models remain
included. The generator fetches each selected model's missing surface contracts
through `/v1/models/schema`, converts them to declarations, and augments the
capability request, response, and event maps in the same output files. Response
and event roots are converted when present; absent output contracts remain `unknown`. Selecting a bundled model adds
no schema fetch. Unavailable models, missing contracts, and conversion failures
leave the previous declarations unchanged. Re-running without the flags removes
previously generated extra models.

The gateway commits contracts for `openai/gpt-6-astra` and `openai/gpt-6-luna`,
covering Chat and Responses requests, buffered results, and SSE events. Astra has
bundled types. Luna exercises dynamic generation with
`npx ai-gateway generate --model openai/gpt-6-luna`.
The schema route must be deployed, or use `LOVABLE_AI_GATEWAY_URL` to point at a
local gateway implementing it.

Inference payloads require a model in both the generated surface catalog and the
capability's payload map. The SDK bundles request contracts for 47 models across
65 combinations without availability feature flags, including deprecated models.
Region and retention restrictions remain enforced through workspace discovery and
the gateway. Before generation, request model types are `never`. Discovery,
video polling, and downloads do not take a model payload.
Generation rejects invalid catalog data and authenticated discovery fallbacks
before changing the existing declarations.

## Capability functions

| Capability | Functions |
| --- | --- |
| models | listModels |
| chat | generateChatCompletion, streamChatCompletion |
| responses | generateResponse, streamResponse |
| messages | generateMessage, streamMessage |
| interactions | generateInteraction, streamInteraction |
| images | generateImage, generateImageStream, editImage, editImageStream |
| embeddings | generateEmbeddings |
| speech | generateSpeech, streamSpeech |
| transcriptions | transcribeAudio, transcribeAudioStream |
| music | generateMusic |
| videos | generateVideo, streamVideo, getVideo, downloadVideo |
| live | connectLive |

Image edits and transcriptions accept an object containing
`Blob`/`File` uploads. The SDK encodes JSON for other inference operations.
Methods select buffered or streaming response modes themselves.

`GatewayStream<Event>` exposes native JSON SSE events through async iteration
or forwards the original SSE bytes through `toResponse()`. Choose one consumer;
iteration and forwarding do not duplicate the stream. `GatewayBinary` exposes
`body`, `contentType`, `blob()`, and `toResponse()`, also with one consumer.
Both preserve the gateway run ID. Pass `CallOptions.runId` to group calls.

Video downloads follow the gateway's signed storage redirect without forwarding
gateway credentials. Job polling remains explicit through `getVideo()`.

## Payload contracts

Each capability keeps its model-to-fields interface in `src/<capability>/payloads.ts`.
Requests form a discriminated union: choosing `model` selects its payload fields.
Shared provider definitions live in `src/contracts/`. Public request types,
provider field types and interfaces such as `ChatFieldsByModel` are exported from
the package root.

```ts
import { generateResponse } from "@lovable.dev/ai-gateway";

await generateResponse({
  model: "openai/gpt-5.4",
  input: "Write a short greeting.",
});
```

This call requires that model to be in the generated Responses catalog. Responses
requests omit provider storage and previous-response references; pass prior items
inline. Stream functions own `stream` and speech `stream_format`. Upload fields
use `Blob` or `File`. Gemini image requests accept native `contents` or translated
`prompt`/`messages` alternatives. Veo uses `instances`/`parameters` or the legacy
flat shape; Omni uses Interactions-style `input`/`response_format`.

A discovered model without a bundled payload contract cannot be called until a
module augmentation supplies its capability fields. The payload maps are open
interfaces for that extension. Empty maps for Messages, Interactions, Music,
SystemOne and Live have no built-in models. Repeated `--model` flags fetch additional contracts from the gateway and generate
their payload map additions alongside catalog membership.

Provider schemas describe endpoint families; runtime validation still enforces
numeric limits and other model/provider constraints that TypeScript cannot express.
Every bundled request model has a response entry, and bundled streaming models
have event entries. Open interfaces such as `ChatResponseByModel` and
`ChatEventByModel` keep entries separate even when they share wire definitions.
Functions infer their result from `payload.model`: `ChatCompletion<Model>`,
`ResponsesResponse<Model>`, or `GatewayStream<EventFor<Surface, Model>>`.
`ResponseFor<Surface, Model>` exposes the corresponding buffered result type.
Dynamic generation augments these maps alongside request fields using the model's
response and event schema roots; it preserves existing static entries. Missing
output contracts return `unknown`. Binary calls retain `GatewayBinary`.

Response model IDs can be provider-native strings, and chat output includes gateway
reasoning/image extensions and finish reasons such as `no_content`. Wire event maps
````

```
//#region src/models/surfaces.d.ts
declare const surfaces: readonly ["chat-completions", "responses", "messages", "interactions", "image-generation", "image-edit", "embeddings", "speech", "transcription", "music", "videos", "systemone", "live"];
//#endregion
//#region src/types.d.ts
/** Server-side credentials and retry overrides for a single call. Retries occur only when the transport can safely replay the request. */
interface RequestOptions {
  /** Overrides `LOVABLE_API_KEY` for this call. Supply credentials only from server-side code. */
  apiKey?: string;
  /** Maximum safe retries after the initial send. Defaults to 2; 0 disables retries. Must be a nonnegative safe integer. */
  maxRetries?: number;
  /** Cumulative retry waiting in milliseconds, excluding request execution. Defaults to 10_000; a wait exceeding the remaining budget returns the last error. Must be an integer from 0 to 2_147_483_647. */
  retryWaitBudgetMs?: number;
}
/** Options shared by capability functions, inheriting credential and retry defaults from {@link RequestOptions}. */
interface CallOptions extends RequestOptions {
  /** Cancels network I/O and pending retry waits. Aborting does not confirm that remote generation stopped. */
  signal?: AbortSignal;
  /** Correlation ID sent in `X-Lovable-AIG-Run-ID`. It does not provide idempotency or prevent duplicate generation. */
  runId?: string;
  /** Opaque correlation fields for gateway logs, such as live_session_id and delegation_id. Keep content and credentials out of metadata. */
  metadata?: Record<string, string>;
}
interface GatewayStream<Event> extends AsyncIterable<Event> {
  readonly runId?: string;
  toResponse(init?: ResponseInit): Response;
}
interface GatewayBinary {
  readonly body: ReadableStream<Uint8Array>;
  readonly contentType: string;
  readonly runId?: string;
  blob(): Promise<Blob>;
  toResponse(init?: ResponseInit): Response;
}
type LiveOptions = Pick<CallOptions, "apiKey" | "signal" | "runId" | "metadata">;
interface LiveSession<Command = never, Event = unknown, Creation = unknown> extends AsyncIterable<Event> {
  readonly runId?: string;
  readonly created: Creation;
  /** Resolves when the socket and final-event drain have ended. Read events to observe remote failures and final usage. */
  readonly closed: Promise<void>;
  send(event: Command): Promise<void>;
  close(): Promise<void>;
}
type GatewayPayload = Record<string, unknown>;
type UploadPayload = Record<string, unknown>;
type Surface = (typeof surfaces)[number];
/** A discovered model and its available gateway surfaces. Catalog discovery does not bypass runtime access checks or supply payload contracts. */
interface ModelInfo {
  /** Gateway model slug used as `payload.model`, such as `openai/gpt-6-astra`; generated types determine which slugs can be used per surface. */
  id: string;
  /** Capabilities available for this model in the returned catalog. One model can support multiple surfaces. */
  supported_surfaces: Surface[];
  /** Additional gateway metadata, such as pricing or context limits; narrow these values before using them. */
  [field: string]: unknown;
}
/** Model discovery response from `listModels()`. Check `workspace_models` before treating the catalog as workspace-specific. */
interface ModelList {
  /** Wire discriminator identifying the gateway model-list response. */
  object: "list";
  /** Entries for the catalog described by `workspace_models`, filtered by the enforced region when `inference_region` is present. */
  data: ModelInfo[];
  /** `included` means workspace-aware discovery; `public_only` means a public catalog, and `public_only_invalid_key` means key validation failed. Omission does not confirm workspace eligibility. */
  workspace_models?: "included" | "public_only" | "public_only_invalid_key";
  /** Enforced workspace inference region, when configured. The returned catalog contains only models available in that region. */
  inference_region?: string;
}
//#endregion
//#region src/models/index.d.ts
/** Discover model IDs and supported surfaces for the current workspace. Check `workspace_models` for a public-only fallback before treating the result as workspace eligibility. */
declare function listModels(options?: CallOptions): Promise<ModelList>;
```

```json
{
  "path": "scripts/_scratch-inspect.ts",
  "instructions": "Temporary inspection script to check the live database state.",
  "content": "import postgres from \"postgres\";\n\nconst sql = postgres(process.env.SUPABASE_DB_URL!, { ssl: \"require\", max: 1 });\ntry {\n  const cols = await sql<{ table_name: string; column_name: string }[]>`\n    select table_name, column_name\n    from information_schema.columns\n    where table_schema = 'public'\n      and table_name in ('document_chunks', 'ingestion_jobs', 'content_items', 'chat_threads', 'chat_messages')\n    order by table_name, ordinal_position`;\n  console.log(cols.map((c) => `${c.table_name}.${c.column_name}`).join(\"\\n\"));\n\n  const [counts] = await sql`select\n      (select count(*) from public.content_items)::int items,\n      (select count(*) from public.document_chunks)::int chunks,\n      (select count(*) from public.ingestion_jobs)::int jobs`;\n  console.log(counts);\n\n  console.log(await sql`select status, count(*)::int n from public.ingestion_jobs group by status`);\n  console.log(await sql`select count(distinct content_id)::int indexed from public.document_chunks`);\n\n  console.log(\n    await sql`select policyname, cmd, roles::text, qual from pg_policies where schemaname = 'public' and tablename = 'content_items'`,\n  );\n\n  const fns = await sql<{ proname: string }[]>`\n    select p.proname from pg_proc p\n    join pg_namespace n on n.oid = p.pronamespace\n    where n.nspname = 'public' order by p.proname`;\n  console.log(fns.map((f) => f.proname).join(\", \"));\n\n  const trig = await sql`select tgname from pg_trigger where not tgisinternal order by tgname`;\n  console.log(trig.map((t) => t.tgname).join(\", \"));\n} catch (error) {\n  console.log(\"DB ERROR:\", error instanceof Error ? error.message : error);\n} finally {\n  await sql.end();\n}\n"
}
```

```
- path: src/lib/industries.functions.ts
  content: |
      import { createServerFn } from "@tanstack/react-start";
      import { z } from "zod";
      import { requireSupabaseAuth } from "@/integrations/supabase/auth-middleware";
      import { slugify } from "./industries";
      
      export const createIndustry = createServerFn({ method: "POST" })
        .middleware([requireSupabaseAuth])
        .inputValidator((d: unknown) =>
          z.object({ name: z.string().trim().min(2).max(50), tagline: z.string().trim().max(80).optional() }).parse(d),
        )
        .handler(async ({ data, context }) => {
          const slug = slugify(data.name);
          if (!slug) return { ok: false as const, error: "Please enter a valid name." };
      
          const { error } = await context.supabase.from("industries").insert({
            slug,
            name: data.name,
            tagline: data.tagline || null,
            created_by: context.userId,
          });
          if (error) {
            return { ok: false as const, error: error.code === "23505" ? "That industry already exists." : "You can't add industries." };
          }
      
          // Generate a themed cover image; the industry stays usable if this fails.
          try {
            const { supabaseAdmin } = await import("@/integrations/supabase/client.server");
            const { data: permitted, error: quotaError } = await supabaseAdmin.rpc("consume_request_quota", {
              _kind: "industry-image", _subject: context.userId, _network: "",
            });
            if (quotaError || !permitted) return { ok: true as const, slug, imageFailed: true };
            const { generateImage } = await import("@lovable.dev/ai-gateway");
            const res = await generateImage({
              model: "openai/gpt-image-2.5-sunburst",
              prompt: `Cinematic wide photograph representing the ${data.name} industry${data.tagline ? ` (${data.tagline})` : ""}, realistic modern scene, teal and amber color grading, premium enterprise feel, no text, no logos`,
              size: "1536x1024",
              quality: "medium",
              output_format: "jpeg",
            });
            const b64 = res.data?.[0]?.b64_json;
            if (b64) {
              const bytes = Uint8Array.from(atob(b64), (c) => c.charCodeAt(0));
              const up = await supabaseAdmin.storage.from("industry-images").upload(`${slug}.jpg`, bytes, { contentType: "image/jpeg", upsert: true });
              if (!up.error) {
                await supabaseAdmin.from("industries").update({ image_url: `/api/public/industry-image/${slug}?v=${Date.now()}` }).eq("slug", slug);
              } else console.error("upload failed", up.error.message);
            }
          } catch (e) {
            console.error("image generation failed", e);
            return { ok: true as const, slug, imageFailed: true };
          }
          return { ok: true as const, slug, imageFailed: false };
        });
      
- path: src/lib/document-format.ts
  content: |
      export type DocumentFormat = "pdf" | "docx" | "pptx" | "image" | "video" | "unsupported";
      export function documentFormat(name: string, mime = ""): DocumentFormat {
        const extension = name.split(".").pop()?.toLowerCase();
        if (extension === "pdf" || mime === "application/pdf") return "pdf";
        if (extension === "docx") return "docx";
        if (extension === "pptx") return "pptx";
        if (mime.startsWith("image/") || /^(png|jpe?g|gif|webp)$/.test(extension ?? "")) return "image";
        if (mime.startsWith("video/") || /^(mp4|webm|mov)$/.test(extension ?? "")) return "video";
        return "unsupported";
      }
- path: src/lib/rag/ingest.ts
  content: |
      /**
       * Ingestion building blocks shared by the worker and tests: chunk shaping,
       * content hashing and batched embedding. Runs in Node/Bun; the worker is the
       * only caller of the provider.
       */
      import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
      import { chunkSections, type ChunkableSection } from "./chunk";
      import {
        EMBEDDING_BATCH_SIZE,
        EMBEDDING_DIMENSIONS,
        EMBEDDING_MODEL,
        EMBEDDING_USD_PER_TOKEN,
      } from "./config";
      
      export type ChunkRow = {
        chunk_index: number;
        content: string;
        estimated_tokens: number;
        page: number | null;
        slide: number | null;
        heading: string | null;
        embedding_model: string;
      };
      
      export type EmbeddedChunk = ChunkRow & { embedding: number[] };
      
      /**
       * Turns extracted sections into database rows. PDF page numbers land in `page`,
       * slide numbers in `slide`; DOCX has neither and keeps only its heading.
       */
      export function buildChunkRows(
        format: string,
        sections: readonly ChunkableSection[],
        sourceFile: string,
      ): ChunkRow[] {
        return chunkSections(sections, { sourceFile }).map((chunk) => ({
          chunk_index: chunk.chunkIndex,
          content: chunk.content,
          estimated_tokens: Math.max(chunk.estimatedTokens, 1),
          page: format === "pdf" ? chunk.pageOrSlide : null,
          slide: format === "pptx" ? chunk.pageOrSlide : null,
          heading: chunk.heading,
          embedding_model: EMBEDDING_MODEL,
        }));
      }
      
      /** Stable fingerprint of the extracted text, used to skip unchanged documents. */
      export function hashChunkContents(contents: readonly string[]): string {
        return createHash("sha256").update(contents.join("\u0000")).digest("hex");
      }
      
      export type EmbedOptions = {
        apiKey: string;
        baseUrl: string;
        fetchImpl?: typeof fetch;
        model?: string;
        dimensions?: number;
        batchSize?: number;
      };
      
      export type EmbeddingResult = {
        embeddings: number[][];
        promptTokens: number | null;
        costUsd: number | null;
        /** True when cost was derived from the token count instead of provider usage. */
        costEstimated: boolean;
      };
      
      type EmbeddingResponse = {
        data?: Array<{ embedding?: number[]; index?: number }>;
        usage?: { prompt_tokens?: number; cost?: number };
        error?: { message?: string } | string;
      };
      
      /** Embeds texts in provider-sized batches, preserving input order. */
      export async function embedTexts(
        texts: readonly string[],
        options: EmbedOptions,
      ): Promise<EmbeddingResult> {
        const embeddings: number[][] = [];
        let promptTokens = 0;
        let costUsd = 0;
        let sawUsage = false;
        let sawCost = false;
      
        const batchSize = Math.max(1, Math.min(options.batchSize ?? EMBEDDING_BATCH_SIZE, 100));
        const dimensions = options.dimensions ?? EMBEDDING_DIMENSIONS;
        const model = options.model ?? EMBEDDING_MODEL;
        const doFetch = options.fetchImpl ?? fetch;
        const baseUrl = options.baseUrl.replace(/\/+$/, "");
      
        for (let start = 0; start < texts.length; start += batchSize) {
          const batch = texts.slice(start, start + batchSize);
          const response = await doFetch(`${baseUrl}/embeddings`, {
            method: "POST",
            headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", Authorization: `Bearer ${options.apiKey}` },
            body: JSON.stringify({ model, input: batch, dimensions }),
          });
      
          const raw = await response.text();
          if (!response.ok) {
            throw new Error(`Embedding provider failed (${response.status}): ${providerMessage(raw)}`);
          }
      
          let body: EmbeddingResponse;
          try {
            body = JSON.parse(raw) as EmbeddingResponse;
          } catch {
            throw new Error("Embedding provider returned a non-JSON response");
          }
      
          const data = body.data;
          if (!Array.isArray(data) || data.length !== batch.length) {
            throw new Error(
              `Embedding provider returned ${Array.isArray(data) ? data.length : 0} vectors for ${batch.length} inputs`,
            );
          }
      
          // Providers may reorder; index is authoritative when present.
          const ordered = [...data].sort((a, b) => (a.index ?? 0) - (b.index ?? 0));
          for (const entry of ordered) {
            const vector = entry.embedding;
            if (!Array.isArray(vector) || vector.length !== dimensions) {
              throw new Error(
                `Embedding dimension mismatch: expected ${dimensions}, received ${vector?.length ?? 0}`,
              );
            }
            embeddings.push(vector);
          }
      
          if (body.usage) {
            sawUsage = true;
            promptTokens += body.usage.prompt_tokens ?? 0;
            if (typeof body.usage.cost === "number") {
              sawCost = true;
              costUsd += body.usage.cost;
            }
          }
        }
      
        // Batched responses omit cost, so fall back to the observed per-token price.
        const costEstimated = sawUsage && !sawCost;
        if (costEstimated) costUsd = promptTokens * EMBEDDING_USD_PER_TOKEN;
      
        return {
          embeddings,
          promptTokens: sawUsage ? promptTokens : null,
          costUsd: sawUsage ? costUsd : null,
          costEstimated,
        };
      }
      
      /** Keeps provider text in logs useful without dumping a whole response body. */
      function providerMessage(raw: string): string {
        try {
          const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as EmbeddingResponse;
          const message = typeof parsed.error === "string" ? parsed.error : (parsed.error?.message ?? "");
          if (message) return message.slice(0, 300);
        } catch {
          // fall through to the raw body
        }
        return raw.slice(0, 300);
      }
```

```
      import { createFileRoute, Link, redirect, useRouter, type ErrorComponentProps } from "@tanstack/react-router";
      import { useSuspenseQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
      import { useState } from "react";
      import { z } from "zod";
      import { Search, HardDrive, Files, FilterX, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, ShieldCheck } from "lucide-react";
      import { adminAccessQuery, adminContentQuery, filterAdminFiles, storageSummary, type AdminFilters } from "@/lib/admin-content";
      import { industriesQuery } from "@/lib/industries";
      import { formatSize } from "@/lib/content";
      import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
      import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
      import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
      import { Select, SelectTrigger, SelectValue, SelectContent, SelectItem } from "@/components/ui/select";
      import { FilePreviewDialog } from "@/components/FilePreviewDialog";
      import { AdminDeleteFileDialog } from "@/components/AdminDeleteFileDialog";
      
      const date = z.string().regex(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/).optional().catch(undefined);
      export const Route = createFileRoute("/_authenticated/admin")({
        validateSearch: z.object({ q: z.string().optional(), industry: z.string().optional(), customer: z.string().optional(), from: date, to: date, sort: z.enum(["newest", "oldest", "largest", "smallest"]).optional(), page: z.number().int().min(1).catch(1).optional() }),
        beforeLoad: async ({ context }) => {
          const allowed = await context.queryClient.fetchQuery(adminAccessQuery);
          if (!allowed) throw redirect({ to: "/library" });
        },
        loader: async ({ context }) => {
          await Promise.all([context.queryClient.ensureQueryData(adminContentQuery), context.queryClient.ensureQueryData(industriesQuery)]);
        },
        head: () => ({ meta: [
          { title: "Admin dashboard — Solution Hub" },
          { name: "description", content: "Manage Solution Hub files by industry, customer and upload date, and review file storage usage." },
          { property: "og:title", content: "Admin dashboard — Solution Hub" },
          { property: "og:description", content: "Admin file management and storage usage for Solution Hub." },
          { property: "og:type", content: "website" }, { name: "twitter:card", content: "summary" },
        ] }),
        errorComponent: AdminError,
        notFoundComponent: () => <main className="mx-auto max-w-7xl p-6"><h1>Dashboard not found</h1><Link to="/library">Return to library</Link></main>,
        pendingComponent: () => <main className="mx-auto max-w-7xl p-6 text-muted-foreground">Loading dashboard…</main>,
        component: AdminDashboard,
      });
      
      function AdminError({ error }: ErrorComponentProps) {
        const router = useRouter();
        return <main className="mx-auto max-w-7xl p-6"><h1 className="text-xl font-semibold">Dashboard unavailable</h1><p role="alert" className="my-4 text-destructive">{error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Could not load the dashboard."}</p><Button onClick={() => router.invalidate()}>Try again</Button></main>;
      }
      
      function AdminDashboard() {
        const { data: items } = useSuspenseQuery(adminContentQuery);
        const { data: industries } = useSuspenseQuery(industriesQuery);
        const search = Route.useSearch();
        const navigate = Route.useNavigate();
        const [view, setView] = useState("files");
        const filtered = filterAdminFiles(items, search);
        const total = storageSummary(items), selected = storageSummary(filtered);
        const pageCount = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(filtered.length / 25));
        const page = Math.min(search.page ?? 1, pageCount);
        const visible = filtered.slice((page - 1) * 25, page * 25);
        const customers = [...new Set(items.filter(i => !search.industry || i.industry === search.industry).flatMap(i => i.customer_name ? [i.customer_name] : []))].sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b));
        const invalidRange = Boolean(search.from && search.to && search.from > search.to);
        function update(patch: Partial<AdminFilters>) { void navigate({ search: prev => ({ ...prev, ...patch, page: 1 }), replace: true }); }
        const size = (bytes: number) => formatSize(bytes) || "0 B";
        const industryName = (slug: string) => industries.find(i => i.slug === slug)?.name ?? slug;
        const groups = industries.map(i => ({ ...i, ...storageSummary(filtered.filter(f => f.industry === i.slug)) })).filter(i => i.files > 0).sort((a, b) => b.bytes - a.bytes);
      
        return <main className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4 border-b pb-6">
            <div><p className="mb-2 flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium text-primary"><ShieldCheck className="h-4 w-4" /> Administration</p><h1 className="text-3xl font-bold tracking-normal">Admin dashboard</h1></div>

              <div className="space-y-2"><Label>Customer</Label><Select value={search.customer ?? "newest"} onValueChange={v => { if (v === "newest" || v === "oldest" || v === "largest" || v === "smallest") update({ sort: v }); }}><SelectTrigger aria-label="Sort files" className="w-full"><SelectValue /></SelectTrigger><SelectContent><SelectItem value="newest">Newest first</SelectItem><SelectItem value="oldest">Oldest first</SelectItem><SelectItem value="largest">Largest first</SelectItem><SelectItem value="smallest">Smallest first</SelectItem></SelectContent></Select></div>

Sizes are recorded at upload.</p><div className="mt-4 grid gap-x-8 sm:grid-cols-2">{groups.map(i => <div key={i.slug} className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 border-b py-3"><span className="min-w-0 break-words text-sm">{i.name}<span className="block text-xs text-muted-foreground">{i.files} files{i.unknown ? ` · ${i.unknown} unknown sizes` : ""}</span></span><span className="shrink-0 font-semibold tabular-nums">{size(i.bytes)}</span></div>)}</div></section>}
          <section className="py-6"><div className="mb-4 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2"><h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">{view === "storage" ? "File storage usage" : "Uploaded files"}</h2><p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{filtered.length} files</p></div>
            {filtered.length === 0 ? <div className="border-y py-14 text-center"><Files className="mx-auto mb-3 h-8 w-8 text-muted-foreground" /><h3 className="font-semibold">{items.length ? "No matching files" : "No uploaded files yet"}</h3></div> : <>
              <div className="overflow-x-auto"><table className="w-full min-w-[760px] text-left text-sm"><thead className="border-y bg-muted text-muted-foreground"><tr>{["File", "Industry / customer", "Uploaded (UTC)", "Storage", "Actions"].map(h => <th key={h} scope="col" className="px-3 py-3 font-medium">{h}</th>)}</tr></thead><tbody>{visible.map(item => <tr key={item.id} className="border-b"><td className="max-w-72 px-3 py-4"><FilePreviewDialog item={item} trigger={<Button variant="link" className="h-auto max-w-full justify-start whitespace-normal p-0 text-left font-semibold">{item.title}</Button>} /><p className="mt-1 break-all text-xs text-muted-foreground">{item.file_name}</p>{item.solution && <p className="mt-1 break-words text-xs text-primary">{item.solution}</p>}</td><td className="max-w-56 px-3 py-4"><p className="break-words">{industryName(item.industry)}</p><p className="mt-1 break-words text-xs text-muted-foreground">{item.customer_name || "Industry-wide"}</p></td><td className="whitespace-nowrap px-3 py-4 tabular-nums">{item.created_at.slice(0, 10)}</td><td className="whitespace-nowrap px-3 py-4 font-semibold tabular-nums" title={item.file_size == null ? "Unknown" : size(item.file_size)}</td><td className="px-3 py-4"><div className="flex items-center gap-1"><FilePreviewDialog item={item} /><AdminDeleteFileDialog item={item} /></div></td></tr>)}</tbody></table></div>

- path: scripts/download-library.ts
  content: |
      /**
       * Downloads the real library files from the private "content" bucket so the
       * extraction spike can run locally. Requires either:
       *   - SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY (local admin download), or
       *   - LIBRARY_USER_EMAIL + LIBRARY_USER_PASSWORD for an allowed member account.
       * Usage: bun scripts/download-library.ts [outputDir]
       *
       * The service role is used only by this local script (and later the ingestion
       * Edge Function). Never expose it to the browser or commit it.
       */
      import { createClient, type SupabaseClient } from "@supabase/supabase-js";
      import { existsSync, mkdirSync, statSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
      import { dirname, join, resolve } from "node:path";
      import type { Database } from "../src/integrations/supabase/types";
      import { documentFormat } from "../src/lib/document-format";
      
      const BUCKET = "content";
      const SUPPORTED = new Set(["pdf", "docx", "pptx"]);
      
      type ManifestEntry = {
        localPath: string;
        id: string;
        title: string;
        fileName: string;
        filePath: string;
        fileSize: number | null;
        industry: string;
        customerName: string | null;
        mimeType: string | null;
      };
      
      async function main() {
        const url = process.env.SUPABASE_URL;
        const serviceKey = process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY;
        const publishableKey = process.env.SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY;
        const email = process.env.LIBRARY_USER_EMAIL;
        const password = process.env.LIBRARY_USER_PASSWORD;
      
        if (!url) {
          console.error("Missing SUPABASE_URL.");
          process.exitCode = 1;
          return;
        }
      
        const options = { auth: { persistSession: false, autoRefreshToken: false } };
        let supabase: SupabaseClient<Database>;
      
        if (serviceKey) {
          supabase = createClient<Database>(url, serviceKey, options);
          console.log("Using the service role for this local download.");
        } else if (publishableKey && email && password) {
          supabase = createClient<Database>(url, publishableKey, options);
          const { error: signInError } = await supabase.auth.signInWithPassword({ email, password });
          if (signInError) {
            console.error(`Could not sign in as ${email}: ${signInError.message}`);
            process.exitCode = 1;
            return;
          }
          console.log(`Signed in as ${email}; RLS applies.`);
        } else {
          console.error("Missing credentials.");
          console.error(
            "Set SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY, or LIBRARY_USER_EMAIL + LIBRARY_USER_PASSWORD for an allowed member account.",
          );
          console.error('The publishable key alone cannot read the private "content" bucket (RLS).');
          process.exitCode = 1;
          return;
        }
      
        const outputDir = resolve(process.argv[2] ?? "spike-input");
      
        const { data, error } = await supabase
          .from("content_items")
          .select("id,title,file_name,file_path,file_size,mime_type,industry,customer_name")
          .order("created_at", { ascending: true });
        if (error) {
          console.error(`Could not read content_items: ${error.message}`);
          process.exitCode = 1;
          return;
        }
      
        const items = data ?? [];
        if (items.length === 0) {
          console.log("No content_items are visible to this session; check the email domain and RLS.");
        }
        const supported = items.filter((item) =>
          SUPPORTED.has(documentFormat(item.file_name, item.mime_type ?? "")),
        );
      
        // Move/copy can point several rows at one stored object.
        const unique = new Map<string, (typeof supported)[number]>();
        for (const item of supported) {
          if (!unique.has(item.file_path)) unique.set(item.file_path, item);
        }
      
        const manifest: ManifestEntry[] = [];
        const failures: Array<{ filePath: string; error: string }> = [];
        let downloaded = 0;
        let skipped = 0;
      
        for (const item of unique.values()) {
          const safeName = item.file_name.replace(/[^\w.-]+/g, "_");
          const localPath = join(outputDir, `${item.id.slice(0, 8)}-${safeName}`);
          const existingSize = existsSync(localPath) ? statSync(localPath).size : -1;
          const alreadyComplete =
            existingSize >= 0 &&
            (item.file_size === null ? existingSize > 0 : existingSize === item.file_size);
      
          if (alreadyComplete) {
            skipped += 1;
            manifest.push({
              localPath,
              id: item.id,
              title: item.title,
              fileName: item.file_name,
              filePath: item.file_path,
              fileSize: item.file_size,
              industry: item.industry,
              customerName: item.customer_name,
              mimeType: item.mime_type,
            });
            continue;
          }
      
          const { data: blob, error: downloadError } = await supabase.storage
            .from(BUCKET)
            .download(item.file_path);
          if (downloadError || !blob) {
            failures.push({
              filePath: item.file_path,
              error: downloadError?.message ?? "empty download",
            });
            continue;
          }
      
          mkdirSync(dirname(localPath), { recursive: true });
          writeFileSync(localPath, new Uint8Array(await blob.arrayBuffer()));
          downloaded += 1;
          manifest.push({
            localPath,
            id: item.id,
            title: item.title,
            fileName: item.file_name,
            filePath: item.file_path,
            fileSize: item.file_size,
            industry: item.industry,
            customerName: item.customer_name,
            mimeType: item.mime_type,
          });
        }
      
        mkdirSync(outputDir, { recursive: true });
        writeFileSync(
          join(outputDir, "manifest.json"),
          `${JSON.stringify({ bucket: BUCKET, downloaded: manifest, failures }, null, 2)}\n`,
        );
      
        console.log(
          `Downloaded ${downloaded}, skipped ${skipped} already complete, ${manifest.length} of ${unique.size} supported files in ${outputDir}/`,
        );
        console.log(`Skipped ${items.length - supported.length} image/video/unsupported items.`);
        for (const failure of failures) {
          console.error(`FAILED ${failure.filePath}: ${failure.error}`);
        }
        if (failures.length > 0) process.exitCode = 1;
      }
      
      void main();
```

```json
{
  "path": "scripts/_scratch-inspect.ts",
  "instructions": "Temporary script to inspect live rows and library content for eval drafting.",
  "content": "import { readdirSync, readFileSync } from \"node:fs\";\nimport { join } from \"node:path\";\nimport postgres from \"postgres\";\n\nconst sql = postgres(process.env.SUPABASE_DB_URL!, { ssl: \"require\", max: 1 });\ntry {\n  const items = await sql`select id, title, file_name, industry, customer_name, content_type from public.content_items order by created_at`;\n  console.log(\"=== content_items ===\");\n  for (const i of items) console.log(JSON.stringify(i));\n  const jobs = await sql`select content_id, status, chunk_count, warnings from public.ingestion_jobs order by created_at`;\n  console.log(\"=== jobs ===\");\n  for (const j of jobs) console.log(JSON.stringify(j));\n  const sample = await sql`select content_id, chunk_index, page, slide, left(content, 120) as preview from public.document_chunks order by content_id, chunk_index limit 10`;\n  console.log(\"=== chunk sample ===\");\n  for (const c of sample) console.log(JSON.stringify(c));\n} finally {\n  await sql.end();\n}\n\nconst dir = \"spike-output\";\nconsole.log(\"=== file digest ===\");\nfor (const file of readdirSync(dir).filter((f) => f.endsWith(\".json\")).sort()) {\n  const parsed = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(dir, file), \"utf8\"));\n  const chunks = parsed.chunks ?? - path: src/lib/admin-content.ts
```
```
  content: |
      import { queryOptions } from "@tanstack/react-query";
      import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";
      import { matches, type ContentItem } from "./content";
      
      async function checkAdminAccess() {
          const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("is_content_admin");
          if (error) throw error;
          return data === true;
      }
      
      export const adminAccessQuery = queryOptions({
        queryKey: ["admin-access"],
        staleTime: 0,
        queryFn: checkAdminAccess,
      });
      
      export const adminContentQuery = queryOptions({
        queryKey: ["content", "admin"],
        queryFn: async () => {
          if (!(await checkAdminAccess())) throw new Error("Administrator access required.");
          const items: ContentItem[] = [];
          for (let start = 0; ; start += 1000) {
            const { data, error } = await supabase.from("content_items").select("*")
              .order("created_at", { ascending: false }).order("id").range(start, start + 999);
            if (error) throw error;
            items.push(...data);
            if (data.length < 1000) return items;
          }
        },
      });
      
      export type AdminFilters = { q?: string | undefined; industry?: string | undefined; customer?: string | undefined; from?: string | undefined; to?: string | undefined; sort?: "newest" | "oldest" | "largest" | "smallest" | undefined };
      
      export function filterAdminFiles(items: ContentItem[], filters: AdminFilters) {
        return items.filter((item) => {
          const day = item.created_at.slice(0, 10);
          return matches(item, filters.q ?? "")
            && (!filters.industry || item.industry === filters.industry)
            && (!filters.customer || (filters.customer === "__industry_wide__" ? !item.customer_name : item.customer_name === filters.customer))
            && (!filters.from || day >= filters.from)
            && (!filters.to || day <= filters.to);
        }).sort((a, b) => {
          if (filters.sort === "largest") return (b.file_size ?? -1) - (a.file_size ?? -1) || a.id.localeCompare(b.id);
          if (filters.sort === "smallest") return (a.file_size ?? Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER) - (b.file_size ?? Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER) || a.id.localeCompare(b.id);
          return filters.sort === "oldest" ? a.created_at.localeCompare(b.created_at) : b.created_at.localeCompare(a.created_at);
        });
      }
      
      export function storageSummary(items: ContentItem[]) {
        const unique = new Map<string, ContentItem>();
        for (const item of items) if (!unique.has(item.file_path)) unique.set(item.file_path, item);
        let bytes = 0, unknown = 0;
        for (const item of unique.values()) {
          if (item.file_size == null) unknown++;
          else bytes += item.file_size;
        }
        return { bytes, unknown, files: unique.size };
      }
- path: src/lib/activity.ts
  content: |
      import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";
      
      type Event =
        | { event_type: "file_open"; content_id: string; industry: string }
        | { event_type: "industry_view"; industry: string };
      
      // Fire-and-forget usage logging; never blocks or breaks the UI.
      export async function logActivity(e: Event) {
        try {
          const { data } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
          const user = data.session?.user;
          if (!user) return;
          await supabase.from("activity_events").insert({ ...e, user_id: user.id, user_email: user.email ?? null });
        } catch {
          /* ignore */
        }
      }
```

```json
{
  "path": "drizzle/migrations/0014_hybrid_search.sql",
  "instructions": "Add the hybrid search RPC used by retrieval.",
  "content": "-- Phase 3: hybrid retrieval over the passage index.\n--\n-- One security-invoker function so RLS on document_chunks and content_items\n-- applies to the caller's JWT. Vector similarity and full-text search each\n-- produce a top-N list; the lists are merged with reciprocal rank fusion (k=60)\n-- so an exact term match and a semantic match both survive. Industry, customer\n-- and file metadata are joined from content_items at query time, never stored\n-- on the chunk, so moves, copies and company renames cannot leave stale values.\nset search_path = public, extensions;\n\ncreate or replace function public.search_chunks(\n  query_embedding vector(1536),\n  query_text text default null,\n  filter_industry text default null,\n  filter_customer text default null,\n  match_count integer default 8\n)\nreturns table (\n  chunk_id uuid,\n  content_id uuid,\n  content text,\n  page integer,\n  slide integer,\n  heading text,\n  title text,\n  file_name text,\n  file_path text,\n  industry text,\n  customer_name text,\n  vector_rank integer,\n  text_rank integer,\n  score double precision\n)\nlanguage sql\nstable\nsecurity invoker\nset search_path = public, extensions\n-- HNSW filters after the index scan, so a narrow industry scope can starve a\n-- small candidate list. Iterative scan keeps walking until the filters are met.\nset hnsw.ef_search = 200\nset hnsw.iterative_scan = relaxed_order\nas $$\n  with scoped as (\n    select\n      c.id,\n      c.content_id,\n      c.content,\n      c.page,\n      c.slide,\n      c.heading,\n      c.tsv,\n      ci.title,\n      ci.file_name,\n      ci.file_path,\n      ci.industry,\n      ci.customer_name,\n      c.embedding <=> query_embedding as distance\n    from public.document_chunks c\n    join public.content_items ci on ci.id = c.content_id\n    where nullif(btrim(coalesce(filter_industry, '')), '') is null\n       or ci.industry = btrim(filter_industry)\n    having true\n  ),\n  scoped_filtered as (\n    select s.*\n    from scoped s\n    where (nullif(btrim(coalesce(filter_customer, '')), '') is null\n           or s.customer_name = btrim(filter_customer))\n  ),\n  vector_hits as (\n    select\n      v.id,\n      row_number() over (order by v.distance)::integer as rank\n    from scoped_filtered v\n    order by v.distance\n    limit 50\n  ),\n  query_ts as (\n    select websearch_to_tsquery('english', btrim(query_text)) as ts\n  ),\n  text_hits as (\n    select\n      t.id,\n      row_number() over (order by t.rank_score desc)::integer as rank\n    from (\n      select\n        s.id,\n        ts_rank(s.tsv, q.ts) as rank_score\n      from scoped_filtered s\n      cross join query_ts q\n      where q.ts is not null\n        and nullif(btrim(coalesce(query_text, '')), '') is not null\n        and s.tsv @@ q.ts\n      order by rank_score desc\n      limit 50\n    ) t\n  ),\n  merged as (\n    select\n      coalesce(v.id, t.id) as id,\n      v.rank as vector_rank,\n      t.rank as text_rank,\n      coalesce(1.0 / (60 + v.rank), 0.0) + coalesce(1.0 / (60 + t.rank), 0.0) as score\n    from vector_hits v\n    full outer join text_hits t on t.id = v.id\n  )\n  select\n    m.id as chunk_id,\n    s.content_id,\n    s.content,\n    s.page,\n    s.slide,\n    s.heading,\n    s.title,\n    s.file_name,\n    s.file_path,\n    s.industry,\n    s.customer_name,\n    m.vector_rank,\n    m.text_rank,\n    m.score\n  from merged m\n  join scoped_filtered s on s.id = m.id\n  order by\n    m.score desc,\n    coalesce(m.vector_rank, 2147483647),\n    coalesce(m.text_rank, 2147483647),\n    s.id\n  limit least(greatest(coalesce(match_count, 8), 1), 50);\n$$;\n\ncomment on function public.search_chunks(vector, text, text, text, integer) is\n  'Hybrid (pgvector + full-text, RRF k=60) passage search. Security invoker: RLS on document_chunks and content_items applies.';\n\nrevoke all on function public.search_chunks(vector, text, text, text, integer)\n  from public, anon;\ngrant execute on function public.search_chunks(vector, text, text, text, integer)\n  to authenticated;\n\nreset search_path;\n"
}
```
