  content: |
      import { lazy, Suspense, useEffect, useRef, useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
      import { Download, Eye, Loader2 } from "lucide-react";
      import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
      import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogHeader, DialogTitle, DialogTrigger } from "@/components/ui/dialog";
      import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";
      import { type ContentItem } from "@/lib/content";
      import { documentFormat } from "@/lib/document-format";
      import { logActivity } from "@/lib/activity";
      const PdfPreview = lazy(() => import("@/components/PdfPreview").then((module) => ({ default: module.PdfPreview })));
      
      export function FilePreviewDialog({ item, trigger }: { item: ContentItem; trigger?: ReactNode }) {
        const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
        return <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={(next) => {
          setOpen(next);
          if (next) void logActivity({ event_type: "file_open", content_id: item.id, industry: item.industry });
        }}>
            <DialogTrigger asChild>{trigger ?? <Button size="sm" variant="outline"><Eye /> Open</Button>}</DialogTrigger>
            <DialogContent className="flex h-[90dvh] w-[96vw] max-w-6xl flex-col gap-3 overflow-hidden p-4" aria-describedby={undefined}>
              <DialogHeader className="shrink-0 pr-8"><DialogTitle className="truncate">{item.title}</DialogTitle></DialogHeader>
              {open && <FilePreview item={item} />}
            </DialogContent>
          </Dialog>;
      }
      
      function FilePreview({ item }: { item: ContentItem }) {
        const frame = useRef<HTMLIFrameElement>(null);
        const [url, setUrl] = useState("");
        const [blob, setBlob] = useState<Blob | null>(null);
        const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
        const [error, setError] = useState("");
        const [frameReady, setFrameReady] = useState(false);
        const format = documentFormat(item.file_name, item.mime_type ?? "");
      
        useEffect(() => {
          let active = true;
          let objectUrl = "";
          void (async () => {
            try {
              const result = await supabase.storage.from("content").download(item.file_path);
              if (result.error) throw result.error;
              if (!active) return;
              const file = format === "pdf" ? new Blob([result.data], { type: "application/pdf" }) : result.data;
              objectUrl = URL.createObjectURL(file);
              setUrl(objectUrl); setBlob(file);
              if (format !== "docx" && format !== "pptx") setLoading(false);
            } catch { if (active) { setError("Could not open this file. Try again."); setLoading(false); } }
          })();
          return () => { active = false; if (objectUrl) URL.revokeObjectURL(objectUrl); };
        }, [item.file_path, format]);
      
        useEffect(() => {
          if (!blob || !frameReady || (format !== "docx" && format !== "pptx")) return;
          let active = true;
          void (async () => {
            try {
              const iframe = frame.current;
              const doc = iframe?.contentDocument;
              if (!iframe || !doc) throw new Error("Viewer unavailable");
              const host = doc.createElement("div");
              doc.body.replaceChildren(host);
              if (format === "docx") {
                const { renderAsync } = await import("docx-preview");
                if (!active) return;
                await renderAsync(blob, host, undefined, { breakPages: true, useBase64URL: true });
              } else {
                const { init } = await import("pptx-preview");
                if (!active) return;
                const width = Math.max(280, iframe.clientWidth - 20);
                const viewer = init(host, { width, height: width * 9 / 16 });
                await viewer.preview(await blob.arrayBuffer());
              }
              if (active) setLoading(false);
            } catch { if (active) { setError("This document could not be displayed. You can download the original."); setLoading(false); } }
          })();
          return () => { active = false; };
        }, [blob, format, frameReady]);
      
        return <>
          <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center justify-between gap-3">
            <p className="truncate text-sm text-muted-foreground">{item.file_name}</p>
            <Button size="sm" variant="outline" disabled={!url} asChild><a href={url || undefined} download={item.file_name}><Download /> Download</a></Button>
          </div>
          <div className="relative min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-auto rounded-md border bg-muted">
            {loading && <div className="absolute inset-0 z-10 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 bg-background text-muted-foreground"><Loader2 className="h-5 w-5 animate-spin" />Opening…</div>}
            {error ? <p role="alert" className="p-8 text-center text-destructive">{error}</p> : <>
              {format === "pdf" && blob && <Suspense fallback={<Loader2 aria-label="Loading PDF viewer" className="m-8 h-5 w-5 animate-spin" />}><PdfPreview blob={blob} /></Suspense>}
              {(format === "docx" || format === "pptx") && <iframe ref={frame} onLoad={() => setFrameReady(true)} title={item.file_name} sandbox="allow-same-origin" referrerPolicy="no-referrer" srcDoc={'<!doctype html><html><head><meta http-equiv="Content-Security-Policy" content="default-src \'none\'; img-src data: blob:; media-src data: blob:; font-src data: blob:; style-src \'unsafe-inline\'; connect-src \'none\'; form-action \'none\'; base-uri \'none\'"></head><body></body></html>'} className="h-full w-full border-0" />}
              {format === "image" && url && <img src={url} alt={item.title} className="h-full w-full object-contain" />}
              {format === "video" && url && <video src={url} controls className="h-full w-full" />}
              {format === "unsupported" && !loading && <p className="p-8 text-center text-muted-foreground">{ /\.(doc|ppt)$/i.test(item.file_name) ? "This older Office format cannot be displayed here. Upload a PDF, DOCX or PPTX version to view it inline." : "Preview is unavailable for this file type. Download the original to open it." }</p>}
            </>}
          </div>
        </>;
      }
- path: src/lib/content.ts
  content: |
      import { queryOptions } from "@tanstack/react-query";
      import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";
      import type { Tables } from "@/integrations/supabase/types";
      
      export type ContentItem = Tables<"content_items">;
      
      export const contentQuery = (industry?: string) =>
        queryOptions({
          queryKey: ["content", industry ?? "all"],
          staleTime: 30_000,
          queryFn: async () => {
            let q = supabase.from("content_items").select("*").order("created_at", { ascending: false });
            if (industry) q = q.eq("industry", industry);
            const { data, error } = await q;
            if (error) throw error;
            return data;
          },
        });
      
      export function matches(item: ContentItem, term: string) {
        const t = term.trim().toLowerCase();
        if (!t) return true;
        return [item.title, item.customer_name, item.solution, item.description, item.file_name]
          .some((v) => v?.toLowerCase().includes(t));
      }
      
      export function formatSize(bytes?: number | null) {
        if (!bytes) return "";
        const u = ["B", "KB", "MB", "GB"];
        let i = 0, n = bytes;
        while (n >= 1024 && i < u.length - 1) { n /= 1024; i++; }
        return `${n.toFixed(n < 10 && i > 0 ? 1 : 0)} ${u[i]}`;
      }
      
      export type DateSort = "newest" | "oldest";
      export function sortByDate<T extends { created_at: string }>(list: T[], sort: DateSort) {
        return [...list].sort((a, b) =>
          sort === "newest" ? b.created_at.localeCompare(a.created_at) : a.created_at.localeCompare(b.created_at),
        );
      }
      
- path: src/routes/_authenticated/industry.$slug.tsx
  content: |
      import { createFileRoute, Link, notFound } from "@tanstack/react-router";
      import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
      import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
      import { logActivity } from "@/lib/activity";
      import { Building2, Globe2, Search, House, ChevronRight } from "lucide-react";
      import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
      import { ContentCard } from "@/components/ContentCard";
      import { UploadDialog } from "@/components/UploadDialog";
      import { contentQuery, matches, sortByDate, type DateSort } from "@/lib/content";
      import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";
      import { AddCustomerDialog } from "@/components/AddCustomerDialog";
      import { DateSortToggle } from "@/components/DateSortToggle";
      import { CONTENT_TYPES, industriesQuery, type Industry } from "@/lib/industries";
      import { useRole } from "@/lib/roles";
      import { ManageCustomerDialog } from "@/components/ManageCustomerDialog";
      import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
      import { ProgressiveList } from "@/components/ProgressiveList";
      
      export const Route = createFileRoute("/_authenticated/industry/$slug")({
        loader: async ({ params, context }) => {
          const list = await context.queryClient.ensureQueryData(industriesQuery);
          const industry = list.find((i) => i.slug === params.slug);
          if (!industry) throw notFound();
          return { industry };
        },
        head: ({ loaderData }) => {
          const name = loaderData?.industry.name ?? "Industry";
          return {
            meta: [
              { title: `${name} — Solution Hub` },
              { name: "description", content: `${name} case studies, pitch decks, POC videos and proposals.` },
              { property: "og:title", content: `${name} — Solution Hub` },
              { property: "og:description", content: `${name} case studies, pitch decks, POC videos and proposals.` },
              { property: "og:type", content: "website" },
              { name: "twitter:card", content: "summary" },
            ],
          };
        },
        component: IndustryPage,
      });
      
      const ALL = "__all";
      const GENERAL = "__general";
      
      function IndustryPage() {
        const { industry } = Route.useLoaderData();
        return <IndustryContent key={industry.slug} industry={industry} />;
      }
      
      function IndustryContent({ industry }: { industry: Industry }) {
        const { isAdmin } = useRole();
        useEffect(() => { void logActivity({ event_type: "industry_view", industry: industry.slug }); }, [industry.slug]);
        const { data = [], isLoading } = useQuery(contentQuery(industry.slug));
        const [customer, setCustomer] = useState(ALL);
        const [type, setType] = useState(ALL);
        const [q, setQ] = useState("");
        const [sort, setSort] = useState<DateSort>("newest");
      
        const { data: saved = [] } = useQuery({
          queryKey: ["customers", industry.slug],
          staleTime: 30_000,
          queryFn: async () => {
            const { data, error } = await supabase.from("customers").select("name").eq("industry", industry.slug);
            if (error) throw error;
            return data.map((c) => c.name);
          },
        });
        const customers = [...new Set([...saved, ...(data.map((d) => d.customer_name).filter(Boolean) as string[])])].sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b));
        const filtered = sortByDate(data.filter(
          (d) =>
            (customer === ALL || (customer === GENERAL ? !d.customer_name : d.customer_name === customer)) &&
            (type === ALL || d.content_type === type) &&
            matches(d, q),
        ), sort);
      
        const chip = (active: boolean) =>
          `flex w-full items-center justify-between rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-left text-sm transition-colors ${active ? "bg-primary text-primary-foreground" : "hover:bg-secondary"}`;
      
        return (
          <main>
            <nav aria-label="Breadcrumb" className="mx-auto flex max-w-7xl items-center gap-2 px-6 py-3 text-sm">
              <Button asChild variant="ghost" size="sm"><Link to="/library"><House className="h-4 w-4" />Home</Link></Button>
              <ChevronRight aria-hidden="true" className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-muted-foreground" />
              <span aria-current="page" className="min-w-0 break-words text-muted-foreground">{industry.name}</span>
            </nav>
            <section className="relative h-72 overflow-hidden bg-ink">
              <img src={industry.image} alt={industry.name} className="absolute inset-0 h-full w-full object-cover" />
              <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-hero" />
              <div className="relative mx-auto flex h-full max-w-7xl flex-col justify-end px-6 pb-10">
                <p className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-accent">Industry</p>
                <h1 className="mt-2 text-4xl font-bold text-ink-foreground md:text-5xl">{industry.name}</h1>
                <p className="mt-2 text-ink-foreground/80">{industry.tagline}</p>
              </div>
            </section>
      
            <div className="mx-auto grid max-w-7xl gap-8 px-6 py-10 lg:grid-cols-[260px_1fr]">
              <aside className="space-y-6">
                <div>
                  <h2 className="mb-2 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-muted-foreground">Browse</h2>
                  <Button variant="ghost" className={`${chip(customer === ALL)} h-auto`} onClick={() => setCustomer(ALL)}>All content <span>{data.length}</span></Button>
                  <Button variant="ghost" className={`${chip(customer === GENERAL)} h-auto`} onClick={() => setCustomer(GENERAL)}>
                    <span className="flex items-center gap-2"><Globe2 className="h-4 w-4" /> Industry-wide</span>
                    <span>{data.filter((d) => !d.customer_name).length}</span>
                  </Button>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <h2 className="mb-2 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-muted-foreground">Customers</h2>
                  {customers.length === 0 && <p className="px-3 text-sm text-muted-foreground">No customers yet.</p>}
                  {customers.map((c) => (
                    <div key={c} className="flex items-center gap-1">
                      <Button variant="ghost" className={`${chip(customer === c)} h-auto min-w-0 flex-1 whitespace-normal`} onClick={() => setCustomer(c)}>
                        <span className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-2"><Building2 className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" /><span className="break-words">{c}</span></span>
                        <span className="shrink-0">{data.filter((d) => d.customer_name === c).length}</span>
                      </Button>
                      {isAdmin && <ManageCustomerDialog industry={industry.slug} name={c} onChanged={(name) => { if (customer === c) setCustomer(name ?? GENERAL); }} />}
                    </div>
                  ))}
                  <AddCustomerDialog industry={industry.slug} industryName={industry.name} onAdded={setCustomer} />
                </div>
              </aside>
      
              <section>
                <div className="mb-4 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
                  <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">{customer === ALL ? "All content" : customer === GENERAL ? "Industry-wide content" : customer}</h2>
                  <UploadDialog industry={industry.slug} customers={customers} defaultCustomer={customer !== ALL && customer !== GENERAL ? customer : ""} />
                </div>
                <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
                  <div className="relative flex-1">
                    <Search className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 h-4 w-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted-foreground" />
                    <Input value={q} onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)} placeholder={`Search ${industry.name} by customer or solution…`} className="pl-9" />
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                  <DateSortToggle value={sort} onChange={setSort} />
                  {[{ value: ALL, label: "All types" }, ...CONTENT_TYPES].map((t) => (
                    <button
                      key={t.value}
                      onClick={() => setType(t.value)}
                      className={`rounded-full border px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium ${type === t.value ? "border-primary bg-primary text-primary-foreground" : "bg-card hover:bg-secondary"}`}
                    >
                      {t.label}
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
      
                {isLoading ? (
                  <p className="mt-8 text-muted-foreground">Loading…</p>
                ) : filtered.length === 0 ? (
                  <div className="mt-8 rounded-2xl border border-dashed p-12 text-center text-muted-foreground">
                    Nothing here yet. Upload industry-wide or customer-specific content for {industry.name}.
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <ProgressiveList key={`${customer}:${type}:${q}:${sort}`} items={filtered} className="mt-6 grid gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3" renderItem={(i) => <ContentCard key={i.id} item={i} />} />
                )}
              </section>
            </div>
          </main>
        );
      }
      
```

> _Reasoning_

**🛠 Write File**

```json
{
  "path": "src/lib/rag/embeddings.ts",
  "instructions": "Move the embedding client into a runtime-agnostic module shared by ingestion and query retrieval.",
  "content": "/**\n * Embedding client shared by ingestion (Bun worker) and query retrieval\n * (Cloudflare Worker). No Node built-ins here on purpose: the Worker imports\n * this module while the ingestion worker imports it too.\n */\nimport {\n  EMBEDDING_BATCH_SIZE,\n  EMBEDDING_DIMENSIONS,\n  EMBEDDING_MODEL,\n  EMBEDDING_USD_PER_TOKEN,\n} from \"./config\";\n\nexport type EmbedOptions = {\n  apiKey: string;\n  baseUrl: string;\n  fetchImpl?: typeof fetch;\n  model?: string;\n  dimensions?: number;\n  batchSize?: number;\n};\n\nexport type EmbeddingResult = {\n  embeddings: number[][];\n  promptTokens: number | null;\n  costUsd: number | null;\n  /** True when cost was derived from the token count instead of provider usage. */\n  costEstimated: boolean;\n};\n\ntype EmbeddingResponse = {\n  data?: Array<{ embedding?: number[]; index?: number }>;\n  usage?: { prompt_tokens?: number; cost?: number };\n  error?: { message?: string } | string;\n};\n\n/** Embeds texts in provider-sized batches, preserving input order. */\nexport async function embedTexts(\n  texts: readonly string[],\n  options: EmbedOptions,\n): Promise<EmbeddingResult> {\n  const embeddings: number[][] = [];\n  let promptTokens = 0;\n  let costUsd = 0;\n  let sawUsage = false;\n  let sawCost = false;\n\n  const batchSize = Math.max(1, Math.min(options.batchSize ?? EMBEDDING_BATCH_SIZE, 100));\n  const dimensions = options.dimensions ?? EMBEDDING_DIMENSIONS;\n  const model = options.model ?? EMBEDDING_MODEL;\n  const doFetch = options.fetchImpl ?? fetch;\n  const baseUrl = options.baseUrl.replace(/\\/+$/, \"\");\n\n  for (let start = 0; start < texts.length; start += batchSize) {\n    const batch = texts.slice(start, start + batchSize);\n    const response = await doFetch(`${baseUrl}/embeddings`, {\n      method: \"POST\",\n      headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\", Authorization: `Bearer ${options.apiKey}` },\n      body: JSON.stringify({ model, input: batch, dimensions }),\n    });\n\n    const raw = await response.text();\n    if (!response.ok) {\n      throw new Error(`Embedding provider failed (${response.status}): ${providerMessage(raw)}`);\n    }\n\n    let body: EmbeddingResponse;\n    try {\n      body = JSON.parse(raw) as EmbeddingResponse;\n    } catch {\n      throw new Error(\"Embedding provider returned a non-JSON response\");\n    }\n\n    const data = body.data;\n    if (!Array.isArray(data) || data.length !== batch.length) {\n      throw new Error(\n        `Embedding provider returned ${Array.isArray(data) ? data.length : 0} vectors for ${batch.length} inputs`,\n      );\n    }\n\n    // Providers may reorder; index is authoritative when present.\n    const ordered = [...data].sort((a, b) => (a.index ?? 0) - (b.index ?? 0));\n    for (const entry of ordered) {\n      const vector = entry.embedding;\n      if (!Array.isArray(vector) || vector.length !== dimensions) {\n        throw new Error(\n          `Embedding dimension mismatch: expected ${dimensions}, received ${vector?.length ?? 0}`,\n        );\n      }\n      embeddings.push(vector);\n    }\n\n    if (body.usage) {\n      sawUsage = true;\n      promptTokens += body.usage.prompt_tokens ?? 0;\n      if (typeof body.usage.cost === \"number\") {\n        sawCost = true;\n        costUsd += body.usage.cost;\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Batched responses omit cost, so fall back to the observed per-token price.\n  const costEstimated = sawUsage && !sawCost;\n  if (costEstimated) costUsd = promptTokens * EMBEDDING_USD_PER_TOKEN;\n\n  return {\n    embeddings,\n    promptTokens: sawUsage ? promptTokens : null,\n    costUsd: sawUsage ? costUsd : null,\n    costEstimated,\n  };\n}\n\n/** Embeds one query string; retrieval always sends exactly one input. */\nexport async function embedQuery(\n  text: string,\n  options: Omit<EmbedOptions, \"batchSize\">,\n): Promise<number[]> {\n  const query = text.trim();\n  if (!query) throw new Error(\"Cannot embed an empty query\");\n  const { embeddings } = await embedTexts([query], options);\n  const vector = embeddings[0];\n  if (!vector) throw new Error(\"Embedding provider returned no vector for the query\");\n  return vector;\n}\n\n/** Keeps provider text in logs useful without dumping a whole response body. */\nfunction providerMessage(raw: string): string {\n  try {\n    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as EmbeddingResponse;\n    const message = typeof parsed.error === \"string\" ? parsed.error : (parsed.error?.message ?? \"\");\n    if (message) return message.slice(0, 300);\n  } catch {\n    // fall through to the raw body\n  }\n  return raw.slice(0, 300);\n}\n"
}
```

{
  "path": "src/lib/rag/ingest.ts",
  "instructions": "Delegate embedding to the shared module and keep the existing exports for the worker and tests.",
  "content": "/**\n * Ingestion building blocks shared by the worker and tests: chunk shaping,\n * content hashing and batched embedding. Runs in Node/Bun; the worker is the\n * only caller of the provider.\n */\nimport { createHash } from \"node:crypto\";\nimport { chunkSections, type ChunkableSection } from \"./chunk\";\nimport { EMBEDDING_MODEL } from \"./config\";\n\nexport { embedTexts } from \"./embeddings\";\nexport type { EmbedOptions, EmbeddingResult } from \"./embeddings\";\n\nexport type ChunkRow = {\n  chunk_index: number;\n  content: string;\n  estimated_tokens: number;\n  page: number | null;\n  slide: number | null;\n  heading: string | null;\n  embedding_model: string;\n};\n\nexport type EmbeddedChunk = ChunkRow & { embedding: number[] };\n\n/**\n * Turns extracted sections into database rows. PDF page numbers land in `page`,\n * slide numbers in `slide`; DOCX has neither and keeps only its heading.\n */\nexport function buildChunkRows(\n  format: string,\n  sections: readonly ChunkableSection[],\n  sourceFile: string,\n): ChunkRow[] {\n  return chunkSections(sections, { sourceFile }).map((chunk) => ({\n    chunk_index: chunk.chunkIndex,\n    content: chunk.content,\n    estimated_tokens: Math.max(chunk.estimatedTokens, 1),\n    page: format === \"pdf\" ? chunk.pageOrSlide : null,\n    slide: format === \"pptx\" ? chunk.pageOrSlide : null,\n    heading: chunk.heading,\n    embedding_model: EMBEDDING_MODEL,\n  }));\n}\n\n/** Stable fingerprint of the extracted text, used to skip unchanged documents. */\nexport function hashChunkContents(contents: readonly string[]): string {\n  return createHash(\"sha256\").update(contents.join(\"\\u0000\")).digest(\"hex\");\n}\n"
}

{
  "path": "src/lib/rag/search.ts",
  "instructions": "Add the reusable retrieval seam and its RPC glue.",
  "content": "/**\n * Retrieval seam. Everything that needs library passages (chat now, deck\n * generation later) goes through retrieve(query, scope, k, deps), so ranking,\n * filtering and citation metadata stay in one place.\n *\n * The database function is security-invoker: the caller's JWT and RLS decide\n * which chunks exist at all. This module never falls back to the service role.\n */\nimport type { SupabaseClient } from \"@supabase/supabase-js\";\nimport type { Database } from \"../../integrations/supabase/types\";\nimport { DEFAULT_MATCH_COUNT, EMBEDDING_DIMENSIONS, MAX_MATCH_COUNT } from \"./config\";\n\nexport type RetrievalScope = {\n  industry?: string | null | undefined;\n  customer?: string | null | undefined;\n};\n\nexport type RetrievedChunk = {\n  chunkId: string;\n  contentId: string;\n  content: string;\n  page: number | null;\n  slide: number | null;\n  heading: string | null;\n  title: string;\n  fileName: string;\n  filePath: string;\n  industry: string;\n  customerName: string | null;\n  vectorRank: number | null;\n  textRank: number | null;\n  score: number;\n};\n\nexport type SearchInput = {\n  embedding: readonly number[];\n  queryText: string;\n  scope: RetrievalScope;\n  matchCount: number;\n};\n\nexport type RetrieveDeps = {\n  embedQuery: (query: string) => Promise<number[]>;\n  search: (input: SearchInput) => Promise<RetrievedChunk[]>;\n};\n\ntype SearchRow = Database[\"public\"][\"Functions\"][\"search_chunks\"][\"Returns\"][number];\n\n/** Blank scope values mean \"no filter\", never an empty-looking customer. */\nexport function normalizeScope(scope: RetrievalScope = {}): {\n  industry: string | null;\n  customer: string | null;\n} {\n  const clean = (value?: string | null) => {\n    const trimmed = (value ?? \"\").trim();\n    return trimmed.length > 0 ? trimmed : null;\n  };\n  return { industry: clean(scope.industry), customer: clean(scope.customer) };\n}\n\nexport function clampMatchCount(count?: number): number {\n  if (count === undefined || !Number.isFinite(count)) return DEFAULT_MATCH_COUNT;\n  return Math.min(Math.max(Math.trunc(count), 1), MAX_MATCH_COUNT);\n}\n\n/** PostgREST passes a `vector` argument as its text form: [0.1,0.2,...]. */\nexport function formatVectorLiteral(vector: readonly number[]): string {\n  if (vector.length !== EMBEDDING_DIMENSIONS) {\n    throw new Error(\n      `Query embedding has ${vector.length} dimensions, expected ${EMBEDDING_DIMENSIONS}`,\n    );\n  }\n  for (const value of vector) {\n    if (!Number.isFinite(value)) throw new Error(\"Query embedding contains a non-finite value\");\n  }\n  return `[${vector.join(\",\")}]`;\n}\n\nexport function mapSearchRows(rows: readonly SearchRow[]): RetrievedChunk[] {\n  return rows.map((row) => ({\n    chunkId: row.chunk_id,\n    contentId: row.content_id,\n    content: row.content,\n    page: row.page,\n    slide: row.slide,\n    heading: row.heading,\n    title: row.title,\n    fileName: row.file_name,\n    filePath: row.file_path,\n    industry: row.industry,\n    customerName: row.customer_name,\n    vectorRank: row.vector_rank,\n    textRank: row.text_rank,\n    score: row.score,\n  }));\n}\n\n/**\n * Single retrieval entry point. Returns ranked passages from files the caller is\n * allowed to read; an empty list means nothing matched (an empty query is not an\n * error, it just has no results).\n */\nexport async function retrieve(\n  query: string,\n  scope: RetrievalScope = {},\n  k: number = DEFAULT_MATCH_COUNT,\n  deps: RetrieveDeps,\n): Promise<RetrievedChunk[]> {\n  const queryText = query.trim();\n  if (!queryText) return [];\n\n  const matchCount = clampMatchCount(k);\n  const embedding = await deps.embedQuery(queryText);\n  return deps.search({ embedding, queryText, scope: normalizeScope(scope), matchCount });\n}\n\n/** Wires retrieve() to an authenticated Supabase client and the gateway embedder. */\nexport async function searchLibraryChunks(\n  client: SupabaseClient<Database>,\n  query: string,\n  scope: RetrievalScope,\n  k: number,\n  embed: (query: string) => Promise<number[]>,\n): Promise<RetrievedChunk[]> {\n  return retrieve(query, scope, k, {\n    embedQuery: embed,\n    search: async ({ embedding, queryText, scope: scoped, matchCount }) => {\n      const { data, error } = await client.rpc(\"search_chunks\", {\n        query_embedding: formatVectorLiteral(embedding),\n        query_text: queryText,\n        filter_industry: scoped.industry,\n        filter_customer: scoped.customer,\n        match_count: matchCount,\n      });\n      if (error) throw new Error(`Library search failed: ${error.message}`);\n      return mapSearchRows((data ?? []) as SearchRow[]);\n    },\n  });\n}\n"
}
```

> Now the server function. `src/lib/rag/search.functions.ts`:
> 
> ```ts
> import { createServerFn } from "@tanstack/react-start";
> import { z } from "zod";
> import { requireSupabaseAuth } from "@/integrations/supabase/auth-middleware";
> import { searchLibraryChunks, type RetrievedChunk, type RetrievalScope } from "./search";
> import { embedQuery } from "./embeddings";
> 
> const input = z.object({
>   query: z.string().trim().min(2).max(500),
>   industry: z.string().trim().max(64).optional(),
>   customer: z.string().trim().max(120).optional(),
>   matchCount: z.number().int().min(1).max(50).optional(),
> });
> 
> export type SearchLibraryResult = { ok: true; chunks: RetrievedChunk[] } | { ok: false; error: string };
> 
> export const searchLibrary = createServerFn({ method: "POST" })
>   .middleware([requireSupabaseAuth])
>   .inputValidator((data: unknown) => input.parse(data))
>   .handler(async ({ data, context }): Promise<SearchLibraryResult> => {
>     const embed = createQueryEmbedder();
>     if (!embed.ok) return embed;
>     try {
>       const chunks = await searchLibraryChunks(context.supabase, data.query, {industry: data.industry, customer: data.customer}, data.matchCount ?? DEFAULT_MATCH_COUNT, embed.embed);
>       return { ok: true, chunks };
>     } catch (error) { console.error(...); return { ok:false, error: "Search is unavailable right now." } }
>   });
> ```
> 
> Where createQueryEmbedder reads env:
> ```ts
> function queryEmbedder(): { ok: true; embed: (q: string)=>Promise<number[]> } | { ok:false; error: string } {
>   const apiKey = process.env["TOKENFACTORY_API_KEY"];
>   const baseUrl = process.env["TOKENFACTORY_API_BASE"];
>   if (!apiKey || !baseUrl) return { ok: false, error: "Search is not configured yet (embedding provider credentials missing)." };
>   return { ok: true, embed: (q) => embedQuery(q, { apiKey, baseUrl }) };
> }
> ```
> 
> Hmm, should this live in search.functions.ts? Yes, server-only env usage there.
> 
> Note: `createServerFn().handler` return value must be serializable — chunks are plain. Fine.
> 
> Should members be able to call searchLibrary? Yes; scoped by RLS. The client-side UI (debug page) is admin-gated.
>

In TanStack Start, server functions are imported and called directly; the auth middleware requires the Authorization header — normally the client attaches it via a global fetch interceptor? // This file is automatically generated. Do not edit it directly.
import { createMiddleware } from '@tanstack/react-start'
import { supabase } from './client'

// Must be registered as a global `functionMiddleware` in `src/start.ts`; otherwise
// the browser never attaches the bearer token to serverFn RPCs.
export const attachSupabaseAuth = createMiddleware({ type: 'function' }).client(
  async ({ next }) => {
    const { data } = await supabase.auth.getSession()
    const token = data.session?.access_token
    return next({
      headers: token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {},
    })
  },
)

import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import {
  Outlet,
  Link,
  createRootRouteWithContext,
  useRouter,
  HeadContent,
  Scripts,
  type ErrorComponentProps,
} from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { useEffect, type ReactNode } from "react";

import appCss from "../styles.css?url";
import { reportLovableError } from "../lib/lovable-error-reporting";
import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";
import { Toaster } from "@/components/ui/sonner";

function NotFoundComponent() {
  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center bg-background px-4">
      <div className="max-w-md text-center">
        <h1 className="text-7xl font-bold text-foreground">404</h1>
        <h2 className="mt-4 text-xl font-semibold text-foreground">Page not found</h2>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
          The page you're looking for doesn't exist or has been moved.
        </p>
        <div className="mt-6">
          <Link
            to="/"
            className="inline-flex items-center justify-center rounded-md bg-primary px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-primary-foreground transition-colors hover:bg-primary/90"
          >
            Go home
          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function ErrorComponent({ error, reset }: ErrorComponentProps) {
  console.error(error);
  const router = useRouter();
  useEffect(() => {
    reportLovableError(error, { boundary: "tanstack_root_error_component" });
  }, [error]);

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center bg-background px-4">
      <div className="max-w-md text-center">
        <h1 className="text-xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-foreground">This page didn't load</h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
          Something went wrong on our end. You can try refreshing or head back home.
        </p>
        <div className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap justify-center gap-2">
          <button
            onClick={() => {
              router.invalidate();

{
  "path": "src/lib/rag/search.functions.ts",
  "instructions": "Add the authenticated server function that embeds the query and searches with the caller's client.",
  "content": "/**\n * Authenticated library search. Retrieval runs with the caller's JWT through\n * requireSupabaseAuth, so RLS decides which files are visible; the service role\n * is never used here. Only the query embedding leaves the server.\n */\nimport { createServerFn } from \"@tanstack/react-start\";\nimport { z } from \"zod\";\nimport { requireSupabaseAuth } from \"@/integrations/supabase/auth-middleware\";\nimport { DEFAULT_MATCH_COUNT } from \"./config\";\nimport { embedQuery } from \"./embeddings\";\nimport { searchLibraryChunks, type RetrievedChunk } from \"./search\";\n\nexport type SearchLibraryResult =\n  | { ok: true; chunks: RetrievedChunk[] }\n  | { ok: false; error: string };\n\nfunction createQueryEmbedder():\n  | { ok: true; embed: (query: string) => Promise<number[]> }\n  | { ok: false; error: string } {\n  const apiKey = process.env[\"TOKENFACTORY_API_KEY\"];\n  const baseUrl = process.env[\"TOKENFACTORY_API_BASE\"];\n  if (!apiKey || !baseUrl) {\n    console.error(\"[RAG] Embedding provider credentials are not configured\");\n    return { ok: false, error: \"Library search is not configured yet.\" };\n  }\n  return { ok: true, embed: (query: string) => embedQuery(query, { apiKey, baseUrl }) };\n}\n\nexport const searchLibrary = createServerFn({ method: \"POST\" })\n  .middleware([requireSupabaseAuth])\n  .inputValidator((data: unknown) =>\n    z\n      .object({\n        query: z.string().trim().min(2).max(500),\n        industry: z.string().trim().max(64).optional(),\n        customer: z.string().trim().max(120).optional(),\n        matchCount: z.number().int().min(1).max(50).optional(),\n      })\n      .parse(data),\n  )\n  .handler(async ({ data, context }): Promise<SearchLibraryResult> => {\n    const embedder = createQueryEmbedder();\n    if (!embedder.ok) return { ok: false, error: embedder.error };\n\n    try {\n      const chunks = await searchLibraryChunks(\n        context.supabase,\n        data.query,\n        { industry: data.industry, customer: data.customer },\n        data.matchCount ?? DEFAULT_MATCH_COUNT,\n        embedder.embed,\n      );\n      return { ok: true, chunks };\n    } catch (error) {\n      // Provider or database detail stays in the server log, not the response.\n      console.error(\"[RAG] search failed\", error instanceof Error ? error.message : error);\n      return { ok: false, error: \"Library search failed. Please try again.\" };\n    }\n  });\n"
}
```

> Tests (src/lib/rag/search.test.ts):

> - clampMatchCount: default 8, clamps 0 → 1, 999 → 50, non-finite → default, truncates
> - formatVectorLiteral: dimension mismatch throws, non-finite throws, builds literal
> - mapSearchRows: maps snake to camel incl nulls
> - retrieve: empty query returns [] without embedding; trims, calls embed once with trimmed query; passes normalized scope and clamped count; returns search results; propagates errors.
> - searchLibraryChunks: fake client rpc recording args; ensures string vector and nulls; throws on error; (use a minimal fake: { rpc: vi.fn(async () => ({ data: rows, error: null })) } cast).

type RetrievedChunk,\n} from \"./search\";\n\nconst vector = new Array<number>(1536).fill(0.5);\n\nconst row = {\n  chunk_id: \"chunk-1\",\n  content_id: \"content-1\",\n  content: \"Overlap content\",\n  page: 3,\n  slide: null,\n  heading: \"Pricing\",\n  title: \"Proposal\",\n  file_name: \"proposal.pdf\",\n  file_path: \"airlines/proposal.pdf\",\n  industry: \"airlines\",\n  customer_name: null,\n  vector_rank: 1,\n  text_rank: 4,\n  score: 0.0325,\n};\n\nconst hit: RetrievedChunk = {\n  chunkId: \"chunk-1\",\n  contentId: \"content-1\",\n  content: \"Overlap content\",\n  page: 3,\n  slide: null,\n  heading: \"Pricing\",\n  title: \"Proposal\",\n  fileName: \"proposal.pdf\",\n  filePath: \"airlines/proposal.pdf\",\n  industry: \"airlines\",\n  customerName: null,\n  vectorRank: 1,\n  textRank: 4,\n  score: 0.0325,\n};\n\ndescribe(\"normalizeScope\", () => {\n  it(\"defaults to no filters\", () => {\n    expect(normalizeScope()).toEqual({ industry: null, customer: null });\n  });\n\n  it(\"treats blank and whitespace values as no filter\", () => {\n    expect(normalizeScope({ industry: \"  \", customer: \"\" })).toEqual({\n      industry: null,\n      customer: null,\n    });\n  });\n\n  it(\"trims the values it keeps\", () => {\n    expect(normalizeScope({ industry: \" airlines \", customer: \"IndiGo\" })).toEqual({\n      industry: \"airlines\",\n      customer: \"IndiGo\",\n    });\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe(\"clampMatchCount\", () => {\n  it(\"defaults to eight results\", () => {\n    expect(clampMatchCount()).toBe(8);\n    expect(clampMatchCount(Number.NaN)).toBe(8);\n  });\n\n  it(\"keeps requests inside the database limit\", () => {\n    expect(clampMatchCount(0)).toBe(1);\n    expect(clampMatchCount(999)).toBe(50);\n    expect(clampMatchCount(12)).toBe(12);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe(\"formatVectorLiteral\", () => {\n  it(\"renders the text form PostgREST sends for a vector argument\", () => {\n    const literal = dimension\", () => {\n    expect(() => formatVectorLiteral([0.1, 0.2])).toThrow(/1536 dimensions/);\n  });\n\n  it(\"rejects non-numeric values\", () => {\n    const broken = [...vector];\n    broken[7] = Number.NaN;\n    expect(() => formatVectorLiteral(broken)).toThrow(/non-finite/);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe(\"mapSearchRows\", () => {\n  it(\"maps database columns and keeps null page or slide\", () => {\n    expect(mapSearchRows([row])).toEqual([hit]);\n    expect(mapSearchRows([{ ...row, page: null, slide: 9 }])[0]).toMatchObject({\n      page: null,\n      slide: 9,\n    });\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe(\"retrieve\", () => {\n  it(\"returns nothing for a blank query without calling the provider\", async () => {\n    const embedQuery = vi.fn();\n    const search = vi.fn();\n    await expect(retrieve(\"   \", {}, 8, { embedQuery, search })).resolves.toEqual([]);\n    expect(embedQuery).not.toHaveBeenCalled();\n    expect(search).not.toHaveBeenCalled();\n  });\n\n  it(\"embeds the trimmed query once and asks for the clamped count\", async () => {\n    const embedQuery = vi.fn(async () => vector);\n    const search = vi.fn(async () => [hit]);\n\n    const result = await retrieve(\"  churn dashboard  \", {}, 500, { embedQuery, search });\n\n    expect(result).toEqual([hit]);\n    expect(embedQuery).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);\n    expect(embedQuery).toHaveBeenCalledWith(\"churn dashboard\");\n    expect(search).toHaveBeenCalledWith({\n      embedding: vector,\n      queryText: \"churn dashboard\",\n      scope: { industry: null, customer: null },\n      matchCount: 50,\n    });\n  });\n\n  it(\"passes a normalized scope to the search stage\", async () => {\n    const search = vi.fn(async () => []);\n    await retrieve(\n      \"pricing\",\n      { industry: \" airlines \", customer: \"\" },\n      5,\n      { embedQuery: async () => vector, search },\n    );\n    expect(search.mock.calls[0]?.[0]).toMatchObject({\n      scope: { industry: \"airlines\", customer: null },\n      matchCount: 5,\n    });\n  });\n\n  it(\"propagates provider failures instead of returning empty results\", async () => {\n    await expect(\n      retrieve(\"pricing\", {}, 8, {\n        embedQuery: async () => {\n          throw new Error(\"Embedding provider failed (429)\");\n        },\n        search: async () => [],\n      }),\n    ).rejects.toThrow(/429/);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe(\"searchLibraryChunks\", () => {\n  it(\"sends the caller's filters and a vector literal to search_chunks\", async () => {\n    const rpc = vi.fn(async () => ({ data: [row], error: null }));\n    const client = { rpc } as unknown as Parameters<typeof searchLibraryChunks>[0];\n\n    const result = await searchLibraryChunks(\n      client,\n      \"churn\",\n      { industry: \"airlines\", customer: \"IndiGo\" },\n      6,\n      async () => vector,\n    );\n\n === assign_solution_hub_role ===
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.assign_solution_hub_role()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$

=== enforce_allowed_domain ===
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.enforce_allowed_domain()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
BEGIN
  IF NOT public.is_allowed_email(NEW.email) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'Email domain not allowed';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END; $function$


CREATE TRIGGER enforce_allowed_domain_trg BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE OF email ON auth.users FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION enforce_allowed_domain()

CREATE TRIGGER solution_hub_assign_role_created AFTER INSERT ON auth.users FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION assign_solution_hub_role()

CREATE TRIGGER solution_hub_assign_role_verified AFTER UPDATE OF email_confirmed_at ON auth.users FOR EACH ROW WHEN (((old.email_confirmed_at IS NULL) AND (new.email_confirmed_at IS NOT NULL))) EXECUTE FUNCTION assign_solution_hub_role()

- path: src/lib/industries.ts
  content: |
      import { queryOptions, useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
      import airlines from "@/assets/airlines.webp";
      import airports from "@/assets/airports.webp";
      import bfsi from "@/assets/bfsi.webp";
      import hospitality from "@/assets/hospitality.webp";
      import logistics from "@/assets/logistics.webp";
      import manufacturing from "@/assets/manufacturing.webp";
      import retail from "@/assets/retail.webp";
      import telecom from "@/assets/telecom.webp";
      import healthcare from "@/assets/healthcare-life-sciences.webp";
      import energy from "@/assets/energy.webp";
      import automotive from "@/assets/automotive.webp";
      import hero from "@/assets/hero.webp";
      import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";
      
      export type Industry = { slug: string; name: string; tagline: string; image: string };
      
      const BUILTIN_IMAGES: Record<string, string> = {
        airlines, airports, bfsi, hospitality, logistics, manufacturing, retail, telecom,
        "healthcare-and-life-sciences": healthcare,
        energy,
        automotive,
      };
      
      export const industriesQuery = queryOptions({
        queryKey: ["industries"],
        staleTime: 5 * 60_000,
        queryFn: async (): Promise<Industry[]> => {
          const { data, error } = await supabase.from("industries").select("slug,name,tagline,image_url");
          if (error) throw error;
          return data
            .map((r) => ({
              slug: r.slug,
              name: r.name,
              tagline: r.tagline ?? "",
              image: BUILTIN_IMAGES[r.slug] ?? r.image_url ?? hero,
            }))
            .sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
        },
      });
      
      export function useIndustries() {
        const q = useQuery(industriesQuery);
        return { industries: q.data ?? [], isLoading: q.isLoading };
      }
      
      export function slugify(name: string) {
        return name.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/&/g, "and").replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-").replace(/^-|-$/g, "").slice(0, 60);
      }
      
      export const CONTENT_TYPES = [
        { value: "case_study", label: "Case study" },
        { value: "pitch_deck", label: "Pitch deck" },
        { value: "poc_video", label: "POC video" },
        { value: "proposal", label: "Proposal" },
        { value: "other", label: "Other" },
      ] as const;
      
      export const contentTypeLabel = (v: string) =>
        CONTENT_TYPES.find((c) => c.value === v)?.label ?? "Other";
      
      export const ACCEPTED_FILES =
        ".ppt,.pptx,.doc,.docx,.xls,.xlsx,.pdf,.csv,image/*,video/*";

> Then run-eval.ts.
> 
> Design decisions for run-eval:
> - Uses postgres directly, simulating an authenticated member. Need a subject: `--email` or first allowed user from auth.users; get id from auth.users.
> - For each case, within one transaction: `set_config('request.jwt.claims', json, true)`; `set local role authenticated`; run `select * from public.search_chunks(${literal}::vector, ${text}, ${industry}, ${customer}, ${k})`; `reset role`.
>   Note: `set local role` then calling RPC via sql works. But postgres.js prepared statements: the `::vector` cast with parameter — need `sql.unsafe`? Can do:
>   ```ts
>   await tx`select * from public.search_chunks(${literal}::vector(1536), ${queryText}, ${industry}, ${customer}, ${k})`
>   ```
>   postgres.js will parameterize literal as text and cast. Should work.
> - Batching embeddings for all questions at once (1 call) then per-case search.
> - Report:
>   - per-case: first rank, files retrieved (unique, ordered), hit/miss.
>   - summary: n, recall@k, MRR, unanswerable count with results.
>   - per category breakdown.
>   - threshold: default 0.8, exit 1 if below (only over answerable cases).
> - Also `--limit`, `--verbose`, `--json out`? Keep `--verbose` and `--k`, `--threshold`, `--email`.
> - Store results to `evals/last-run.json`? Maybe useful; the brief says report recall@8 and MRR. Writing a run artifact is nice but optional; skip to keep lean. Actually to iterate on chunk size/fusion, having per-case misses printed is enough.
> 
> For matching: 
> ```ts
> function normalize(name: string) {
>   return name.toLowerCase().replace(/^[0-9a-f]{8}-/, "").replace(/[^a-z0-9.]+/g, "-");
> }
> function matchesExpected(chunk, expected) {
>   const stem = normalize(expected).replace(/\.[a-z0-9]+$/, "");
>   const file = normalize(chunk.file_name);
>   const title = normalize(chunk.title);
>   return file === normalize(expected) || file.endsWith(normalize(expected)) || file.includes(stem) && stem.length > 8 ? ... 
> }
> ```
> Keep it simple and readable in a pure module `src/lib/rag/eval.ts` so it's unit-testable:
> 
> ```ts
> export type EvalCase = {...}
> export type RankedFile = { contentId, fileName, title, rank }
> export function rankFiles(chunks): RankedFile[]  // dedupe by contentId preserving order, rank 1..
> export function fileMatches(fileName, title, expected): boolean
> export function scoreCase(files, expected): { recall: 0|1, reciprocalRank: number, firstRank: number|null }
> export function summarize(rows, k): {...}
> ```
> Put in `src/lib/rag/eval.ts`, unit test with made-up rows. Then run-eval.ts imports it. Good separation and testable.
> 
> Sum: `summarize` computes answerable-specific recall/MRR + per-category + unanswerable stats. Let me define:
> 
> ```ts
> export type EvalOutcome = { id, category, question, answerable, expectedFiles, files: RankedFile[], firstRank: number | null };
> export function evaluateCase(c: EvalCase, chunks: RetrievedChunk[], k): EvalOutcome
> export function summarize(outcomes): {
>   total, answerable, unanswerable,
>   recallAtK, mrr, byCategory: Record<string, { total, answerable, recallAtK, mrr }>,
>   unanswerableWithResults
> }
> ```
> Ranking considers only top k chunks for recall@k? For MRR, firstRank over all returned (which is k anyway since we request k).
> 
> Answerable = expectedFiles.length > 0.
> 
> `fileMatches(fileName, title, expected)`:
> - normalize both
> - expectedStem = normalized expected minus extension
> - match if normalized file equals expected, or file endsWith expected, or file includes expectedStem (length>5)
> - or title includes expectedStem? Titles are like "[Ingestion Test] c772bed6-Case_study..." — includes works. But title matching can cause false positives across similar docs. Include title check only if file checks fail and stem length >= 8. Reasonable.

{
  "path": "src/lib/rag/eval.ts",
  "instructions": "Add pure scoring helpers for the retrieval evaluation set.",
  "content": "/**\n * Scoring for the retrieval evaluation set. Pure functions so the numbers can be\n * tested without a database or an embedding provider.\n */\nimport type { RetrievedChunk } from \"./search\";\n\nexport type EvalScope = { industry?: string | null; customer?: string | null };\n\nexport type EvalCase = {\n  id: string;\n  category: string;\n  question: string;\n  /** File names that contain the answer; empty means the library has no answer. */\n  expectedFiles: string[];\n  scope?: EvalScope;\n  notes?: string;\n};\n\nexport type EvalSet = {\n  version: number;\n  k: number;\n  cases: EvalCase[];\n};\n\nexport type RankedFile = {\n  rank: number;\n  contentId: string;\n  fileName: string;\n  title: string;\n};\n\nexport type EvalOutcome = {\n  id: string;\n  category: string;\n  question: string;\n  answerable: boolean;\n  expectedFiles: string[];\n  files: RankedFile[];\n  /** Rank (1-based) of the first expected file, or null when none was found. */\n  firstRank: number | null;\n  recall: number;\n  reciprocalRank: number;\n};\n\nexport type CategorySummary = {\n  total: number;\n  answerable: number;\n  recallAtK: number;\n  mrr: number;\n};\n\nexport type EvalSummary = {\n  total: number;\n  answerable: number;\n  unanswerable: number;\n  recallAtK: number;\n  mrr: number;\n  byCategory: Record<string, CategorySummary>;\n  /** Unanswerable questions that still returned passages; useful for fabrication checks. */\n  unanswerableWithResults: number;\n};\n\n/** Strips the download prefix (`<id8>-`) and punctuation so names compare cleanly. */\nfunction normalize(value: string): string {\n  return value\n    .toLowerCase()\n    .replace(/^[0-9a-f]{8}-/, \"\")\n    .replace(/[^a-z0-9.]+/g, \"-\")\n    .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, \"\");\n}\n\nexport function fileMatchesExpected(\n  file: { fileName: string; title: string },\n  expected: string,\n): boolean {\n  const normalizedExpected = normalize(expected);\n  if (!normalizedExpected) return false;\n  const stem = normalizedExpected.replace(/\\.[a-z0-9]+$/, \"\");\n  const normalizedFile = normalize(file.fileName);\n  const normalizedTitle = normalize(file.title);\n\n  return (\n    normalizedFile === normalizedExpected ||\n    normalizedFile.endsWith(`-${normalizedExpected}`) ||\n    normalizedFile.endsWith(normalizedExpected) ||\n    (stem.length >= 8 && normalizedFile.includes(stem)) ||\n    (stem.length >= 8 && normalizedTitle.includes(stem))\n  );\n}\n\n/** Deduplicates by document while preserving the retrieval order. */\nexport function rankFiles(chunks: readonly RetrievedChunk[]): RankedFile[] {\n  const seen = new Set<string>();\n  const files: RankedFile[] = [];\n  for (const chunk of chunks) {\n    if (seen.has(chunk.contentId)) continue;\n    seen.add(chunk.contentId);\n    files.push({\n      rank: files.length + 1,\n      contentId: chunk.contentId,\n      fileName: chunk.fileName,\n      title: chunk.title,\n    });\n  }\n  return files;\n}\n\nexport function evaluateCase(\n  testCase: EvalCase,\n  chunks: readonly RetrievedChunk[],\n): EvalOutcome {\n  const files = rankFiles(chunks);\n  const answerable = testCase.expectedFiles.length > 0;\n  const match = answerable\n    ? files.find((file) =>\n        testCase.expectedFiles.some((expected) => fileMatchesExpected(file, expected)),\n      )\n    : undefined;\n  const firstRank = match?.rank ?? null;\n\n  return {\n    id: testCase.id,\n    category: testCase.category,\n    question: testCase.question,\n    answerable,\n    expectedFiles: testCase.expectedFiles,\n    files,\n    firstRank,\n    recall: firstRank === null ? 0 : 1,\n    reciprocalRank: firstRank === null ? 0 : 1 / firstRank,\n  };\n}\n\nfunction emptySummary(): CategorySummary {\n  return { total: 0, answerable: 0, recallAtK: 0, mrr: 0 };\n}\n\nexport function summarize(outcomes: readonly EvalOutcome[]): EvalSummary {\n  const categories = new Map<string, { summary: CategorySummary; recall: number; rr: number }>();\n  let answerable = 0;\n  let recall = 0;\n  let reciprocalRank = 0;\n  let unanswerableWithResults = 0;\n\n  for (const outcome of outcomes) {\n    const bucket =\n      categories.get(outcome.category) ??\n      { summary: emptySummary(), recall: 0, rr: 0 };\n    bucket.summary.total += 1;\n\n    if (outcome.answerable) {\n      answerable += 1;\n      recall += outcome.recall;\n      reciprocalRank += outcome.reciprocalRank;\n      bucket.summary.answerable += 1;\n      bucket.recall += outcome.recall;\n      bucket.rr += outcome.reciprocalRank;\n    } else if (outcome.files.length > 0) {\n      unanswerableWithResults += 1;\n    }\n\n    categories.set(outcome.category, bucket);\n  }\n\n  const byCategory: Record<string, CategorySummary> = {};\n  for (const [name, bucket] of categories) {\n    byCategory[name] = {\n      ...bucket.summary,\n      recallAtK: bucket.summary.answerable ? bucket.recall / bucket.summary.answerable : 0,\n      mrr: bucket.summary.answerable ? bucket.rr / bucket.summary.answerable : 0,\n    };\n  }\n\n  return {\n    total: outcomes.length,\n    answerable,\n    unanswerable: outcomes.length - answerable,\n    recallAtK: answerable ? recall / answerable : 0,\n    mrr: answerable ? reciprocalRank / answerable : 0,\n    byCategory,\n    unanswerableWithResults,\n  };\n}\n"
}

RetrievedChunk } from \"./search\";\n\nfunction chunk(contentId: string, fileName: string, title = fileName): RetrievedChunk {\n  return {\n    chunkId: `${contentId}-0`,\n    contentId,\n    content: \"passage\",\n    page: 1,\n    slide: null,\n    heading: null,\n    title,\n    fileName,\n    filePath: `airlines/${fileName}`,\n    industry: \"airlines\",\n    customerName: null,\n    vectorRank: 1,\n    textRank: null,\n    score: 0.03,\n  };\n}\n\ndescribe(\"fileMatchesExpected\", () => {\n  it(\"matches the same name, with or without the download prefix\", () => {\n    const file = { fileName: \"2dfa09fc-United_Airlines_Strategic_Brief.docx\", title: \"Brief\" };\n    expect(fileMatchesExpected(file, \"2dfa09fc-United_Airlines_Strategic_Brief.docx\")).toBe(true);\n    expect(fileMatchesExpected(file, \"United_Airlines_Strategic_Brief.docx\")).toBe(true);\n  });\n\n  it(\"matches a title that names the document\", () => {\n    const file = {\n      fileName: \"upload-1234.pdf\",\n      title: \"[Ingestion Test] c772bed6-Case_study_-_Bajaj_Pulsar.pdf\",\n    };\n    expect(fileMatchesExpected(file, \"c772bed6-Case_study_-_Bajaj_Pulsar.pdf\")).toBe(true);\n  });\n\n  it(\"does not match unrelated files\", () => {\n    const file = { fileName: \"Kotak_Deck.pptx\", title: \"Kotak\" };\n    expect(fileMatchesExpected(file, \"United_Airlines_Strategic_Brief.docx\")).toBe(false);\n  });\n\n  it(\"ignores empty expectations\", () => {\n    expect(fileMatchesExpected({ fileName: \"a.pdf\", title: \"a\" }, \"  \")).toBe(false);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe(\"rankFiles\", () => {\n  it(\"deduplicates by document and keeps retrieval order\", () => {\n    const files = rankFiles([\n      chunk(\"b\", \"b.pdf\"),\n      chunk(\"a\", \"a.pdf\"),\n      { ...chunk(\"a\", \"a.pdf\"), chunkId: \"a-1\" },\n    ]);\n    expect(files.map((f) => f.contentId)).toEqual([\"b\", \"a\"]);\n    expect(files.map((f) => f.rank)).toEqual([1, 2]);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe(\"evaluateCase\", () => {\n  const base: EvalCase = {\n    id: \"case\",\n    category: \"exact\",\n    question: \"q\",\n    expectedFiles: [\"target.pdf\"],\n  };\n\n  it(\"records the rank of the first expected file\", () => {\n    const outcome = evaluateCase(base, [chunk(\"1\", \"other.pdf\"), chunk(\"2\", \"target.pdf\")]);\n    expect(outcome.firstRank).toBe(2);\n    expect(outcome.recall).toBe(1);\n    expect(outcome.reciprocalRank).toBe(0.5);\n  });\n\n  it(\"scores a miss as zero\", () => {\n    const outcome = evaluateCase(base, [chunk(\"1\", \"other.pdf\")]);\n    expect(outcome.firstRank).toBeNull();\n    expect(outcome.recall).toBe(0);\n    expect(outcome.reciprocalRank).toBe(0);\n  });\n\n  it(\"treats a case with no expected file as unanswerable\", () => {\n    const outcome = evaluateCase({ ...base, expectedFiles: [] }, [chunk(\"1\", \"other.pdf\")]);\n    expect(outcome.answerable).toBe(false);\n    expect(outcome.recall).toBe(0);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe(\"summarize\", () => {\n  it(\"averages recall and MRR over answerable cases only\", () => {\n    const outcomes = [\n      evaluateCase({ id: \"a\", category: \"exact\", question: \"\", expectedFiles: [\"a.pdf\"] }, [\n        chunk(\"1\", \"a.pdf\"),\n      ]),\n      evaluateCase({ id: \"b\", category: \"exact\", question: \"\", expectedFiles: [\"b.pdf\"] }, [\n        chunk(\"1\", \"a.pdf\"),\n        chunk(\"2\", \"b.pdf\"),\n      ]),\n      evaluateCase({ id: \"c\", category: \"vague\", question: \"\", expectedFiles: [\"c.pdf\"] }, [\n        chunk(\"1\", \"a.pdf\"),\n      ]),\n      evaluateCase({ id: \"d\", category: \"unanswerable\", question: \"\", expectedFiles: [] }, []),\n    ];\n    const summary = summarize(outcomes);\n\n    expect(summary.total).toBe(4);\n    expect(summary.answerable).toBe(3);\n    expect(summary.unanswerable).toBe(1);\n    expect(summary.recallAtK).toBeCloseTo(2 / 3);\n    expect(summary.mrr).toBeCloseTo((1 + 0.5 + 0) / 3);\n    expect(summary.byCategory.exact).toMatchObject({ total: 2, answerable: 2 });\n    expect(summary.byCategory.unanswerable).toMatchObject({ total: 1, answerable: 0, mrr: 0 });\n  });\n\n  it(\"counts unanswerable questions that still returned passages\", () => {\n    const summary = summarize([\n      evaluateCase({ id: \"a\", category: \"unanswerable\", question: \"\", expectedFiles: [] }, [\n        chunk(\"1\", \"a.pdf\"),\n      ]),\n    ]);\n    expect(summary.unanswerableWithResults).toBe(1);\n > Flow:
> 1. parse args
> 2. env checks: SUPABASE_DB_URL, TOKENFACTORY_API_BASE/KEY
> 3. read eval set (default evals/rag-eval.json), validate: cases length, unique ids, expectedFiles arrays, unanswerable count; fail loudly if malformed.
> 4. resolve subject email: --email or first user in auth.users where is_allowed_email(email) is true; get id + email.
> 5. embed all questions in one batched call (embedTexts with texts) — provider batches internally.
> 6. for each case: transaction with set_config + set local role authenticated; query search_chunks with vector literal cast.
>    Using one sql connection; do:
>    ```
>    const chunks = await sql.begin(async (tx) => {
>      await tx`select set_config('request.jwt.claims', ${claims}, true)`;
>      await tx`set local role authenticated`;
>      const rows = await tx`select * from public.search_chunks(${literal}::vector(1536), ${q}, ${industry}, ${customer}, ${k})`;
>      return rows;
>    });
>    ```
>    Note: `set local role` inside a transaction; after begin ends, role resets. set_config with is_local true also resets.
>    Problem: the tx helper returns after commit; reading rows in tx is fine.

> 7. evaluateCase, print progress, summary.
> 8. Print table: id, category, firstRank, expected vs top files.
> 9. exit code 1 if recallAtK < threshold.

{
  "path": "scripts/run-eval.ts",
  "instructions": "Add the recall@k / MRR evaluation runner.",
  "content": "/**\n * Retrieval evaluation: recall@k and MRR for evals/rag-eval.json.\n *\n * Runs the real hybrid search (public.search_chunks) as an authenticated member,\n * so RLS applies and the numbers match what a signed-in user would get. It needs\n * the database connection string and the embedding provider; a member password\n * session is not required, which keeps it usable before the service role and new\n * API keys are in place.\n *\n * Usage:\n *   bun scripts/run-eval.ts                     # recall@8, needs 80% to pass\n *   bun scripts/run-eval.ts --k 5 --verbose\n *   bun scripts/run-eval.ts --email someone@aionos.ai\n *   bun scripts/run-eval.ts --set evals/other.json --threshold 0.9\n */\nimport { readFileSync } from \"node:fs\";\nimport postgres from \"postgres\";\nimport { embedTexts } from \"../src/lib/rag/embeddings\";\nimport { evaluateCase, summarize, type EvalCase, type EvalSet, type EvalOutcome } from \"../src/lib/rag/eval\";\nimport {\n  clampMatchCount,\n  formatVectorLiteral,\n  mapSearchRows,\n  normalizeScope,\n  type RetrievedChunk,\n} from \"../src/lib/rag/search\";\n\nconst args = process.argv.slice(2);\nconst flag = (name: string) => args.includes(name);\nconst value = (name: string) => {\n  const index = args.indexOf(name);\n  return index >= 0 ? args[index + 1] : undefined;\n};\n\nconst verbose = flag(\"--verbose\");\nconst setPath = value(\"--set\") ?? \"evals/rag-eval.json\";\nconst threshold = Number(value(\"--threshold\") ?? 0.8);\nconst requestedK = Number(value(\"--k\") ?? 0);\nconst requestedEmail = value(\"--email\");\n\nif (!process.env.SUPABASE_DB_URL) {\n  console.error(\"SUPABASE_DB_URL is missing from .env\");\n  process.exit(1);\n}\nif (!process.env.TOKENFACTORY_API_BASE || !process.env.TOKENFACTORY_API_KEY) {\n  console.error(\"TOKENFACTORY_API_BASE / TOKENFACTORY_API_KEY are missing from .env\");\n  process.exit(1);\n}\n\nfunction fail(message: string): never {\n  console.error(message);\n  process.exit(1);\n}\n\nfunction loadEvalSet(path: string): EvalSet {\n  let parsed: unknown;\n  try {\n    parsed = JSON.parse(readFileSync(path, \"utf8\"));\n  } catch (error) {\n    fail(`Could not read ${path}: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : error}`);\n  }\n  const set = parsed as EvalSet;\n  if (!set || !Array.isArray(set.cases) || set.cases.length === 0) {\n    fail(`${path} has no cases`);\n  }\n  const seen = new Set<string>();\n  for (const testCase of set.cases) {\n    if (!testCase.id || !testCase.question || !Array.isArray(testCase.expectedFiles)) {\n      fail(`Malformed eval case: ${JSON.stringify(testCase).slice(0, 120)}`);\n    }\n    if (seen.has(testCase.id)) fail(`Duplicate eval case id: ${testCase.id}`);\n    seen.add(testCase.id);\n  }\n  return { version: set.version ?? 1, k: requestedK || set.k || 8, cases: set.cases };\n}\n\nconst set = loadEvalSet(setPath);\nconst k = clampMatchCount(set.k);\n\nconst sql = postgres(process.env.SUPABASE_DB_URL, { ssl: \"require\", max: 1 });\n\ntype Subject = { id: string; email: string };\n\nasync function resolveSubject(): Promise<Subject> {\n  if (requestedEmail) {\n    const [row] = await sql<Subject[]>`\n      select id, email from auth.users\n      where lower(email) = lower(${requestedEmail})\n      limit 1`;\n    if (!row) fail(`No auth user with email ${requestedEmail}`);\n    return row;\n  }\n  const rows = await sql<Subject[]>`\n    select id, email from auth.users\n    where public.is_allowed_email(email)\n    order by created_at\n    limit 2`;\n  if (rows.length === 0) fail(\"No allowed auth user found; pass --email <address>\");\n  if (rows.length > 1) {\n    console.warn(\n      `Multiple allowed users found; using ${rows[0]!.email}. Pass --email to choose another.`,\n    );\n  }\n  return rows[0]!;\n}\n\nasync function searchAsMember(\n  subject: Subject,\n  embedding: readonly number[],\n  queryText: string,\n  testCase: EvalCase,\n): Promise<RetrievedChunk[]> {\n  const scope = normalizeScope(testCase.scope ?? {});\n  const literal = formatVectorLiteral(embedding);\n  const claims = JSON.stringify({ sub: subject.id, email: subject.email, role: \"authenticated\" });\n\n  return sql.begin(async (tx) => {\n    await tx`select set_config('request.jwt.claims', ${claims}, true)`;\n    await tx.unsafe(\"set local role authenticated\");\n    const rows = await tx`\n      select * from public.search_chunks(\n        ${literal}::vector(1536),\n        ${queryText},\n        ${scope.industry},\n        ${scope.customer},\n        ${k}\n      )`;\n    return mapSearchRows(rows as never);\n  });\n}\n\nfunction printOutcome(outcome: EvalOutcome) {\n  const hit = outcome.answerable ? (outcome.firstRank === null ? \"MISS\" : `hit@${outcome.firstRank}`) : \"n/a\";\n  console.log(`\\n[${outcome.category}] ${hit} ${outcome.id}`);\n  console.log(`  Q: ${outcome.question}`);\n  const top = outcome.files.slice(0, verbose ? k : 3);\n  for (const file of top) console.log(`  ${file.rank}. ${file.fileName}`);\n  if (outcome.answerable && outcome.firstRank === null) {\n    console.log(`  expected: ${outcome.expectedFiles.join(\", \")}`);\n  }\n}\n\nasync function main() {\n  const subject = await resolveSubject();\n  const [library] = await sql<{ items: number; indexed: number; ready: number }[]>`\n    select\n      (select count(*) from public.content_items)::int as items,\n      (select count(distinct content_id) from public.document_chunks)::int as indexed,\n      (select count(*) from public.ingestion_jobs where status = 'ready')::int as ready`;\n  console.log(\n    `Library: ${library?.items ?? 0} files, ${library?.indexed ?? 0} indexed ` +\n      `(${library?.ready ?? 0} jobs ready). Subject: ${subject.email}`,\n  );\n  console.log(`Eval: ${setPath} (${set.cases.length} cases, k=${k})\\n`);\n\n  const texts = set.cases.map((testCase) => testCase.question);\n  const { embeddings, promptTokens } = await embedTexts(texts, {\n    apiKey: process.env.TOKENFACTORY_API_KEY!,\n    baseUrl: process.env.TOKENFACTORY_API_BASE!,\n  });\n\n  const outcomes: EvalOutcome[] = [];\n  const startedAt = Date.now();\n  for (const [index, testCase] of set.cases.entries()) {\n    const embedding = embeddings[index];\n    if (!embedding) fail(`No embedding returned for case ${testCase.id}`);\n    let chunks: RetrievedChunk[] = [];\n    try {\n      chunks = await searchAsMember(subject, embedding, testCase.question, testCase);\n    } catch (error) {\n      fail(\n        `Search failed for ${testCase.id}: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`,\n      );\n    }\n    const outcome = evaluateCase(testCase, chunks);\n    outcomes.push(outcome);\n    printOutcome(outcome);\n  }\n\n  const elapsed = (Date.now() - startedAt) / 1000;\n  const summary = summarize(outcomes);\n\n  console.log(\"\\n=== Summary ===\");\n  console.log(\n    `recall@${k}: ${(summary.recallAtK * 100).toFixed(1)}% ` +\n      `(${outcomes.filter((o) => o.recall === 1).length}/${summary.answerable} answerable cases)`,\n  );\n  console.log(`MRR: ${summary.mrr.toFixed(3)}`);\n  console.log(\n    `Unanswerable: ${summary.unanswerable} cases, ${summary.unanswerableWithResults} still returned passages`,\n  );\n  for (const [category, bucket] of Object.entries(summary.byCategory)) {\n    console.log(\n      `  ${category.padEnd(12)} n=${String(bucket.total).padEnd(3)} ` +\n        `answerable=${String(bucket.answerable).padEnd(3)} ` +\n        `recall=${(bucket.recallAtK * 100).toFixed(1)}% mrr=${bucket.mrr.toFixed(3)}`,\n    );\n  }\n  console.log(\n    `\\n${summary.total} queries in ${elapsed.toFixed(1)}s ` +\n      `(${((elapsed * 1000) / summary.total).toFixed(0)}ms each, ${promptTokens ?? \"?\"} query tokens)`,\n  );\n\n  if (summary.answerable === 0) {\n    console.log(\"\\nNo answerable cases; nothing to score.\");\n  } else if (summary.recallAtK < threshold) {\n    console.log(\n      `\\nBELOW THRESHOLD: recall@${k} must reach ${(threshold * 100).toFixed(0)}%. ` +\n        \"Iterate on chunk size, fusion or add the rerank step, and report before/after.\",\n    );\n    process.exitCode = 1;\n  } else {\n    console.log(`\\nThreshold met: recall@${k} >= ${(threshold * 100).toFixed(0)}%.`);\n  }\n}\n\ntry {\n  await main();\n} catch (error) {\n  console.error(error instanceof Error ? error.message : error);\n  process.exitCode = 1;\n} finally {\n  await sql.end();\n}\n"
}
```

> _Reasoning_
> The runner works. Recall is 7.3% because only 3 of 31 documents are indexed. That's expected and I'll report it as "pipeline check, not a meaningful eval score; needs the library ingested".
> 
> Interesting: the 3 hits are the 3 indexed docs (Bajaj exact, Adani attendees, InterGlobe?). Good sign.
> 
> Also note: 46 queries in 45s ≈ 977ms each — because `sql.begin` + search; embeddings already batched (669 tokens). 977ms per query is slowish — likely the HNSW scan + join on tiny data shouldn't take ~1s. Maybe it's the `set local role`/transaction overhead + network. Fine.
> 
> But wait: unanswerable cases return arbitrary passages — expected (no threshold). Chat will need "not found" handling in Phase 4.
> 
> Scoped cases 0% — because the scoped industries don't match current test rows (all "airlines"), and expected files aren't indexed. Fine.
> 
> Now: the scoped eval assumption risk. Also `formatVectorLiteral` accepted real embeddings (1536). Good — PostgREST vector literal path not yet tested but SQL cast path works.
> 
> Next: admin debug page. Route `_authenticated/rag-debug.tsx`. Wait — route naming with hyphen: TanStack file-based routes convert `rag-debug.tsx` → `/rag-debug`. Fine.
> 
> UI:
> - beforeLoad: adminAccessQuery gate (same as admin).
> - head meta.
> - Page with Input (query), industry Select (industriesQuery), customer optional text input? Use Input for customer. Match count Select (5/8/12/20).
> - Submit → `useMutation` calling `searchLibrary({ data: {...} })`.
> - Results: score, vector/text rank, file name, page/slide/heading, snippet. Errors, empty state.
> - Disable submit while pending.
> 
> Keep it functional but simple; it's a debug page.
> 
> Also: should the debug page show quota? No.
> 
> Need a hint that it's temporary/admin-only.
> 
> Let me write it following the code style of admin.tsx (dense, but I'll use readable formatting).
> 
> Check src/routes/_authenticated/route.tsx to know auth gating.
> 
> Also there's `src/routeTree.gen.ts`? File-based routing generates it during build. Fine.
> 
> Write the page.

- path: src/routes/_authenticated/route.tsx
  content: |
      import { createFileRoute, Outlet, redirect } from "@tanstack/react-router";
      import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";
      import { AppHeader } from "@/components/AppHeader";
      
      export const Route = createFileRoute("/_authenticated")({
        ssr: false,
        beforeLoad: async () => {
          const { data } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
          if (!data.user) throw redirect({ to: "/auth" });
          return { user: data.user };
        },
        component: () => (
          <div className="min-h-screen">
            <AppHeader />
            <Outlet />
          </div>
        ),
      });
      
- path: src/lib/roles.ts
  content: |
      import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
      import { useRouteContext } from "@tanstack/react-router";
      import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";
      import { roleFromRows } from "./permissions";
      
      export function useRole() {
        const { user } = useRouteContext({ from: "/_authenticated" });
        const query = useQuery({
          queryKey: ["role", user.id],
          staleTime: 60_000,
          queryFn: async () => {
            const { data, error } = await supabase.from("user_roles").select("role").eq("user_id", user.id);
            if (error) throw error;
            return roleFromRows(data);
          },
        });
        return { role: query.data ?? "member", isAdmin: query.data === "admin" };
      }
```

> _Reasoning_
> Typecheck issues:
> 
> 1. `search.ts:128` — args include `filter_industry: string | null | undefined` from RetrievalScope optional. Since normalizeScope returns {industry: string|null; customer: string|null}, but the type of the search input is RetrievalScope (with undefined). Fix: type SearchInput.scope as `{ industry: string | null; customer: string | null }` (normalized). Then retrieve passes normalized scope; deps.search receives normalized. Adjust types: SearchInput.scope: NormalizedScope. But then tests pass scope {industry:null,...} fine.
> 
> Actually retrieve passes `scope: normalizeScope(scope)`; with SearchInput.scope typed as RetrievalScope, TS narrows to include undefined. Change SearchInput to use NormalizedScope type.
> 
> 2. Test property access with index signature → use bracket notation.

            embedQuery: async () => vector,
            search,
          });

            matchCount: 5,
          });
        });

          await expect(
            retrieve("pricing", {}, 8, {
              embedQuery: async () => {
                throw new Error("Embedding provider failed (429)");
              },
              search: async () => [],
            }),
          ).rejects.toThrow(/429/);
        });

      describe("searchLibraryChunks", () => {
        it("sends the caller's filters and a vector literal to search_chunks", async () => {
          const rpc = vi.fn(async () => ({ data: [row], error: null }));
          const client = { rpc } as unknown as Parameters<typeof searchLibraryChunks>[0];
      
          const result = await searchLibraryChunks(
            client,
            "churn",
            { industry: "airlines", customer: "IndiGo" },
            6,
            async () => vector,
          );
      
          expect(result).toEqual([hit]);
          const [fn, args] = rpc.mock.calls[0] as unknown as [string, Record<string, unknown>];
          expect(fn).toBe("search_chunks");
          expect(args).toMatchObject({
            query_text: "churn",
            filter_industry: "airlines",
            filter_customer: "IndiGo",
            match_count: 6,
          });
          expect(String(args.query_embedding).startsWith("[0.5,0.5,")).toBe(true);
        });
      
        it("surfaces a database error instead of an empty result set", async () => {
          const client = {
            rpc: async () => ({ data: null, error: { message: "permission denied" } }),
          } as unknown as Parameters<typeof searchLibraryChunks>[0];
      
          await expect(searchLibraryChunks(client, "churn", {}, 8, async () => vector)).rejects.toThrow(
            /permission denied/,
          );
        });
      });

> Also I should double-check the SQL for a subtle bug: in `scoped`, if `query_text` is empty, `query_ts` yields `websearch_to_tsquery('english','')` = empty tsquery; `tsv @@ ''::tsquery` is false — fine. Also `nullif(btrim(coalesce(query_text,'')),'')` guard.
> 
> Another subtle issue: RRF ordering — `coalesce(1.0/(60+v.rank), 0.0)`: v.rank is integer; 1.0/(60+rank) → numeric. fine.
> 
> But there's a real concern: `vector_hits` gets top 50 by distance within scope; `text_hits` similar. RRF merges. Good.
> 
> Potential issue with `limit least(greatest(coalesce(match_count, 8), 1), 50)`.
> 
> Now run full test suite and eval.

import { QueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { createRouter, rootRouteId } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import { routeTree } from "@/routeTree.gen";

// Match routes without running loaders or rendering: loaders may need a server or
// network the test run lacks, and jsdom never loads the stylesheets React waits on.
describe("App routing", () => {
  it("matches a page for / instead of falling back to not found", () => {
    const router = createRouter({ routeTree, context: { queryClient: new QueryClient() } });

    const matches = router.matchRoutes("/");

    expect(matches.at(-1)?.routeId).not.toBe(rootRouteId);
  });
});

where schemaname = 'public' and tablename = 'document_chunks'`;\n    console.log(\"chunk policies:\", pol);\n    await tx.unsafe(\"reset role\");\n\n    throw new Error(\"__ROLLBACK__\");\n  });\n}
