> _Reasoning_
> The chunk table has 9 rows: 8 pre-existing + 1 inserted. So the RLS test's `chunkCount === 1` assertion is wrong because the database is no longer empty — verify-rag assumed an empty table. It's a script bug (test data isolation), pre-existing. The db has 8 chunks from the 3 ingested docs.
> 
> The isolation test should count only the test item's chunks, not the whole table. Similarly member B, admin jobs counts, etc. Better fix: filter counts by `content_id = ${content.id}` for chunks, and for jobs filter by content_id; but the admin/member jobs checks also count globally (2 existing jobs). Those checks would fail too, after the first failure.
> 
> Also "member B reads chunks" would count 9.
> 
> The proper fix: make assertions scoped to the test content item. For jobs: `select count(*) from ingestion_jobs where content_id = ...`. For chunks: same. For the "member cannot read ingestion jobs" (expect 0) a scoped count is right.
> 
> Also `full-text search misses unrelated terms` uses global count for 'zebra' — fine (0 either way).
> `chunksAfterDelete === 0` globally — should scope, since other docs exist.
> 
> And `rpcCount === 1` for match_document_chunks: globally, 8 existing + 1 = 9. Need to filter match_count? Can't filter by item. Change to check that results include the test chunk: `some(r => r.content_id === content.id)`. The RPC returns up to 5 rows; the test chunk may not be in top 5 with a zero query vector. Hmm.
> 
> The cleanest fix: run verify-rag against an isolated content item but assertions scoped by content id; for the RPC, request match_count=50 and check it contains the test chunk (or check count > 0 and that all returned ids are visible). Since document_chunks is small, 50 covers all.
> 
> Also `filteredIn === 1` becomes: results filtered to 'airlines' should include the test content; and `filteredOut === 0` for 'bfsi' — if there are no bfsi docs, 0 holds; but if the library has bfsi docs later, it breaks. Better: assert no returned row has industry 'bfsi' and that the test chunk is absent. Since the RPC doesn't return industry... `match_document_chunks` returns industry. Use `every(r => r.industry === 'bfsi')` and `!ids.includes(content.id)`.
> 
> Similarly hybrid checks I added: `hybrid.length === 1` globally → should scope by content_id.
> 
> Let me rewrite the affected assertions to be robust with existing data:
> 
> - member chunk count scoped: `where content_id = ${content.id}` → 1.
> - rpcCount: change to fetch rows with match_count 50 and check `rows.some(r => r.content_id === content.id)`.
> - filteredIn: rows from 'airlines' contain the test item.
> - filteredOut: for 'bfsi', every row has industry 'bfsi' and none is the test item.
> - memberBChunks scoped → 1 (content visible to all allowed users).
> - memberJobs scoped → 0; adminJobs scoped → 1.
> - replace duplicate test: `afterReplace` scoped already? It uses `where content_id = ${content.id}` — yes.
> - fts scoped: `where content_id = ${content.id}` — yes; ftsFiltered global 'zebra' — fine.
> - chunk delete cascade: scoped → 0.
> - jobs delete cascade: scoped → 0.
> - chat thread/messages: scoped by thread id — messagesAfterDelete global count: currently `select count(*) from chat_messages` — should scope by thread. It's 0 anyway but fix.
> - hybrid checks: count rows with content_id = test id.
> 
> That makes the suite re-runnable against a populated DB, which is a real improvement for Phase 3 verification (and needed now). Update.

      import { createFileRoute, Link, redirect } from "@tanstack/react-router";
      import { useMutation } from "@tanstack/react-query";
      import { useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
      import { Search } from "lucide-react";
      import { adminAccessQuery } from "@/lib/admin-content";
      import { industriesQuery } from "@/lib/industries";
      import { searchLibrary, type SearchLibraryResult } from "@/lib/rag/search.functions";
      import type { RetrievedChunk } from "@/lib/rag/search";
      import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
      import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
      import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
      import {
        Select,
        SelectContent,
        SelectItem,
        SelectTrigger,
        SelectValue,
      } from "@/components/ui/select";
      
      // Temporary debug surface for retrieval quality; remove once chat ships.
      export const Route = createFileRoute("/_authenticated/rag-debug")({
        beforeLoad: async ({ context }) => {
          const allowed = await context.queryClient.fetchQuery(adminAccessQuery);
          if (!allowed) throw redirect({ to: "/library" });
        },
        loader: async ({ context }) => ({
          industries: await context.queryClient.ensureQueryData(industriesQuery),

        head: () => ({

A concise explanation of the reasoning behind Phase 3 work: why hybrid search, why RRF, why security-invoker, why retrieval seam, why eval set, why debug page, why the specific module split (embeddings.ts), and how it helps the product (chat, deck generation later), plus honest limits (eval score not meaningful yet).
> 
> Keep it brief, non-code, structured. Mention the key decisions and their payoff. No tool calls needed — a question gets an answer.
> 
> Structure:
> 1. Where the project stood (Phases 0–2 done: extraction, schema, ingestion worker).
> 2. What Phase 3 is and why it's the current bottleneck.
> 3. Each piece and why:
>    - search_chunks SQL (hybrid + RRF): exact terms (product names, acronyms) and semantic phrasing both work; one round trip; RLS applies inside so citations can't leak; joins content_items so renames/moves don't go stale.
>    - Why not external vector DB: RLS + cascade deletes are the security model; a second store would duplicate privacy rules.
>    - retrieve() seam: single function so chat now and deck generator later share it; deps injected so unit-testable and runtime-agnostic.
>    - embeddings.ts split: query embedding must run in Worker on request; keeping node:crypto out avoids runtime trouble; same model for docs and queries (mixing models breaks similarity).
>    - searchLibrary server fn: uses caller JWT not service role — that's what enforces "no citation for a file you can't access".
>    - debug page: see scores/ranks before wiring chat, tune chunking/fusion without guessing.
>    - eval set + runner: turns "looks fine" into a number; recall@8/MRR; unanswerable cases guard against fabrication/over-retrieval; runner works with DB URL only (no service role, no member password) and simulates a member so RLS is exercised.
>    - verify-rag extension + fix: proves isolation and that anon cannot call search; also made the test re-runnable on a populated DB (it assumed empty).
> 4. How it helps: chat grounded with citations is now one call away; deck gen reuse; cost/latency profile; measurable iteration.
> 5. Honest limits: eval numbers meaningless now (3 of 31 files indexed), GoTrue broken, scoped eval assumptions.
> 
> Keep it tight, maybe 250-350 words. Use short sections/bullets. No steps to take.

## Where this sits

Phases 0–2 (extraction, schema/RLS, ingestion worker) were already in the repo and working — the live database had chunks and `ready` jobs. What was missing was the piece that turns a stored passage index into something a user can ask a question of. That's Phase 3, and it's the gate for chat, because chat can only be as good and as safe as retrieval.

## Why each piece exists

**`search_chunks` (vector + full text, merged with RRF).** Pure vector search misses exact product names, customer names and acronyms — the things sales users actually type. Full text alone misses paraphrases. Running both and merging by reciprocal rank keeps a strong match from either side, in one round trip. It joins `content_items` at query time instead of copying industry/customer onto chunks, so moves, copies and company renames can't leave stale metadata behind.

**Security-invoker, caller's JWT, no service role.** This is what makes the later citations trustworthy: the database decides which chunks exist for that user, so the UI cannot cite a file they can't open. `anon` is explicitly denied, and I verified that.

**One `retrieve(query, scope, k)` seam.** Chat needs it now; the deck generator and the company-URL theming work will need the same thing later. Keeping it behind one function (with the embedder and RPC injected) means those features reuse it unchanged, and it stays unit-testable without a database.

**Splitting embeddings out of `ingest.ts`.** Query embedding happens inside the Cloudflare Worker on every search, so it must not drag in Node-only imports. Same model for documents and queries is non-negotiable — mixing models silently ruins similarity, so both call the same client.

**Debug page and eval harness.** Retrieval quality is invisible without numbers. The admin page shows scores and the vector/text rank split, so chunk size or fusion weights can be tuned on evidence rather than vibes; the eval set gives recall@8 and MRR, plus "no answer" cases that expose how often retrieval returns *something* when it should return nothing — which is exactly the fabrication risk chat has to handle in Phase 4.

## How it helps

A working, measurable retrieval layer: chat becomes "rewrite → retrieve → rerank → answer → cite", the future deck feature gets a stable API, and every change to chunking or ranking can be scored instead of guessed.

## Honest limits

The 7.3% recall from the run is not a real score — only 3 of the 31 drafted library files are in this database, so 28 questions *cannot* succeed yet. Also, member password sign-in currently dies inside Supabase Auth (500 "Database error querying schema" for any password), so I verified retrieval through the database as an authenticated member rather than through the app.

- path: src/lib/rag/search.ts
  content: |
      /**
       * Retrieval seam. Everything that needs library passages (chat now, deck
       * generation later) goes through retrieve(query, scope, k, deps), so ranking,
       * filtering and citation metadata stay in one place.
       *
       * The database function is security-invoker: the caller's JWT and RLS decide
       * which chunks exist at all. This module never falls back to the service role.
       */
      import type { SupabaseClient } from "@supabase/supabase-js";
      import type { Database } from "../../integrations/supabase/types";
      import { DEFAULT_MATCH_COUNT, EMBEDDING_DIMENSIONS, MAX_MATCH_COUNT } from "./config";
      
      export type RetrievalScope = {
        industry?: string | null | undefined;
        customer?: string | null | undefined;
      };
      
      export type RetrievedChunk = {
        chunkId: string;
        contentId: string;
        content: string;
        page: number | null;
        slide: number | null;
        heading: string | null;
        title: string;
        fileName: string;
        filePath: string;
        industry: string;
        customerName: string | null;
        vectorRank: number | null;
        textRank: number | null;
        score: number;
      };
      
      export type NormalizedScope = {
        industry: string | null;
        customer: string | null;
      };
      
      export type SearchInput = {
        embedding: readonly number[];
        queryText: string;
        scope: NormalizedScope;
        matchCount: number;
      };
      
      export type RetrieveDeps = {
        embedQuery: (query: string) => Promise<number[]>;
        search: (input: SearchInput) => Promise<RetrievedChunk[]>;
      };
      
      type SearchRow = Database["public"]["Functions"]["search_chunks"]["Returns"][number];
      
      /** Blank scope values mean "no filter", never an empty-looking customer. */
      export function normalizeScope(scope: RetrievalScope = {}): NormalizedScope {
        const clean = (value?: string | null) => {
          const trimmed = (value ?? "").trim();
          return trimmed.length > 0 ? trimmed : null;
        };
        return { industry: clean(scope.industry), customer: clean(scope.customer) };
      }
      
      export function clampMatchCount(count?: number): number {
        if (count === undefined || !Number.isFinite(count)) return DEFAULT_MATCH_COUNT;
        return Math.min(Math.max(Math.trunc(count), 1), MAX_MATCH_COUNT);
      }
      
      /** PostgREST passes a `vector` argument as its text form: [0.1,0.2,...]. */
      export function formatVectorLiteral(vector: readonly number[]): string {
        if (vector.length !== EMBEDDING_DIMENSIONS) {
          throw new Error(
            `Query embedding has ${vector.length} dimensions, expected ${EMBEDDING_DIMENSIONS}`,
          );
        }
        for (const value of vector) {
          if (!Number.isFinite(value)) throw new Error("Query embedding contains a non-finite value");
        }
        return `[${vector.join(",")}]`;
      }
      
      export function mapSearchRows(rows: readonly SearchRow[]): RetrievedChunk[] {
        return rows.map((row) => ({
          chunkId: row.chunk_id,
          contentId: row.content_id,
          content: row.content,
          page: row.page,
          slide: row.slide,
          heading: row.heading,
          title: row.title,
          fileName: row.file_name,
          filePath: row.file_path,
          industry: row.industry,
          customerName: row.customer_name,
          vectorRank: row.vector_rank,
          textRank: row.text_rank,
          score: row.score,
        }));
      }
      
      /**
       * Single retrieval entry point. Returns ranked passages from files the caller is
       * allowed to read; an empty list means nothing matched (an empty query is not an
       * error, it just has no results).
       */
      export async function retrieve(
        query: string,
        scope: RetrievalScope = {},
        k: number = DEFAULT_MATCH_COUNT,
        deps: RetrieveDeps,
      ): Promise<RetrievedChunk[]> {
        const queryText = query.trim();
        if (!queryText) return [];
      
        const matchCount = clampMatchCount(k);
        const embedding = await deps.embedQuery(queryText);
        return deps.search({ embedding, queryText, scope: normalizeScope(scope), matchCount });
      }
      
      /** Wires retrieve() to an authenticated Supabase client and the gateway embedder. */
      export async function searchLibraryChunks(
        client: SupabaseClient<Database>,
        query: string,
        scope: RetrievalScope,
        k: number,
        embed: (query: string) => Promise<number[]>,
      ): Promise<RetrievedChunk[]> {
        return retrieve(query, scope, k, {
          embedQuery: embed,
          search: async ({ embedding, queryText, scope: scoped, matchCount }) => {
            const { data, error } = await client.rpc("search_chunks", {
              query_embedding: formatVectorLiteral(embedding),
              query_text: queryText,
              filter_industry: scoped.industry,
              filter_customer: scoped.customer,
              match_count: matchCount,
            });
            if (error) throw new Error(`Library search failed: ${error.message}`);
            return mapSearchRows((data ?? []) as SearchRow[]);
          },
        });
      }
      
- path: src/lib/rag/search.functions.ts
  content: |
      /**
       * Authenticated library search. Retrieval runs with the caller's JWT through
       * requireSupabaseAuth, so RLS decides which files are visible; the service role
       * is never used here. Only the query embedding leaves the server.
       */
      import { createServerFn } from "@tanstack/react-start";
      import { z } from "zod";
      import { requireSupabaseAuth } from "@/integrations/supabase/auth-middleware";
      import { DEFAULT_MATCH_COUNT } from "./config";
      import { embedQuery } from "./embeddings";
      import { searchLibraryChunks, type RetrievedChunk } from "./search";
      
      export type SearchLibraryResult =
        { ok: true; chunks: RetrievedChunk[] } | { ok: false; error: string };
      
      function createQueryEmbedder():
        { ok: true; embed: (query: string) => Promise<number[]> } | { ok: false; error: string } {
        const apiKey = process.env["TOKENFACTORY_API_KEY"];
        const baseUrl = process.env["TOKENFACTORY_API_BASE"];
        if (!apiKey || !baseUrl) {
          console.error("[RAG] Embedding provider credentials are not configured");
          return { ok: false, error: "Library search is not configured yet." };
        }
        return { ok: true, embed: (query: string) => embedQuery(query, { apiKey, baseUrl }) };
      }
      
      export const searchLibrary = createServerFn({ method: "POST" })
        .middleware([requireSupabaseAuth])
        .inputValidator((data: unknown) =>
          z
            .object({
              query: z.string().trim().min(2).max(500),
              industry: z.string().trim().max(64).optional(),
              customer: z.string().trim().max(120).optional(),
              matchCount: z.number().int().min(1).max(50).optional(),
            })
            .parse(data),
        )
        .handler(async ({ data, context }): Promise<SearchLibraryResult> => {
          const embedder = createQueryEmbedder();
          if (!embedder.ok) return { ok: false, error: embedder.error };
      
          try {
            const chunks = await searchLibraryChunks(
              context.supabase,
              data.query,
              { industry: data.industry, customer: data.customer },
              data.matchCount ?? DEFAULT_MATCH_COUNT,
              embedder.embed,
            );
            return { ok: true, chunks };
          } catch (error) {
            // Provider or database detail stays in the server log, not the response.
            console.error("[RAG] search failed", error instanceof Error ? error.message : error);
            return { ok: false, error: "Library search failed. Please try again." };
          }
        });
      
- path: src/lib/rag/embeddings.ts
  content: |
      /**
       * Embedding client shared by ingestion (Bun worker) and query retrieval
       * (Cloudflare Worker). No Node built-ins here on purpose: the Worker imports
       * this module while the ingestion worker imports it too.
       */
      import {
        EMBEDDING_BATCH_SIZE,
        EMBEDDING_DIMENSIONS,
        EMBEDDING_MODEL,
        EMBEDDING_USD_PER_TOKEN,
      } from "./config";
      
      export type EmbedOptions = {
        apiKey: string;
        baseUrl: string;
        fetchImpl?: typeof fetch;
        model?: string;
        dimensions?: number;
        batchSize?: number;
      };
      
      export type EmbeddingResult = {
        embeddings: number[][];
        promptTokens: number | null;
        costUsd: number | null;
        /** True when cost was derived from the token count instead of provider usage. */
        costEstimated: boolean;
      };
      
      type EmbeddingResponse = {
        data?: Array<{ embedding?: number[]; index?: number }>;
        usage?: { prompt_tokens?: number; cost?: number };
        error?: { message?: string } | string;
      };
      
      /** Embeds texts in provider-sized batches, preserving input order. */
      export async function embedTexts(
        texts: readonly string[],
        options: EmbedOptions,
      ): Promise<EmbeddingResult> {
        const embeddings: number[][] = [];
        let promptTokens = 0;
        let costUsd = 0;
        let sawUsage = false;
        let sawCost = false;
      
        const batchSize = Math.max(1, Math.min(options.batchSize ?? EMBEDDING_BATCH_SIZE, 100));
        const dimensions = options.dimensions ?? EMBEDDING_DIMENSIONS;
        const model = options.model ?? EMBEDDING_MODEL;
        const doFetch = options.fetchImpl ?? fetch;
        const baseUrl = options.baseUrl.replace(/\/+$/, "");
      
        for (let start = 0; start < texts.length; start += batchSize) {
          const batch = texts.slice(start, start + batchSize);
          const response = await doFetch(`${baseUrl}/embeddings`, {
            method: "POST",
            headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", Authorization: `Bearer ${options.apiKey}` },
            body: JSON.stringify({ model, input: batch, dimensions }),
          });
      
          const raw = await response.text();
          if (!response.ok) {
            throw new Error(`Embedding provider failed (${response.status}): ${providerMessage(raw)}`);
          }
      
          let body: EmbeddingResponse;
          try {
            body = JSON.parse(raw) as EmbeddingResponse;
          } catch {
            throw new Error("Embedding provider returned a non-JSON response");
          }
      
          const data = body.data;
          if (!Array.isArray(data) || data.length !== batch.length) {
            throw new Error(
              `Embedding provider returned ${Array.isArray(data) ? data.length : 0} vectors for ${batch.length} inputs`,
            );
          }
      
          // Providers may reorder; index is authoritative when present.
          const ordered = [...data].sort((a, b) => (a.index ?? 0) - (b.index ?? 0));
          for (const entry of ordered) {
            const vector = entry.embedding;
            if (!Array.isArray(vector) || vector.length !== dimensions) {
              throw new Error(
                `Embedding dimension mismatch: expected ${dimensions}, received ${vector?.length ?? 0}`,
              );
            }
            embeddings.push(vector);
          }
      
          if (body.usage) {
            sawUsage = true;
            promptTokens += body.usage.prompt_tokens ?? 0;
            if (typeof body.usage.cost === "number") {
              sawCost = true;
              costUsd += body.usage.cost;
            }
          }
        }
      
        // Batched responses omit cost, so fall back to the observed per-token price.
        const costEstimated = sawUsage && !sawCost;
        if (costEstimated) costUsd = promptTokens * EMBEDDING_USD_PER_TOKEN;
      
        return {
          embeddings,
          promptTokens: sawUsage ? promptTokens : null,
          costUsd: sawUsage ? costUsd : null,
          costEstimated,
        };
      }
      
      /** Embeds one query string; retrieval always sends exactly one input. */
      export async function embedQuery(
        text: string,
        options: Omit<EmbedOptions, "batchSize">,
      ): Promise<number[]> {
        const query = text.trim();
        if (!query) throw new Error("Cannot embed an empty query");
        const { embeddings } = await embedTexts([query], options);
        const vector = embeddings[0];
        if (!vector) throw new Error("Embedding provider returned no vector for the query");
        return vector;
      }
      
      /** Keeps provider text in logs useful without dumping a whole response body. */
      function providerMessage(raw: string): string {
        try {
          const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as EmbeddingResponse;
          const message = typeof parsed.error === "string" ? parsed.error : (parsed.error?.message ?? "");
          if (message) return message.slice(0, 300);
        } catch {
          // fall through to the raw body
        }
        return raw.slice(0, 300);
      }
      
- path: src/lib/rag/config.ts
  content: |
      /**
       * RAG provider settings. Shared by the ingestion worker, query embedding and
       * retrieval. EMBEDDING_DIMENSIONS must match document_chunks.embedding
       * (vector(1536)) in drizzle/migrations/0010_rag.sql; changing one without the
       * other silently breaks retrieval, so change them together.
       */
      export const EMBEDDING_MODEL = "qwen/qwen3-embedding-8b";
      export const EMBEDDING_DIMENSIONS = 1536;
      
      /** Provider limit is 100 input texts per embeddings call. */
      export const EMBEDDING_BATCH_SIZE = 100;
      
      /** Recorded on every chunk so a model switch is visible, not guessed. */
      export const EMBEDDING_MODEL_KEY = EMBEDDING_MODEL;
      
      /**
       * Provider price per embedding token, derived from reported usage
       * (859 tokens cost $0.0000859). Only used when a batched response omits cost,
       * which this provider does above a few inputs.
       */
      export const EMBEDDING_USD_PER_TOKEN = 1e-7;
      
      /**
       * Retrieval defaults. public.search_chunks (drizzle/migrations/0014) merges a
       * pgvector top-50 with a full-text top-50 using reciprocal rank fusion k=60, so
       * retrieve() only has to ask for the final result count.
       */
      export const DEFAULT_MATCH_COUNT = 8;
      export const MAX_MATCH_COUNT = 50;
      
- path: drizzle/migrations/0014_hybrid_search.sql
  content: |
      -- Phase 3: hybrid retrieval over the passage index.
      --
      -- One security-invoker function so RLS on document_chunks and content_items
      -- applies to the caller's JWT. Vector similarity and full-text search each
      -- produce a top-N list; the lists are merged with reciprocal rank fusion (k=60)
      -- so an exact term match and a semantic match both survive. Industry, customer
      -- and file metadata are joined from content_items at query time, never stored
      -- on the chunk, so moves, copies and company renames cannot leave stale values.
      set search_path = public, extensions;
      
      create or replace function public.search_chunks(
        query_embedding vector(1536),
        query_text text default null,
        filter_industry text default null,
        filter_customer text default null,
        match_count integer default 8
      )
      returns table (
        chunk_id uuid,
        content_id uuid,
        content text,
        page integer,
        slide integer,
        heading text,
        title text,
        file_name text,
        file_path text,
        industry text,
        customer_name text,
        vector_rank integer,
        text_rank integer,
        score double precision
      )
      language sql
      stable
      security invoker
      set search_path = public, extensions
      as $$
        with scoped as (
          select
            c.id,
            c.content_id,
            c.content,
            c.page,
            c.slide,
            c.heading,
            c.tsv,
            ci.title,
            ci.file_name,
            ci.file_path,
            ci.industry,
            ci.customer_name,
            c.embedding <=> query_embedding as distance
          from public.document_chunks c
          join public.content_items ci on ci.id = c.content_id
          where (
              nullif(btrim(coalesce(filter_industry, '')), '') is null
              or ci.industry = btrim(filter_industry)
            )
            and (
              nullif(btrim(coalesce(filter_customer, '')), '') is null
              or ci.customer_name = btrim(filter_customer)
            )
        ),
        vector_hits as (
          select
            v.id,
            row_number() over (order by v.distance)::integer as rank
          from scoped v
          order by v.distance
          limit 50
        ),
        query_ts as (
          select websearch_to_tsquery('english', btrim(coalesce(query_text, ''))) as ts
        ),
        text_matches as (
          select
            s.id,
            ts_rank(s.tsv, q.ts) as rank_score
          from scoped s
          cross join query_ts q
          where nullif(btrim(coalesce(query_text, '')), '') is not null
            and s.tsv @@ q.ts
          order by rank_score desc
          limit 50
        ),
        text_hits as (
          select
            t.id,
            row_number() over (order by t.rank_score desc)::integer as rank
          from text_matches t
        ),
        merged as (
          select
            coalesce(v.id, t.id) as id,
            v.rank as vector_rank,
            t.rank as text_rank,
            coalesce(1.0 / (60 + v.rank), 0.0) + coalesce(1.0 / (60 + t.rank), 0.0) as score
          from vector_hits v
          full outer join text_hits t on t.id = v.id
        )
        select
          m.id as chunk_id,
          s.content_id,
          s.content,
          s.page,
          s.slide,
          s.heading,
          s.title,
          s.file_name,
          s.file_path,
          s.industry,
          s.customer_name,
          m.vector_rank,
          m.text_rank,
          m.score
        from merged m
        join scoped s on s.id = m.id
        order by
          m.score desc,
          coalesce(m.vector_rank, 2147483647),
          coalesce(m.text_rank, 2147483647),
          s.id
        limit least(greatest(coalesce(match_count, 8), 1), 50);
      $$;
      
      comment on function public.search_chunks(vector, text, text, text, integer) is
        'Hybrid (pgvector + full-text, RRF k=60) passage search. Security invoker: RLS on document_chunks and content_items applies.';
      
      revoke all on function public.search_chunks(vector, text, text, text, integer)
        from public, anon;
      grant execute on function public.search_chunks(vector, text, text, text, integer)
        to authenticated;
      
      reset search_path;
      
```

  content: |
      /**
       * Scoring for the retrieval evaluation set. Pure functions so the numbers can be
       * tested without a database or an embedding provider.
       */
      import type { RetrievedChunk } from "./search";
      
      export type EvalScope = { industry?: string | null; customer?: string | null };
      
      export type EvalCase = {
        id: string;
        category: string;
        question: string;
        /** File names that contain the answer; empty means the library has no answer. */
        expectedFiles: string[];
        scope?: EvalScope;
        notes?: string;
      };
      
      export type EvalSet = {
        version: number;
        k: number;
        cases: EvalCase[];
      };
      
      export type RankedFile = {
        rank: number;
        contentId: string;
        fileName: string;
        title: string;
      };
      
      export type EvalOutcome = {
        id: string;
        category: string;
        question: string;
        answerable: boolean;
        expectedFiles: string[];
        files: RankedFile[];
        /** Rank (1-based) of the first expected file, or null when none was found. */
        firstRank: number | null;
        recall: number;
        reciprocalRank: number;
      };
      
      export type CategorySummary = {
        total: number;
        answerable: number;
        recallAtK: number;
        mrr: number;
      };
      
      export type EvalSummary = {
        total: number;
        answerable: number;
        unanswerable: number;
        recallAtK: number;
        mrr: number;
        byCategory: Record<string, CategorySummary>;
        /** Unanswerable questions that still returned passages; useful for fabrication checks. */
        unanswerableWithResults: number;
      };
      
      /** Strips the download prefix (`<id8>-`) and punctuation so names compare cleanly. */
      function normalize(value: string): string {
        return value
          .toLowerCase()
          .replace(/^[0-9a-f]{8}-/, "")
          .replace(/[^a-z0-9.]+/g, "-")
          .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, "");
      }
      
      export function fileMatchesExpected(
        file: { fileName: string; title: string },
        expected: string,
      ): boolean {
        const normalizedExpected = normalize(expected);
        if (!normalizedExpected) return false;
        const stem = normalizedExpected.replace(/\.[a-z0-9]+$/, "");
        const normalizedFile = normalize(file.fileName);
        const normalizedTitle = normalize(file.title);
      
        return (
          normalizedFile === normalizedExpected ||
          normalizedFile.endsWith(`-${normalizedExpected}`) ||
          normalizedFile.endsWith(normalizedExpected) ||
          (stem.length >= 8 && normalizedFile.includes(stem)) ||
          (stem.length >= 8 && normalizedTitle.includes(stem))
        );
      }
      
      /** Deduplicates by document while preserving the retrieval order. */
      export function rankFiles(chunks: readonly RetrievedChunk[]): RankedFile[] {
        const seen = new Set<string>();
        const files: RankedFile[] = [];
        for (const chunk of chunks) {
          if (seen.has(chunk.contentId)) continue;
          seen.add(chunk.contentId);
          files.push({
            rank: files.length + 1,
            contentId: chunk.contentId,
            fileName: chunk.fileName,
            title: chunk.title,
          });
        }
        return files;
      }
      
      export function evaluateCase(testCase: EvalCase, chunks: readonly RetrievedChunk[]): EvalOutcome {
        const files = rankFiles(chunks);
        const answerable = testCase.expectedFiles.length > 0;
        const match = answerable
          ? files.find((file) =>
              testCase.expectedFiles.some((expected) => fileMatchesExpected(file, expected)),
            )
          : undefined;
        const firstRank = match?.rank ?? null;
      
        return {
          id: testCase.id,
          category: testCase.category,
          question: testCase.question,
          answerable,
          expectedFiles: testCase.expectedFiles,
          files,
          firstRank,
          recall: firstRank === null ? 0 : 1,
          reciprocalRank: firstRank === null ? 0 : 1 / firstRank,
        };
      }
      
      function emptySummary(): CategorySummary {
        return { total: 0, answerable: 0, recallAtK: 0, mrr: 0 };
      }
      
      export function summarize(outcomes: readonly EvalOutcome[]): EvalSummary {
        const categories = new Map<string, { summary: CategorySummary; recall: number; rr: number }>();
        let answerable = 0;
        let recall = 0;
        let reciprocalRank = 0;
        let unanswerableWithResults = 0;
      
        for (const outcome of outcomes) {
          const bucket = categories.get(outcome.category) ?? {
            summary: emptySummary(),
            recall: 0,
            rr: 0,
          };
          bucket.summary.total += 1;
      
          if (outcome.answerable) {
            answerable += 1;
            recall += outcome.recall;
            reciprocalRank += outcome.reciprocalRank;
            bucket.summary.answerable += 1;
            bucket.recall += outcome.recall;
            bucket.rr += outcome.reciprocalRank;
          } else if (outcome.files.length > 0) {
            unanswerableWithResults += 1;
          }
      
          categories.set(outcome.category, bucket);
        }
      
        const byCategory: Record<string, CategorySummary> = {};
        for (const [name, bucket] of categories) {
          byCategory[name] = {
            ...bucket.summary,
            recallAtK: bucket.summary.answerable ? bucket.recall / bucket.summary.answerable : 0,
            mrr: bucket.summary.answerable ? bucket.rr / bucket.summary.answerable : 0,
          };
        }
      
        return {
          total: outcomes.length,
          answerable,
          unanswerable: outcomes.length - answerable,
          recallAtK: answerable ? recall / answerable : 0,
          mrr: answerable ? reciprocalRank / answerable : 0,
          byCategory,
          unanswerableWithResults,
        };
      }

  content: |
      /**
       * Retrieval evaluation: recall@k and MRR for evals/rag-eval.json.
       *
       * Runs the real hybrid search (public.search_chunks) as an authenticated member,
       * so RLS applies and the numbers match what a signed-in user would get. It needs
       * the database connection string and the embedding provider; a member password
       * session is not required, which keeps it usable before the service role and new
       * API keys are in place.
       *
       * Usage:
       *   bun scripts/run-eval.ts                     # recall@8, needs 80% to pass
       *   bun scripts/run-eval.ts --k 5 --verbose
       *   bun scripts/run-eval.ts --email someone@aionos.ai
       *   bun scripts/run-eval.ts --set evals/other.json --threshold 0.9
       */
      import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
      import postgres from "postgres";
      import { embedTexts } from "../src/lib/rag/embeddings";
      import {
        evaluateCase,
        summarize,
        type EvalCase,
        type EvalSet,
        type EvalOutcome,
      } from "../src/lib/rag/eval";
      import {
        clampMatchCount,
        formatVectorLiteral,
        mapSearchRows,
        normalizeScope,
        type RetrievedChunk,
      } from "../src/lib/rag/search";
      
      const args = process.argv.slice(2);
      const flag = (name: string) => args.includes(name);
      const value = (name: string) => {
        const index = args.indexOf(name);
        return index >= 0 ? args[index + 1] : undefined;
      };
      
      const verbose = flag("--verbose");
      const setPath = value("--set") ?? "evals/rag-eval.json";
      const threshold = Number(value("--threshold") ?? 0.8);
      const requestedK = Number(value("--k") ?? 0);
      const requestedEmail = value("--email");
      
      if (!process.env.SUPABASE_DB_URL) {
        console.error("SUPABASE_DB_URL is missing from .env");
        process.exit(1);
      }
      if (!process.env.TOKENFACTORY_API_BASE || !process.env.TOKENFACTORY_API_KEY) {
        console.error("TOKENFACTORY_API_BASE / TOKENFACTORY_API_KEY are missing from .env");
        process.exit(1);
      }
      
      function fail(message: string): never {
        console.error(message);
        process.exit(1);
      }

- path: drizzle/migrations/0010_rag.sql
  content: |
      -- Phase 1: passage index, ingestion state, and owner-only chat history.
      -- Vector search stays in Postgres so RLS and cascade deletes apply to it.
      create extension if not exists vector with schema extensions;
      set search_path = public, extensions;
      
      create table public.document_chunks (
        id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
        content_id uuid not null references public.content_items(id) on delete cascade,
        chunk_index integer not null check (chunk_index >= 0),
        content text not null check (content <> ''),
        estimated_tokens integer not null check (estimated_tokens > 0),
        page integer check (page is null or page > 0),
        slide integer check (slide is null or slide > 0),
        heading text,
        embedding vector(1536) not null,
        embedding_model text not null default 'qwen/qwen3-embedding-8b',
        created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
        unique (content_id, chunk_index),
        check (page is null or slide is null)
      );
      
      create index document_chunks_embedding_idx
        on public.document_chunks
        using hnsw (embedding vector_cosine_ops);
      
      create table public.ingestion_jobs (
        id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
        content_id uuid not null unique references public.content_items(id) on delete cascade,
        status text not null default 'queued'
          check (status in ('queued', 'running', 'ready', 'empty', 'skipped', 'failed')),
        attempts integer not null default 0 check (attempts >= 0),
        chunk_count integer not null default 0 check (chunk_count >= 0),
        embedding_model text not null default 'qwen/qwen3-embedding-8b',
        dimensions integer not null default 1536,
        warnings jsonb not null default '[]'::jsonb
          check (jsonb_typeof(warnings) = 'array'),
        error text,
        started_at timestamptz,
        finished_at timestamptz,
        created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
        updated_at timestamptz not null default now()
      );
      
      create table public.chat_threads (
        id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
        user_id uuid not null default auth.uid()
          references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,
        title text not null default 'New chat',
        created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
        updated_at timestamptz not null default now()
      );
      
      create index chat_threads_owner_idx
        on public.chat_threads (user_id, updated_at desc);
      
      create table public.chat_messages (
        id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
        thread_id uuid not null references public.chat_threads(id) on delete cascade,
        role text not null check (role in ('user', 'assistant')),
        content text not null check (content <> ''),
        citations jsonb not null default '[]'::jsonb
          check (jsonb_typeof(citations) = 'array'),
        created_at timestamptz not null default now()
      );
      
      create index chat_messages_thread_idx
        on public.chat_messages (thread_id, created_at);
      
      grant select on public.document_chunks to authenticated;
      grant all on public.document_chunks to service_role;

      /**
       * Standalone ingestion worker. Claims one queued job at a time, downloads the
       * private file, extracts text, chunks, embeds and replaces the document's
       * passages atomically.
       *
       * Usage:
       *   bun scripts/ingest-worker.ts                 # drain the queue, then exit
       *   bun scripts/ingest-worker.ts --watch         # poll until interrupted
       *   bun scripts/ingest-worker.ts --limit 5       # stop after five jobs
       *   bun scripts/ingest-worker.ts --local-dir spike-input
       *   bun scripts/ingest-worker.ts --backfill     # queue library items missing a job
       *
       * File bytes come from the private "content" bucket, authenticated with the
       * service role key when present, otherwise a member session. --local-dir reads
       * the same files from disk instead, for local pipeline checks without Storage.
       *
       * Never print document text or credentials from here: logs carry counts only.
       */
      import { createClient, type SupabaseClient } from "@supabase/supabase-js";
      import { existsSync, readFileSync, readdirSync, statSync } from "node:fs";
      import { basename, join, resolve } from "node:path";
      import postgres from "postgres";
      import type { Database } from "../src/integrations/supabase/types";
      import { documentFormat } from "../src/lib/document-format";
      import { extractDocument } from "../src/lib/rag/extract";
      import {
        buildChunkRows,
        embedTexts,
        hashChunkContents,
        type EmbeddedChunk,
      } from "../src/lib/rag/ingest";
      
      const BUCKET = "content";
      const MAX_ATTEMPTS = 3;
      const SUPPORTED = new Set(["pdf", "docx", "pptx"]);
      
      type Job = {
        id: string;
        content_id: string;
        attempts: number;
        content_hash: string | null;
        chunk_count: number;
        file_path: string;
        file_name: string;
        mime_type: string | null;
      };
      
      type Counters = { claimed: number; ready: number; empty: number; skipped: number; failed: number };
      
      const args = process.argv.slice(2);

Those were already in the repo. Phase 3 (0014, embeddings.ts, search.ts, search.functions.ts, rag-debug.tsx, eval.ts, run-eval.ts, evals/rag-eval.json, verify-rag.ts additions) was mine.

Here's what exists now and why each piece is shaped the way it is.

## Where the work sits

The RAG feature is being built in four phases. Phases 0–2 were already in the repo; this session added **Phase 3 — retrieval**, which is the gate for chat: chat can only be as good and as safe as the passages it can find.

## Phase 0–2 (already there, kept intact)

- **`src/lib/rag/extract.ts`** — pulls text, page/slide numbers and headings out of PDF/DOCX/PPTX. Deterministic and unit-tested, so extraction bugs are caught before they become "the AI answered wrong".
- **`src/lib/rag/chunk.ts`** — token-budgeted chunks with heading and page/slide anchors. Anchors are kept because a citation that says "page 7" is verifiable; a citation that says "somewhere in this deck" is not.
- **`drizzle/migrations/0010_rag.sql`** — `document_chunks` (pgvector `vector(1536)`, HNSW index), `ingestion_jobs`, and owner-only `chat_threads`/`chat_messages`. Vector storage lives **in Postgres** rather than a separate vector service specifically so RLS and `on delete cascade` apply to it: deleting a file deletes its passages, and a user can't retrieve chunks from a file they can't open.
- **`0011`–`0013`** — grants locked down, a reconcile pass for drifted jobs, and a trigger that enqueues a job on upload/edit. Ingestion is queue-driven so a slow deck never blocks the upload request.
- **`scripts/ingest-worker.ts`** — claims one job at a time, downloads the private file, extracts → chunks → embeds → replaces the document's passages in a single transaction (so a re-index can't leave a half-updated document), with attempt limits and retry. Has a `--local-dir` mode so the pipeline can be tested without Storage.

## Phase 3 — this session

**`drizzle/migrations/0014_hybrid_search.sql` — `public.search_chunks`**
Vector similarity *and* full-text search, merged with reciprocal rank fusion (k=60). Reason: pure vector search is bad at exactly the things this library is searched with — product names ("United Next", "Smart Verify"), acronyms, customer names. Full text alone misses paraphrases. RRF keeps a strong hit from either side without having to tune a score blend. Industry/customer/title are joined from `content_items` at query time rather than copied onto each chunk, so moving a file between industries or renaming a company can never leave stale metadata behind.

**Security-invoker, caller's JWT, no service role.** `anon` is explicitly revoked and `authenticated` granted, and I verified anon is actually denied. This is the property that makes future citations trustworthy: the database, not the UI, decides which chunks exist for that user.

**`src/lib/rag/search.ts` — one `retrieve()` seam.** Chat needs it now; deck generation and the company-URL theming work will need the same thing. It takes the embedder and the query as injected dependencies, plus `normalizeScope`/`clampMatchCount`/`formatVectorLiteral`/`mapSearchRows` as pure helpers — so the ranking and filtering rules live in one place and are unit-testable with no database.

**`src/lib/rag/embeddings.ts` — split out of `ingest.ts`.** Query embedding runs inside the Cloudflare Worker on every search, so it must not drag in `node:crypto` or other Node-only imports. Documents and queries call the *same* client and model on purpose: mixing embedding models silently destroys similarity, and the failure looks like "bad results", not an error.

**`src/lib/rag/search.functions.ts` — `searchLibrary` server function.** Authenticated via `requireSupabaseAuth`, input validated with zod (query 2–500 chars, bounded scope and match count). Provider/database detail is logged server-side and never returned to the client; only the query text leaves the server.

**`src/routes/_authenticated/rag-debug.tsx`** — admin-only debug page showing score, vector rank and text rank per passage, so chunk size and fusion weights can be tuned on evidence. Gated by the same admin check as `/admin`, `noindex`, and marked temporary — it comes out once chat ships.

**`src/lib/rag/eval.ts` + `evals/rag-eval.json` + `scripts/run-eval.ts`** — recall@k and MRR, scored by tested pure functions, run against the real `search_chunks` as an authenticated member (so the numbers match a real user). The set deliberately includes "no answer exists" questions and reports how often retrieval returns *something* anyway — that's the fabrication risk chat has to handle next.

## Honest status

- **Verified:** unit tests for chunk/extract/ingest/search/eval pass, `tsc`, lint, prettier and `bun run build` are clean, the extended `scripts/verify-rag.ts` RLS smoke test passes against the live database, and the RPC is confirmed reachable by a member and denied to anon.
- **Not a real quality score:** the eval run reported ~7.3% recall, but only 3 of the 31 drafted library files are in this database — 28 questions *cannot* succeed yet. The harness is correct; the corpus is incomplete.
- **Blocked:** member password sign-in currently fails inside Supabase Auth itself (500 "Database error querying schema" regardless of password), so retrieval was verified through the database as an authenticated member rather than through the app UI.
